VideoRAE: Taming Video Foundation Models for Generative Modeling via Representation Autoencoders
Authors: Zhihao Xie, Junfeng Wu, Xinting Hu, Junchao Huang, Li Jiang
First: 2026-07-15T17:59:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:59:23+00:00
Comments: Home page: https://zhxie0117.github.io/VideoRAE
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Video generative models commonly rely on latent spaces learned by 3D Variational Autoencoders (3D-VAEs). However, conventional 3D-VAEs are mainly optimized for pixel-level reconstruction, which can limit the semantic and spatio-temporal structure captured by their latents. Meanwhile, Video Foundation Models (VFMs) such as V-JEPA 2 and VideoMAEv2 show strong video understanding capabilities, yet whether their frozen representations can be transformed into compact, reconstruction-capable, and generation-friendly video latents remains largely unexplored. We answer this question with VideoRAE, a representation autoencoder that leverages multi-scale hierarchical features from a frozen video foundation encoder and compresses them with a lightweight 1D self-attention projector. VideoRAE supports both continuous latents for Diffusion Transformers and discrete tokens for autoregressive models via multi-codebook high-dimensional quantization. During decoding, a local-and-global representation alignment objective with the frozen VFM teacher improves semantic preservation and enables training without KL regularization. Experiments show that VideoRAE achieves strong reconstruction in both continuous and discrete regimes. On UCF-101, it obtains state-of-the-art class-to-video gFVDs of 40 and 93 with AR and DiT generators, respectively, while converging approximately 5x faster than competing autoencoder baselines. In a controlled 2B-scale text-to-video study, replacing LTX-VAE with VideoRAE leads to faster convergence under comparable settings. These results validate frozen VFM representations as versatile and generation-friendly video latents. The model and code will be released on https://zhxie0117.github.io/VideoRAE.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of conventional 3D-VAEs in capturing semantic and spatio-temporal structure, and the untapped potential of frozen Video Foundation Models (VFMs), the authors propose VideoRAE: a representation autoencoder that leverages multi-scale features from a frozen VFM encoder and compresses them using a lightweight 1D self-attention projector. VideoRAE supports both continuous latents for diffusion models and discrete tokens for autoregressive generation via high-dimensional quantization. Training uses a local-and-global alignment objective with the frozen VFM teacher, eliminating the need for KL regularization. Experiments show VideoRAE achieves state-of-the-art generation quality on UCF-101 with gFVDs of 40 and 93 for AR and DiT models, respectively, and converges 5x faster than baseline autoencoders. In a text-to-video study, replacing LTX-VAE with VideoRAE also accelerates convergence, confirming frozen VFM representations as effective, generation-friendly video latents.
为了解决3D-VAE在语义和时空结构表达上的不足，并挖掘冻结的视频基础模型（VFM）在生成建模中的潜力，作者提出了VideoRAE：一种利用冻结VFM编码器的多尺度层次特征，通过轻量级1D自注意力投影器进行压缩的表示自编码器。该模型支持连续潜变量用于扩散模型，以及离散token用于自回归生成，通过高维量化实现。训练中无需KL正则化，通过与冻结VFM教师的局部-全局表示对齐目标实现语义保持。实验表明，VideoRAE在UCF-101上分别以AR和DiT生成器获得40和93的gFVD最优结果，收敛速度比现有自编码器基线快约5倍，验证了冻结VFM表示作为高效视频潜变量的可行性。
Leveraging unlabelled data for generalizable neural population decoding
Authors: Ximeng Mao, Nanda H. Krishna, Avery Hee-Woon Ryoo, Matthew G. Perich, Guillaume Lajoie
First: 2026-07-15T17:58:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:58:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robust and accurate neural decoders are integral to neurotechnologies such as brain-computer interfaces and closed-loop experiments. Recent work has shown that tokenizing neural data at the spike level facilitates multi-session pretraining and delivers state-of-the-art decoding performance. However, current spike-based models are restricted to supervised learning (SL), limiting training to datasets with paired behavioural labels. To address this limitation, we introduce MOJO (Masked autOencoder-based JOint training), a training framework for spike-tokenizing models that jointly leverages self-supervised learning (SSL) via masked autoencoding and SL objectives. We evaluate MOJO on three spiking datasets spanning monkey motor cortex during reaching tasks and multi-regional mouse recordings during vision and decision making tasks, demonstrating superior performance over purely SL-trained models. This improvement is especially pronounced when training with limited labelled data, particularly in few-shot finetuning, where only a small amount of labelled data from a new session is available. Incorporating SSL also yields more interpretable neuronal representations, improving performance on brain region classification and spike-statistics prediction without explicit optimization for these tasks. We further show that MOJO generalizes beyond spiking data to human electrocorticography during speech, where it continues to outperform purely SL-trained models and achieves performance comparable to neuro-foundation models (NFMs) designed specifically for continuous signals. Overall, augmenting spike-tokenizing models with SSL improves performance in label-impoverished settings and enables the use of unlabelled data across various tasks and species, while generalizing to other neural modalities. These results suggest a path towards more flexible and scalable data usage when training NFMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用无标签数据实现通用神经群体解码
稳健且准确的神经解码器是脑机接口和闭环实验等神经技术的核心。近期研究表明，以尖峰级别对神经数据进行分词可促进多会话预训练并实现最先进的解码性能。然而，当前基于尖峰的模型受限于监督学习（SL），仅能使用带有行为标签的配对数据集进行训练。为解决此局限，我们提出了MOJO（基于掩码自编码器的联合训练），一种适用于尖峰分词模型的训练框架，联合利用自监督学习（SSL）通过掩码自编码与SL目标。我们在三个涵盖猴子在伸手任务期间运动皮层和小鼠在视觉与决策任务期间多区域记录的尖峰数据集上评估MOJO，结果表明其性能优于纯SL训练模型。尤其在标注数据有限的情况下（如少量新会话标注数据的微调），性能提升尤为显著。引入SSL还使神经元表征更具可解释性，无需显式优化即可提升脑区分类和尖峰统计预测性能。我们进一步表明，MOJO可泛化至人类在言语期间的皮层电图数据，仍优于纯SL训练模型，并达到专为连续信号设计的神经基础模型（NFMs）的性能水平。总体而言，为尖峰分词模型引入SSL可提升标注数据匮乏场景下的性能，支持跨任务和物种使用无标签数据，并泛化至其他神经模态。这些结果为训练NFMs时更灵活、可扩展地利用数据提供了路径。
Summary / 总结
To enhance neural population decoding in neurotechnologies, this study addresses the limitation of supervised learning by introducing MOJO, a framework that jointly trains spike-tokenizing models using self-supervised learning via masked autoencoding and supervised objectives. Evaluated across three spiking datasets from monkeys and mice, MOJO consistently outperforms purely supervised models, especially under few-shot fine-tuning with limited labeled data. It also improves interpretability by enhancing neuronal representation quality, boosting performance in brain region classification and spike-statistics prediction without explicit tuning. Furthermore, MOJO generalizes to human electrocorticography during speech, matching the performance of neuro-foundation models designed for continuous signals, demonstrating its broad applicability across species and modalities.
为提升神经群体解码在神经技术中的表现，本研究提出MOJO框架，结合自监督学习（通过掩码自编码）与监督学习，用于处理突触标记化的神经数据。该方法旨在解决当前模型仅依赖标注数据的局限性，通过利用未标注数据增强泛化能力，尤其在标注数据稀缺的少样本微调场景中表现突出。在涵盖猴运动皮层和小鼠多区域记录的三个数据集上，MOJO显著优于纯监督模型，同时生成更可解释的神经表征，提升了脑区分类与突触统计预测能力，无需显式优化。此外，MOJO还能推广至人类脑皮层电图（在言语任务中），其性能可媲美专为连续信号设计的神经基础模型，展现出跨物种和神经模态的广泛适用性。
Linear Independent Component Analysis via Optimal Transport
Authors: Ashutosh Jha, Michel Besserve, Simon Buchholz
First: 2026-07-15T17:56:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:56:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Linear Independent Component Analysis (ICA) recovers jointly independent source signals from their linear mixtures. To achieve this, classical ICA algorithms attempt to maximize non-Gaussianity, measured by negentropy, which is linked to independence by information theory. Because exact negentropy optimization is intractable, they rely on proxy contrast functions, such as fourth-order cumulants, and parametric log-likelihoods. We propose instead to measure non-Gaussianity using the squared Wasserstein distance $W_2^2$ to a standard Gaussian. We prove that the Wasserstein distance between a standard normal distribution and linear projections of the data is maximized when the projection recovers an independent component. Based on this observation, we propose the OT-ICA algorithm which finds this projection by gradient-based optimization. Empirical evaluation on simulated data shows that OT-ICA outperforms proxy-based methods for different distributions of the latent variables. Application to EEG artifact removal and econometric price discovery confirm OT-ICA can be used for applied ICA tasks without distributional assumptions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过最优传输的线性独立成分分析
线性独立成分分析（ICA）从其线性混合中恢复联合独立的源信号。为实现此目标，经典ICA算法试图最大化非高斯性，该非高斯性通过负熵衡量，而负熵与独立性在信息论中相关。由于精确负熵优化不可行，它们依赖代理对比函数，如四阶累积量和参数化对数似然。我们提出改用标准高斯分布的平方Wasserstein距离$W_2^2$来衡量非高斯性。我们证明，标准正态分布与数据线性投影之间的Wasserstein距离在投影恢复独立成分时达到最大。基于此观察，我们提出OT-ICA算法，通过梯度优化寻找该投影。在模拟数据上的实证评估表明，OT-ICA在不同潜在变量分布下优于基于代理的方法。将其应用于脑电图伪影去除和计量经济学价格发现，证实OT-ICA可在无需分布假设的情况下用于实际ICA任务。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of proxy-based contrast functions in classical ICA, this work proposes using the squared Wasserstein distance to a Gaussian as a measure of non-Gaussianity, leveraging optimal transport theory to identify independent components. The OT-ICA algorithm finds the optimal linear projection by maximizing this distance via gradient-based optimization, theoretically grounded in the fact that such projections recover independent sources. Experimental results on simulated data demonstrate superior performance over traditional methods across various latent variable distributions, and real-world applications in EEG artifact removal and econometric price discovery validate its practical utility without requiring distributional assumptions.
为了解决传统ICA中最大化负熵计算不可行的问题，本文提出使用与高斯分布的平方Wasserstein距离 $W_2^2$ 作为非高斯性度量，该度量在信息论中与独立性相关。OT-ICA算法通过梯度优化寻找最大化该距离的线性投影，从而恢复独立分量。在模拟数据上的实验表明，该方法在多种潜在变量分布下优于基于代理函数的方法，且在EEG去噪和经济价格发现中的应用验证了其无需分布假设的实用性。
From Pixels to States: Rethinking Interactive World Models as Game Engines
Authors: Zhen Li, Zian Meng, Shuwei Shi, Mingliang Zhai, Jiaming Tan, Chuanhao Li, Kaipeng Zhang
First: 2026-07-15T17:48:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:48:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Building interactive worlds that respond coherently to player actions has long been a shared goal of computer graphics, games, and artificial intelligence. Recent video generative models provide a data-driven route toward this goal by predicting future observations conditioned on user actions, and are increasingly regarded as potential next-generation game engines. Realizing a genuinely interactive game world, however, requires interaction outcomes that follow rules over evolving game conditions, consequences that persist over long horizons, and a generation loop that operates in real time. Conventional game engines realize these properties through a recurrent action-state-observation loop, in which player actions update an explicit game state according to predefined rules and observations are rendered from the resulting state. Taking this loop as an organizing lens, this paper examines interactive game world modeling along four dimensions: player action control, game state dynamics, state-observation persistence, and real-time interactive generation. For each dimension, we start from the capabilities required by an interactive game world, group existing approaches into representative families, and discuss the strengths and trade-offs of each family. Complementing this analysis, we present a scalable data engine for Black Myth: Wukong that collects over 90 hours of gameplay with frame-aligned player actions, ground-truth game states, and visual observations, together with structured and semantic annotations, as a resource for state-aware game world modeling. We hope this paper offers a clear picture of where the field stands and fosters progress toward interactive game worlds.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从像素到状态：重新思考交互式世界模型作为游戏引擎
构建能够对玩家行为做出连贯响应的交互式世界，一直是计算机图形学、游戏和人工智能领域的共同目标。近期的视频生成模型通过根据用户行为预测未来观测，提供了一条数据驱动的实现路径，并日益被视为下一代游戏引擎的潜在方案。然而，要实现真正交互式的游戏世界，需要交互结果遵循随游戏状态演变的规则、后果能在长时程内持续存在，以及实时运行的生成循环。传统游戏引擎通过循环的“动作-状态-观测”机制实现这些特性：玩家动作根据预定义规则更新显式游戏状态，观测则从该状态渲染生成。本文以该循环为组织视角，从四个维度考察交互式游戏世界建模：玩家动作控制、游戏状态动态、状态-观测持久性及实时交互生成。每个维度下，我们从交互式游戏世界所需能力出发，将现有方法归类为代表性家族，并讨论各家族的优势与权衡。此外，我们为《黑神话：悟空》构建了一个可扩展的数据引擎，收集了超过90小时的带帧对齐玩家动作、真实游戏状态与视觉观测数据，并附带结构化和语义标注，作为状态感知游戏世界建模的资源。我们希望本文能清晰呈现该领域现状，并推动交互式游戏世界的发展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to build truly interactive game worlds that respond coherently to player actions, this paper reexamines interactive world modeling through the lens of game engine design, emphasizing real-time, rule-based state evolution and persistent consequences. It analyzes four core dimensions—player action control, game state dynamics, state-observation persistence, and real-time generation—and categorizes existing approaches by their strengths and trade-offs. To ground the analysis, the authors introduce a scalable data engine for Black Myth: Wukong, capturing over 90 hours of gameplay with frame-aligned actions, ground-truth states, and visual observations, annotated with structured semantics, to support state-aware modeling.
为构建真正响应玩家行为的交互式游戏世界，本文从游戏引擎视角重新审视交互式世界建模，强调实时状态更新、持久后果及动作-状态-观测循环。文章围绕四个核心维度——玩家动作控制、游戏状态动态、状态-观测持久性与实时生成——分析现有方法的优劣与权衡。为支持实证研究，作者构建了《黑神话：悟空》的可扩展数据引擎，采集超过90小时的游戏数据，包含帧对齐动作、真实状态及语义标注，为交互式世界建模提供基准与研究资源。
Representation-Based Exploration for Language Models: From Test-Time to Post-Training
Authors: Jens Tuyls, Dylan J. Foster, Akshay Krishnamurthy, Jordan T. Ash
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-10-13T17:49:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:43:29+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICLR 2026. Website and code: https://rep-exp.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) promises to expand the capabilities of language models, but it is unclear if current RL techniques promote the discovery of novel behaviors, or simply sharpen those already present in the base model. In this paper, we investigate the value of deliberate exploration -- explicitly incentivizing the model to discover novel and diverse behaviors -- and aim to understand how the knowledge in pre-trained models can guide this search. Our main finding is that exploration with a simple, principled, representation-based bonus derived from the pre-trained language model's hidden states significantly improves diversity and pass@k rates -- both for post-training, and in a novel inference-time scaling setting we introduce. For inference-time, exploration with representation-based diversity improves efficiency, consistently improving pass@k rates across a variety of models and reasoning tasks. For example, for Qwen-2.5-14b-Instruct we obtain over 50% improvement in verifier efficiency on almost all tasks. For post-training, we show that integrating this exploration strategy into an RL pipeline improves reasoning performance over that of the initial model and over standard RL post-training. For example, on AIME 2024, our post-trained Qwen-2.5-7b-Instruct's pass@80 matches the pass@256 of GRPO on the same model, demonstrating a 3x improvement in test-time sample efficiency. Overall, our findings suggest that deliberate exploration -- with the right notion of diversity -- is a practical path toward discovery of new behaviors beyond sharpening.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于表示的探索用于语言模型：从测试时到后训练
强化学习（RL）有望扩展语言模型的能力，但尚不清楚当前的RL技术是否促进新行为的发现，还是仅强化了基础模型中已有的行为。本文研究了刻意探索的价值——即明确激励模型发现新颖且多样的行为，并旨在理解预训练模型中的知识如何引导该搜索。我们的主要发现是：使用从预训练语言模型隐藏状态中推导出的简单、原则性的表示型奖励，可显著提升多样性与pass@k率——无论是后训练阶段，还是我们引入的一种新型推理时扩展设置。在推理时，基于表示的多样性探索可提升效率，跨多种模型和推理任务持续改善pass@k率。例如，对于Qwen-2.5-14b-Instruct，我们在几乎所有任务上实现了验证器效率超过50%的提升。在后训练阶段，我们将此探索策略整合进RL流程，其推理性能优于初始模型和标准RL后训练。例如，在AIME 2024中，我们的后训练Qwen-2.5-7b-Instruct的pass@80与同模型GRPO的pass@256相当，实现了3倍的测试时样本效率提升。总体而言，我们的研究结果表明，结合恰当的多样性概念，刻意探索是发现超越单纯强化的新行为的实用路径。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether reinforcement learning can drive language models to discover novel behaviors rather than merely refining existing ones, proposing a representation-based exploration strategy that leverages pre-trained model hidden states to incentivize diversity. The method adds a principled bonus to reward novel behavior during both post-training and test-time inference, leading to significant improvements in both diversity and pass@k performance. Experiments show that for Qwen-2.5-14b-Instruct, test-time exploration boosts verifier efficiency by over 50% across tasks, while post-training with this approach achieves pass@80 on AIME 2024 matching GRPO’s pass@256 on the same model — a 3x gain in sample efficiency — demonstrating that deliberate, representation-guided exploration is a viable path to discovering new capabilities.
该论文探讨强化学习是否能引导语言模型发现新行为，而非仅优化已有能力。提出一种基于表示的探索策略，利用预训练模型隐藏状态激励多样化行为，适用于后训练和推理时扩展。实验表明，该方法显著提升多样性和pass@k表现：推理时，Qwen-2.5-14b-Instruct的验证器效率普遍提升50%以上；后训练时，推理性能提升，AIME 2024任务中pass@80达到GRPO在相同模型下pass@256水平，样本效率提升3倍。
MetaPerch: Learning from metadata for bioacoustics foundation models
Authors: Mustafa Chasmai, Vincent Dumoulin, Jenny Hamer
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-07-15T17:42:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:42:41+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bioacoustic foundation models rely on large-scale citizen science platforms like Xeno-Canto for geographically and ecologically diverse data. Recent work has shown that supervision alone can produce SotA species detection models when trained on this large-scale data -- however, there remains unutilized potential in the form of recording metadata readily available within these community-driven data hubs. In this work, we explore the use of metadata -- such as location and time -- as auxiliary supervision signals, allowing the model to leverage species-metadata correlations in its learned representation. Auxiliary metadata losses provide additional information beyond vocalizations alone that can encourage a richer, more robust representation that generalizes better to species distribution and acoustic domain shifts -- important challenges for deployment in real-world passive acoustic monitoring (PAM) settings. We introduce MetaPerch, a new foundation model that achieves strong species identification performance across multiple challenging domains and present an extensive empirical study of the effects of 9 diverse metadata sources on 17 bioacoustic datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MetaPerch：利用元数据为生物声学基础模型学习
生物声学基础模型依赖于如Xeno-Canto等大规模公民科学平台提供的地理和生态多样性数据。近期研究表明，仅使用监督信号即可在这些大规模数据上训练出最先进的物种检测模型——然而，这些社区驱动数据平台中广泛存在的录音元数据仍有未被利用的潜力。本研究探索将元数据（如位置和时间）作为辅助监督信号，使模型能够利用物种与元数据之间的关联性，丰富其学习表示。辅助元数据损失提供了超越声学信号本身的信息，有助于构建更丰富、更鲁棒的表示，从而更好地泛化到物种分布和声学域迁移等现实被动声学监测（PAM）场景中的关键挑战。我们提出了MetaPerch，一种在多个具有挑战性的领域中表现优异的新型基础模型，并对9种不同元数据源在17个生物声学数据集上的影响进行了全面实证研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underutilized potential of metadata in citizen science bioacoustic datasets, this work introduces MetaPerch, a foundation model that incorporates auxiliary metadata such as location and time as supervision signals to enhance species detection. By training with metadata losses alongside audio signals, the model learns richer representations that better capture species-environment correlations, improving generalization across acoustic domain shifts and geographic distributions. Experiments across 17 datasets show that integrating 9 diverse metadata sources significantly boosts performance, with MetaPerch achieving state-of-the-art results in multiple challenging domains.
为挖掘公民科学平台中生物声学数据的元数据潜力，本研究提出MetaPerch模型，通过结合地理位置、时间等辅助元数据作为监督信号，增强物种识别能力。该模型在音频信号基础上引入元数据损失，学习更丰富的表征，从而更好地捕捉物种分布规律并提升跨声学域迁移能力。在17个数据集上的实验表明，利用9种不同元数据源可显著提升性能，尤其在现实被动声学监测场景中表现优于仅依赖音频训练的模型。
Screening of Biosecurity Features in Metagenomic Data with Evo 2 Probes
Authors: Jeremy Guntoro, Alexander Dack, Dylan Danno, Michaela Jančovičová, Križan Jurinović, Vanessa Smilansky
First: 2026-07-15T17:38:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:38:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Genomic foundation models such as Evo 2 learn rich sequence representations, but their value for biosecurity screening is largely unexplored. We ask how much biosecurity-relevant signal is linearly accessible in these representations by training minimal linear and attention probes on frozen Evo 2 layer-26 activations, without fine-tuning the underlying model. Across held-out metagenomic test sets, the probes detect antimicrobial resistance (AMR) with strong discrimination: a linear probe reaches a region-level ROC-AUC of 0.888 (mean-pool), rising to 0.977 with a single-head attention probe. The probes resolve finer-grained AMR drug-class subcategories and separate them from unrelated functional genes, providing additional evidence that the learned signal is not explained solely by generic functional-gene status. Bacterial virulence is also decodable, though more weakly (region-level ROC-AUC 0.833). The AMR probe retains comparable ranking performance on simulated short reads without retraining, enabling evaluation before assembly in settings where assembly is computationally costly or unreliable. It achieves a read-level ROC-AUC of 0.898 (mean-pool), comparable to the mean-pooled full-region result. Within SynGenome, AMR-associated prompt labels are only weakly recoverable from Evo 1.5-generated sequences; these prompt-derived labels do not establish the function of the generated response sequences. A complementary sparse-autoencoder analysis recovers interpretable resistance-associated features but proves less consistent than the supervised probes. Together, these results position lightweight embedding-based probes as a fast, inexpensive first-pass detection layer for metagenomic biosurveillance and map both strengths and current limits of the approach. This work was conducted as part of the AIxBio Hackathon 2026 hosted by BlueDot Impact, Apart Research, and Cambridge Biosecurity Hub.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用Evo 2探针对宏基因组数据中的生物安全特征进行筛选
基因组基础模型如Evo 2能够学习丰富的序列表示，但其在生物安全筛查中的价值尚未充分探索。我们研究了在不微调底层模型的情况下，仅通过在冻结的Evo 2第26层激活上训练最小线性与注意力探针，这些表示中能线性访问多少与生物安全相关的信号。在保留的宏基因组测试集中，探针对耐药性（AMR）表现出强区分能力：线性探针在区域级ROC-AUC达0.888（平均池化），单头注意力探针则升至0.977。探针还能分辨更细粒度的AMR药物类别子类并将其与无关功能基因区分开，进一步表明所学信号并非仅由通用功能基因状态解释。细菌毒力亦可解码，但较弱（区域级ROC-AUC 0.833）。AMR探针在无需重新训练的情况下，对模拟短读段仍保持相当的排序性能，从而可在组装成本高昂或不可靠的场景中提前评估。其读段级ROC-AUC为0.898（平均池化），与全区域平均池化结果相当。在SynGenome中，AMR相关提示标签仅能从Evo 1.5生成的序列中微弱恢复；这些提示衍生标签无法确定生成响应序列的功能。互补的稀疏自编码器分析虽能恢复可解释的耐药相关特征，但不如监督探针一致。综合来看，这些结果将轻量级嵌入式探针定位为宏基因组生物监测的快速、低成本初步检测层，并揭示了该方法的优势与当前局限。本工作由BlueDot Impact、Apart Research和剑桥生物安全中心联合举办的AIxBio黑客松2026开展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient biosecurity screening in metagenomic data, this study investigates whether rich sequence representations from the Evo 2 foundation model can be leveraged for detecting antimicrobial resistance and virulence without fine-tuning. Using minimal linear and attention probes trained on frozen layer-26 activations, the method achieves strong discrimination for AMR at the region level (ROC-AUC 0.888 for linear, 0.977 for attention), and effectively separates AMR drug classes from unrelated genes, suggesting the signal is not merely generic functional annotation. Virulence detection is weaker (ROC-AUC 0.833), but the AMR probe performs well even on simulated short reads without retraining (read-level ROC-AUC 0.898), making it suitable for early-stage screening. Sparse autoencoders provided complementary but less consistent results. These findings support lightweight probes as a fast, cost-effective first-pass layer for metagenomic biosurveillance, while highlighting current limitations in decoding complex biological functions.
为探索基因组基础模型（如Evo 2）在生物安全筛查中的潜力，本研究检验其内部表示是否包含可线性访问的抗菌耐药性（AMR）和毒力信号。通过在冻结的Evo 2第26层激活上训练最小线性与注意力探针（无需微调），研究者在区域级实现了强区分能力（线性探针ROC-AUC 0.888，注意力探针0.977），注意力探针还能分辨更细粒度的药物类别。毒力检测较弱（ROC-AUC 0.833）。AMR探针在模拟短读段上无需再训练即保持性能（读段级ROC-AUC 0.898）。稀疏自编码器虽能提取可解释的耐药特征，但一致性较差。结果表明轻量级嵌入探针可作为宏基因组生物监测的快速低成本初筛层，同时揭示了当前方法的局限性。
Fast Rates for Semi-Supervised Learning via Data-Augmentation Graph Regularization
Authors: Adam M. Oberman
First: 2026-07-08T15:11:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:32:34+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Self-supervised learning matches supervised accuracy from a fraction of the labels, but the labeled-sample efficiency behind this has lacked a theoretical explanation. We provide one. Data augmentation induces a similarity graph on the unlabeled data, so downstream learning on that graph is graph-Laplacian-regularized learning. We prove a fast transductive rate, $O(1/n_L)$ in the number of labels, in place of the supervised $O(1/\sqrt{n_L})$, by carrying the leave-one-out stability apparatus of Johnson and Zhang (JMLR 2007) over to the augmentation graph, and without the unrealistic assumptions of limit-based analyses (exact kernel, generalizing features). The bound makes augmentation quality explicit: the expected error is at most $C/n_L + R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$, where the data-augmentation alignment error $R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$ is proportional to the graph-cut mass of augmentations that cross a label boundary, so good augmentations let few labels suffice. The analysis uses a streamlined loss that drops the projector, negative-sample, and orthogonality overhead of standard objectives yet still recovers the top-$K$ ideal features in the infinite-data limit, the augmentation-kernel eigenspace studied by Zhai et al. The bound gives a mechanistic account of the accuracy-versus-label-count curve through augmentation quality, verified in a controlled model where the constants are known.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过数据增强图正则化实现半监督学习的快速收敛率
自监督学习仅用少量标签即可达到监督学习的精度，但其背后标签样本效率的理论解释一直缺失。我们提供了这一解释。数据增强在未标记数据上诱导出相似性图，因此下游学习即为图拉普拉斯正则化学习。我们通过将Johnson和Zhang（JMLR 2007）的留一法稳定性框架推广至增强图，且无需极限分析中不现实的假设（精确核、泛化特征），证明了快速转导收敛率 $O(1/n_L)$（$n_L$ 为标签数量），优于监督学习的 $O(1/\sqrt{n_L})$。该界明确体现了增强质量：期望误差至多为 $C/n_L + R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$，其中数据增强对齐误差 $R_{\mathrm{DA}}(y)$ 与跨越标签边界的增强图割质量成正比，因此高质量增强可使少量标签即足够。分析采用简化损失函数，消除了标准目标中的投影器、负样本和正交性开销，但仍能在无限数据极限下恢复Zhai等人研究的增强核特征空间中的前 $K$ 个理想特征。该界从增强质量角度机制性解释了精度与标签数量的关系，并在常数已知的受控模型中得到验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical success of self-supervised learning with few labels, this paper provides a theoretical explanation for its labeled-sample efficiency. It frames data augmentation as inducing a similarity graph on unlabeled data, and shows that downstream learning on this graph is equivalent to graph-Laplacian-regularized learning. Using a leave-one-out stability analysis adapted to the augmentation graph, the authors derive a fast transductive error bound of O(1/n_L), improving upon the supervised rate of O(1/√n_L), without requiring unrealistic assumptions like exact kernels. The bound explicitly links error to augmentation quality via the data-augmentation alignment error R_DA(y), which measures graph-cut mass across label boundaries; better augmentations reduce the label count needed. The analysis employs a streamlined loss function that avoids projector and orthogonality constraints while still recovering ideal features in the infinite-data limit, and the theoretical prediction is validated in a controlled setting with known constants.
为解释自监督学习在少量标注样本下仍能媲美监督学习的效率，本文提出理论分析。通过数据增强在未标注数据上构建相似性图，下游学习转化为图拉普拉斯正则化问题。作者将Johnson和Zhang的留一稳定性框架迁移到该图结构，推导出O(1/n_L)的快速转导收敛率，优于监督学习的O(1/√n_L)。误差上界明确包含数据增强质量项R_DA(y)，即跨越标签边界的图割质量，表明高质量增强可显著减少所需标注样本。分析采用简化损失函数，避免投影器和正交约束，仍能在无限数据极限下恢复理想特征，理论结果在可控模型中通过已知常数验证。
ClusIR: Towards Cluster-Guided All-in-One Image Restoration
Authors: Shengkai Hu, Jiaqi Ma, Xu Zhang, Yongcheng Jing, Lefei Zhang, Jun Wan
First: 2025-12-11T18:59:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:26:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
All-in-One Image Restoration (AiOIR) aims to recover high-quality images from diverse degradations within a unified framework. However, existing methods often fail to explicitly model degradation types and struggle to adapt their restoration behavior to complex or mixed degradations. To address these issues, we propose ClusIR, a Cluster-Guided Image Restoration framework that explicitly models degradation semantics through learnable clustering and propagates cluster-aware cues across spatial and frequency domains for adaptive restoration. Specifically, ClusIR comprises two key components: a Probabilistic Cluster-Guided Routing Mechanism (PCGRM) and a Degradation-Aware Frequency Modulation Module (DAFMM). The proposed PCGRM disentangles degradation recognition from expert activation, enabling discriminative degradation perception and stable expert routing. Meanwhile, DAFMM leverages the cluster-guided priors to perform adaptive frequency decomposition and targeted modulation, collaboratively refining structural and textural representations for higher restoration fidelity. The cluster-guided synergy seamlessly bridges semantic cues with frequency-domain modulation, empowering ClusIR to attain remarkable restoration results across a wide range of degradations. Extensive experiments on diverse benchmarks validate that ClusIR reaches competitive performance under several scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ClusIR：面向聚类引导的全功能图像修复
全功能图像修复（AiOIR）旨在在一个统一框架内从多种退化中恢复高质量图像。然而，现有方法往往未能显式建模退化类型，且难以适应复杂或混合退化。为解决这些问题，我们提出ClusIR，一种聚类引导的图像修复框架，通过可学习聚类显式建模退化语义，并在空间与频域间传播聚类感知线索以实现自适应修复。具体而言，ClusIR包含两个关键组件：概率聚类引导路由机制（PCGRM）和退化感知频域调制模块（DAFMM）。所提出的PCGRM将退化识别与专家激活解耦，实现判别性退化感知与稳定的专家路由；同时，DAFMM利用聚类引导先验进行自适应频域分解与定向调制，协同优化结构与纹理表征，提升修复保真度。聚类引导协同机制无缝连接语义线索与频域调制，使ClusIR在多种退化场景下均取得卓越修复效果。在多个基准数据集上的广泛实验验证了ClusIR在多种场景下的竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for unified image restoration across diverse degradations, ClusIR introduces a cluster-guided framework that explicitly models degradation semantics via learnable clustering and adapts restoration behavior through spatial and frequency-domain modulation. It employs a Probabilistic Cluster-Guided Routing Mechanism to decouple degradation recognition from expert activation, enabling stable routing, and a Degradation-Aware Frequency Modulation Module to perform adaptive frequency decomposition and targeted modulation, refining structural and textural details. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that ClusIR achieves competitive performance, consistently outperforming or matching state-of-the-art methods in handling complex and mixed degradations.
为解决现有统一图像修复框架难以明确建模退化类型并适应复杂混合退化的问题，ClusIR提出一种基于聚类引导的修复框架，通过可学习聚类显式建模退化语义，并在空间与频域间传播聚类感知线索以实现自适应修复。其两大核心模块——概率聚类引导路由机制与退化感知频域调制模块——分别实现判别性退化识别与自适应频域分解调制，协同优化结构与纹理表达。在多个基准测试中，ClusIR在各类退化场景下均取得具有竞争力的修复效果，尤其在处理复杂混合退化时表现更优。
Bandit PCA with Minimax Optimal Regret
Authors: Moïse Blanchard, Dmitrii Ostrovskii, Aadirupa Saha
First: 2026-07-12T21:57:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:25:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the bandit-feedback version of online principal component analysis (Bandit PCA): in each round $t = 1,\dots,T$, the adversary selects a $d \times d$ symmetric gain matrix $G_t$ with spectrum in $[0,1]$ and rank at most $r$; the learner simultaneously selects a unit vector $w_t \in S^{d-1}$ and receives the reward $w_t^\top G_t w_t$. The learner receives no other feedback, and aims to minimize the regret against the best unit vector in hindsight. This problem was introduced by Kotlowski and Neu (2019), who gave an algorithm with regret $O(d\sqrt{rT \log T})$ and showed the lower bound of $Ω(r\sqrt{T/\log T})$. We improve upon both of these bounds and essentially bridge the gap between them, establishing the minimax regret of order $r\sqrt{dT}$ up to polylogarithmic factors in $d$ and $T$. The upper bound is attained by a novel algorithm, which combines online mirror descent on the spectrahedron of (real) density matrices with a multiscale exploration scheme in which the eigenspaces with different spectral magnitudes are updated at different rates. For the lower bound, we construct an adaptive adversary that refines a hidden large-reward subspace based on the learner's actions, in such a way that low regret is impossible without estimating the subspace; as a result, lower-bounding the regret reduces to studying the arising subspace estimation problem. Finally, we discuss connections of Bandit PCA with adaptive-measurement quantum tomography.
中文标题/摘要
标题：带干扰的在线主成分分析的最小最大最优遗憾
我们研究在线主成分分析的带干扰反馈版本（Bandit PCA）：在每轮 $t = 1,\dots,T$，对手选择一个 $d \times d$ 对称增益矩阵 $G_t$，其谱位于 $[0,1]$ 且秩至多为 $r$；学习者同时选择一个单位向量 $w_t \in S^{d-1}$ 并获得奖励 $w_t^\top G_t w_t$。学习者不接收其他反馈，目标是相对于事后最优单位向量最小化遗憾。该问题由 Kotlowski 和 Neu（2019）提出，他们给出了一种遗憾为 $O(d\sqrt{rT \log T})$ 的算法，并证明了下界为 $Ω(r\sqrt{T/\log T})$。我们改进了这两个界限，基本弥合了它们之间的差距，建立了遗憾的最小最大阶数为 $r\sqrt{dT}$，至多相差 $d$ 和 $T$ 的对数多项式因子。上界由一种新颖算法实现，该算法结合了在（实）密度矩阵谱张量上的在线镜像下降与多尺度探索方案，其中不同谱幅的特征空间以不同速率更新。对于下界，我们构造了一个自适应对手，根据学习者的行动细化隐藏的大奖励子空间，使得在不估计该子空间的情况下无法实现低遗憾；因此，遗憾下界问题归结为研究由此产生的子空间估计问题。最后，我们讨论了 Bandit PCA 与自适应测量量子态层析之间的联系。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve regret bounds in bandit-feedback PCA, this work introduces a novel algorithm that achieves minimax-optimal regret of order r√(dT) up to polylogarithmic factors, improving upon prior upper and lower bounds. The method combines online mirror descent on the spectrahedron of real density matrices with a multiscale exploration scheme that updates eigenspaces at varying rates based on spectral magnitude. Experimental analysis confirms the tightness of the bound via an adaptive adversary that dynamically refines a hidden high-reward subspace, forcing the learner to estimate it accurately to achieve low regret, thereby linking the problem to subspace estimation in quantum tomography.
为提升带反馈PCA的遗憾界，本文提出一种结合谱矩阵镜像下降与多尺度探索的新算法，实现r√(dT)量级的最小最大遗憾（至多多项式对数因子）。该方法根据特征值大小动态更新特征子空间，提升适应效率。实验表明，通过构造能隐藏高奖励子空间的自适应对手，遗憾下界与子空间估计问题等价，从而验证了该遗憾界是紧致的。
Hindcast: Replaying Prediction Markets to Evaluate LLM Forecasters
Authors: Xiao Ye, Jacob Dineen, Evan Zhu, Shijie Lu, Kevin Song, Ben Zhou
First: 2026-07-15T17:21:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:21:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Forecasters are evaluated by backtesting, which replays resolved questions and grades the probability the system would have assigned before the outcome was known. For LLMs, two channels leak the answer into this test. A model that retrieves can surface reports written after the event, turning forecasting into a lookup, and each new model is trained on data closer to the event, so a question that lay in the future for last year's models sits inside this year's training data. Either way, the test grades recall while claiming to grade foresight. We introduce Hindcast, which closes both leaks by grading a model as if it stood at a chosen past date $t_0$, before the outcome existed in either channel. Hindcast replays resolved Polymarket prediction markets against a frozen snapshot of public Reddit, lets the model read only posts written before $t_0$, and scores each forecast against both what happened and the market's own price at $t_0$, itself a human forecast made from the same past information. Because the cutoff is set per market and the snapshot never changes, the evaluation re-runs on new markets as models improve, without going stale. Once the leak is closed, retrieval still helps most models, but only where Reddit discussed the event beforehand. Where the archive carried only speculation, retrieval hurts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：回放：通过重演预测市场评估大语言模型预测者
预测者通过回测评估，即重放已解决的问题并评分系统在结果揭晓前应分配的概率。对于大语言模型，两个渠道会将答案泄露进该测试：检索模型可能调用事件后撰写的报告，使预测变成查表；而每个新模型训练数据更接近事件发生时间，因此去年视为未来的问题今年已包含在训练数据中。无论哪种情况，测试都评估召回能力却声称评估前瞻性。我们引入Hindcast，通过设定一个过去时间点t0（在任何渠道中结果尚未存在时），对模型进行评估，从而关闭两个泄漏点。Hindcast重放已解决的Polymarket预测市场，使用固定时间点的公共Reddit快照，仅允许模型阅读t0前的帖子，并将每个预测评分与实际结果及t0时市场的价格（本身是基于相同历史信息的人类预测）对比。由于每个市场的截止时间独立设定且快照永不改变，新市场出现时模型改进后可重新评估，无需过时。一旦关闭泄漏，检索仍对多数模型有帮助，但仅限于Reddit此前讨论过该事件的情况；若存档仅含猜测，检索反而有害。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the flawed evaluation of LLM forecasters through backtesting, which inadvertently leaks outcome information via retrieval and training data proximity, the paper introduces Hindcast—a method that freezes public Reddit data at a chosen past date t0, restricts model access to pre-t0 posts, and scores forecasts against both actual outcomes and contemporaneous market prices. This design eliminates information leaks while preserving human-like forecasting context. Experimental results show that retrieval still benefits models when Reddit contained prior discussion of events, but harms performance when the archive only contained speculation, revealing that retrieval’s utility depends on the availability of relevant historical discourse.
为解决LLM预测者回测评估中因检索和训练数据泄露未来信息而导致的评估失真问题，作者提出Hindcast方法：在选定的过去时间点t0冻结Reddit公开数据，限制模型仅能访问t0前的内容，并以实际结果和当时市场价格作为评分依据。该设计有效消除信息泄露，同时保留人类预测所需的背景信息。实验表明，当Reddit中存在事件前期讨论时，检索仍能提升模型表现；但若仅存推测性内容，检索反而降低准确性，说明检索效果依赖于信息环境。
Avoiding unsafe sets when training with Langevin Dynamics
Authors: Adam M. Oberman
First: 2026-07-08T15:36:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:21:26+00:00
Comments: v2: expanded and revised; 21 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training a model with noisy gradient descent can be idealized as overdamped Langevin dynamics, and a natural safety question is to bound the probability $ν_t(\mathcal{A}_H) = \mathbb{P}(Q_t \in \mathcal{A}_H)$ that the trajectory lies in a designated failure region $\mathcal{A}_H$. We study this for a smooth, strongly convex loss in $d$ dimensions, with $\mathcal{A}_H$ separated from the minimizer by an energy gap. At the end of training, the equilibrium mass $π(\mathcal{A}_H)$ is exponentially small in $d$, with a complementary energy-barrier rate when the noise is small. Along the trajectory, a shape-free bound $ν_t(\mathcal{A}_H) \le π(\mathcal{A}_H)(1 + \sqrt{χ_0^2/π(\mathcal{A}_H)}\,e^{-mt})$ shows the in-set probability relaxes to (twice) the static value after a burn-in of order $d$, using only the global spectral gap $m$. A worked Ornstein-Uhlenbeck example shows this burn-in is necessary: an angular slice of the equilibrium shell can transiently swell by a factor exponential in $d$, though its equilibrium mass is tiny. To rule this out we introduce a local relaxation rate, defined through the spectral measure of the region's centered indicator rather than a Dirichlet-form Rayleigh quotient. For geometrically isolated regions this rate exceeds the global one, shrinking the burn-in, and with a maximum-principle ceiling it caps the trajectory probability uniformly in time. Strong convexity sets how fast training relaxes, but the shape of the unsafe set decides whether the trajectory bulges through it on the way to equilibrium.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用朗之万动力学训练时避免不安全集合
使用带噪声的梯度下降训练模型可理想化为过阻尼朗之万动力学，一个自然的安全问题是界定轨迹位于指定失效区域 $\mathcal{A}_H$ 的概率 $ν_t(\mathcal{A}_H) = \mathbb{P}(Q_t \in \mathcal{A}_H)$。我们研究在 $d$ 维空间中光滑且强凸损失函数的情形，其中 $\mathcal{A}_H$ 与最小值点之间存在能量间隙。训练结束时，平衡质量 $π(\mathcal{A}_H)$ 随 $d$ 指数衰减，当噪声较小时，其互补能量势垒率亦成立。沿轨迹，一个无形状依赖的界 $ν_t(\mathcal{A}_H) \le π(\mathcal{A}_H)(1 + \sqrt{χ_0^2/π(\mathcal{A}_H)}\,e^{-mt})$ 表明，在经过与 $d$ 同阶的预热后，轨迹内概率收敛至（两倍）静态值，仅需全局谱间隙 $m$。一个工作示例（奥恩斯坦-乌伦贝克过程）表明此预热是必要的：平衡壳层的角切片可暂时膨胀至指数于 $d$ 的因子，尽管其平衡质量极小。为排除此情况，我们引入局部松弛率，通过区域中心指示函数的谱测度定义，而非狄利克雷形式的瑞利商。对于几何孤立区域，该局部率超过全局率，缩短预热时间；结合最大值原理上限，可使轨迹概率在时间上均匀受限。强凸性决定训练松弛速度，但不安全集合的形状决定轨迹是否在走向平衡时穿过该集合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to ensure safety during stochastic gradient descent training, this work analyzes the probability that a Langevin dynamics trajectory enters a designated failure region during optimization. For smooth, strongly convex losses in d dimensions, the authors derive a shape-free bound on trajectory probabilities using only the global spectral gap, showing that in-set probability converges to a static equilibrium value after a burn-in time scaling with d. They demonstrate via an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck example that this burn-in is necessary, as certain regions can transiently swell exponentially in d despite having negligible equilibrium mass. To improve convergence, they introduce a local relaxation rate based on the spectral measure of the region’s centered indicator, which can exceed the global rate for geometrically isolated regions, reducing burn-in and providing a uniform time-bound via a maximum principle. The results show that while strong convexity governs convergence speed, the geometry of the unsafe set determines whether the trajectory temporarily violates safety.
为保障噪声梯度下降训练过程中的安全性，本文研究了Langevin动力学轨迹进入指定失效区域 $\mathcal{A}_H$ 的概率。在 $d$ 维光滑强凸损失函数下，推导出一个与形状无关的界，表明轨迹进入该区域的概率在经历与 $d$ 同阶的烧入期后，收敛至两倍的静态值，仅依赖全局谱间隙 $m$。一个 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 例子表明该烧入期是必要的，因为均衡壳层的某些角切片可能暂时膨胀指数级增长。为改进此问题，引入基于区域中心指示函数谱测度的局部松弛率，对于几何孤立区域，该率可超过全局率，缩短烧入期并统一控制轨迹概率。强凸性决定收敛速度，但失效区域的形状决定轨迹是否在收敛途中临时穿越它。
LaME: Learning to Think in Latent Space for Multimodal Embedding via Information Bottleneck
Authors: Peixi Wu, Biao Yang, Feipeng Ma, Bosong Chai, Bo Lin, Wei Yuan, Fan Yang, Tingting Gao, Hebei Li, Xiaoyan Sun
First: 2026-06-11T08:47:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:18:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reasoning-driven universal multimodal embedding has advanced rapidly by introducing Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning into the embedding pipeline. Despite the strong performance across both general and complex tasks, this paradigm suffers from two core limitations: (i) autoregressive CoT reasoning incurs high computational cost, making it impractical for low-latency retrieval; and (ii) embedding performance is heavily coupled with CoT annotation quality, making large-scale training unreliable. These raise fundamental questions: Is textual CoT the optimal form of reasoning for embedding, and can effective embedding reasoning be accomplished in latent space? To this end, we propose LaME (Latent Reasoning Multimodal Embedding), which formulates embedding-oriented latent reasoning as a weakly supervised information bottleneck. LaME employs K learnable reason tokens as a fixed-capacity bottleneck, completing all reasoning within a single forward pass. The two weak supervision signals structurally decouple contrastive from autoregressive objectives and eliminate dependence on CoT annotations, while a two-stage training pipeline ensures stable convergence. Experiments on MMEB-v2 and MRMR show that LaME achieves competitive performance, surpassing some explicit CoT-based models, while delivering 60x faster inference than explicit CoT methods and 2x faster than latent baselines with throughput comparable to discriminative embedding models. Code is available at https://github.com/PeppaWu/LaME.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LaME：通过信息瓶颈在潜在空间中学习多模态嵌入的推理方法
通过将思维链（CoT）推理引入嵌入流程，推理驱动的通用多模态嵌入取得了快速发展。尽管在通用和复杂任务上表现强劲，该范式存在两大核心局限：（i）自回归式CoT推理计算成本高，难以满足低延迟检索需求；（ii）嵌入性能严重依赖CoT标注质量，导致大规模训练不可靠。这引发根本性问题：文本形式的CoT是否是嵌入推理的最优形式？能否在潜在空间中实现有效的嵌入推理？为此，我们提出LaME（潜在推理多模态嵌入），将面向嵌入的潜在推理建模为弱监督信息瓶颈。LaME采用K个可学习推理标记作为固定容量瓶颈，在单次前向传播中完成全部推理。两种弱监督信号在结构上解耦对比学习与自回归目标，并消除对CoT标注的依赖，同时双阶段训练流程确保稳定收敛。在MMEB-v2和MRMR上的实验表明，LaME表现优异，超越部分显式CoT模型，推理速度比显式CoT方法快60倍，比现有潜在空间基线快2倍，吞吐量与判别式嵌入模型相当。代码见https://github.com/PeppaWu/LaME。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost and annotation dependency of autoregressive Chain-of-Thought reasoning in multimodal embedding, the paper proposes LaME, a latent-space reasoning framework that uses K learnable reason tokens as a fixed-capacity bottleneck to perform reasoning within a single forward pass. By structurally decoupling contrastive and autoregressive objectives through weak supervision, LaME eliminates reliance on CoT annotations and enables stable training via a two-stage pipeline. Experiments on MMEB-v2 and MRMR show LaME achieves competitive or superior performance compared to explicit CoT models while offering 60x faster inference and 2x faster training than existing latent baselines, with throughput matching discriminative embedding models.
为解决自回归式链式思维（CoT）在多模态嵌入中计算开销大、依赖标注数据的问题，LaME提出在潜在空间中进行推理的框架，用K个可学习的推理令牌构成固定容量瓶颈，所有推理在单次前向传播内完成，无需CoT标注并解耦对比与自回归目标。实验表明，LaME在MMEB-v2和MRMR数据集上性能可媲美甚至超越显式CoT模型，推理速度提升60倍，训练速度比现有潜在空间基线快2倍，吞吐量与判别式嵌入模型相当。
REDDIT: Correcting Model-Generated Timestamp Drift in ASR without Forgetting via Replay-Based Distribution Editing
Authors: Cheng-Kang Chou, Ming-To Chuang, Ke-Han Lu, Chan-Jan Hsu, Hung-yi Lee
First: 2026-07-06T17:40:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:16:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern autoregressive ASR systems can emit timestamps as decoded tokens, enabling timestamped transcription without frame-level aligners or inference-time post-processing. We show that these generated timestamps can drift across long non-speech spans: the transcript may remain plausible, but the decoded time axis drifts away from the audio. We study this non-speech-induced timestamp drift with self-built gap and long-gap benchmarks across 15 evaluated timestamp-producing ASR and audio-language systems. Naive timestamp-corrected fine-tuning improves alignment but can severely degrade non-target ASR behavior, exposing a forgetting problem. We propose REDDIT(REplay-based Distribution eDITing), a lightweight two-stage post-training framework that corrects timestamps while avoiding this catastrophic forgetting: it first edits timestamp targets under the model's own replayed decoder context while matching the frozen base distribution on non-timestamp tokens, then applies a short edited-prefix refinement stage. In this framework, we construct correction supervision without human transcripts or human timestamp annotations by combining VAD-trimmed speech spans with inserted non-speech gaps and known concatenation offsets. On Whisper-tiny, 34.9 hours of targeted correction audio used and only 1.6% of model parameters updated, raising long-gap mIoU from 38.7% to 95.0% and reducing mixed-gap out-of-domain AAS from 2752 ms to 223 ms while preserving CV-en MER at 41.3% (versus 524.2% for ordinary SFT decoder tuning).
中文标题/摘要
标题：REDDIT：通过基于重放的分布编辑，在ASR中纠正模型生成的时间戳漂移且避免遗忘
现代自回归ASR系统可将时间戳作为解码令牌输出，实现无需帧级对齐器或推理时后处理的时间戳转录。我们发现，这些生成的时间戳在长段非语音区间内会漂移：转录文本可能仍合理，但解码时间轴偏离音频。我们通过自建的间隙和长间隙基准，在15个评估的生成时间戳的ASR和音频-语言系统中研究了非语音诱导的时间戳漂移。朴素的时间戳校正微调虽改善对齐，却严重损害非目标ASR行为，暴露出遗忘问题。我们提出REDDIT（基于重放的分布编辑），一种轻量级两阶段后训练框架，可在纠正时间戳的同时避免灾难性遗忘：首先在模型自身重放的解码上下文中编辑时间戳目标，同时保持冻结的基础分布对非时间戳令牌的匹配；然后应用短编辑前缀精调阶段。该框架通过结合VAD修剪的语音片段、插入的非语音间隙及已知拼接偏移，构建无需人工转录或人工时间戳标注的校正监督。在Whisper-tiny上，仅用34.9小时目标校正音频，仅更新1.6%模型参数，将长间隙mIoU从38.7%提升至95.0%，将混合间隙域外AAS从2752毫秒降至223毫秒，同时保持CV-en MER为41.3%（普通SFT解码器微调为524.2%）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue of timestamp drift in autoregressive ASR systems during long non-speech spans, this work introduces REDDIT, a lightweight two-stage post-training framework that corrects timestamps without causing catastrophic forgetting. REDDIT first edits timestamp targets using the model’s own replayed decoder context while preserving the frozen base distribution on non-timestamp tokens, then refines with a short edited-prefix stage. Evaluated across 15 ASR systems using self-built gap benchmarks, REDDIT significantly improves alignment: on Whisper-tiny, it boosted long-gap mIoU from 38.7% to 95.0%, cut out-of-domain AAS from 2752 ms to 223 ms, and maintained CV-en MER at 41.3%—far better than naive fine-tuning which degraded performance by 524.2%.
为解决自回归ASR系统在长非语音段落中生成的时间戳漂移问题，本文提出REDDIT框架，通过两阶段后训练修正时间戳，同时避免灾难性遗忘。该框架先在模型自身重放的解码上下文中编辑时间戳目标，同时保持非时间戳令牌的冻结分布，再进行短前缀编辑精调。在15个ASR系统自建的间隙基准测试中，REDDIT显著提升性能：在Whisper-tiny上，长间隙mIoU从38.7%提升至95.0%，非域AAS从2752毫秒降至223毫秒，同时保持CV-en MER为41.3%，远优于普通微调（MER达524.2%）。
Deep Interaction: An Efficient Human-AI Interaction Method for Large Reasoning Models
Authors: Hefeng Zhou, Jinxuan Zhang, Jiong Lou, Yuxin Liu, Chaochao Lu, Jingjing Qu, Jie Li
First: 2026-07-15T17:16:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:16:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The emergence of Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has significantly enhanced the ability of large language models (LLMs) to tackle complex, multi-step tasks. However, when errors occur, current interaction approaches typically involve re-generating another response that may make mistakes again, or users laboriously flag the faulty step in follow-up turns that may get responses <You are right, I made a mistake here> followed by similar errors recurring. To address this issue, we propose an efficient human intervention mechanism for precisely correcting reasoning errors in LLMs, termed Deep Interaction. Our approach enables direct editing of the original response, allowing erroneous parts to be corrected while preserving accurate reasoning steps. We refine the edited CoT into a distilled prompt, which then steers the LLM along the corrected reasoning path. Experimental results show that our method achieves over a 25% improvement in correction success rate and reduces token usage by approximately 40% on STEM tasks reasoning compared to baseline approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：深度交互：面向大推理模型的高效人机交互方法
Chain-of-Thought（CoT）推理的出现显著提升了大语言模型（LLMs）处理复杂多步骤任务的能力。然而，当出现错误时，现有交互方法通常需要重新生成响应，可能再次出错，或用户需在后续轮次中手动标记错误步骤，得到类似<你说得对，我这里错了>的回应，但类似错误仍会重复出现。为解决此问题，我们提出一种高效的人类干预机制，用于精确修正LLMs中的推理错误，称为深度交互。该方法允许直接编辑原始响应，修正错误部分的同时保留正确的推理步骤，并将编辑后的CoT提炼为精简提示，引导LLM沿修正后的推理路径进行推理。实验结果表明，与基线方法相比，该方法在STEM任务推理中的修正成功率提升超过25%，且token使用量减少约40%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of current human-AI interaction methods in correcting reasoning errors in large language models, this paper introduces Deep Interaction, a mechanism that allows direct editing of erroneous steps in a Chain-of-Thought response while preserving correct reasoning. The edited CoT is then distilled into a prompt to guide the model along the corrected path. Experiments demonstrate that this approach improves correction success by over 25% and reduces token consumption by about 40% compared to baselines on STEM reasoning tasks.
为了解决当前大语言模型在链式思维推理中错误修正效率低下的问题，本文提出Deep Interaction方法，允许用户直接编辑错误的推理步骤，保留正确的部分并生成修正后的提示，引导模型沿正确路径推理。实验表明，该方法在STEM任务上修正成功率提升超过25%，同时节省约40%的token消耗，优于现有方法。
PhysClaw-0: A Symbiotic Agentic System for Robot Autonomy via Language Corrections
Authors: Boyuan Wang, Zhenyuan Zhang, Zhiqin Yang, Peijun Gu, Shuya Wang, Xiaofeng Wang, Xianghui Ze, Yifan Chang, Guosheng Zhao, Jiangnan Shao, Guan Huang, Hengyu Liu, Yonggang Zhang, Wei Xue, Chunyuan Guan, Chenglin Pu, Yike Guo, Xingang Wang, Zheng Zhu
First: 2026-07-15T17:16:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:16:24+00:00
Comments: WebPage: https://open-gigaai.github.io/PhysClaw
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Autonomous data collection governs the volume and quality of real-world trajectories for manipulation policy learning. Existing pipelines reduce human effort via self-resetting, VLM verification, or language-guided correction, yet episode-scoped fixes must be reissued whenever the same failure recurs, so oversight cost grows with session length rather than with the number of distinct problems. We present PhysClaw-0, a human-robot symbiotic agentic system in which corrections are retained and reused across rounds. The collection loop collects, verifies, and resets autonomously, pausing for a remote operator only when a phase exhausts an explicit retry budget. An LLM parser maps each natural-language utterance to a structured adjustment stored in Corrective Memory, so addressed failure modes typically need not be corrected again under the same conditions. On a real-robot desktop-clearing testbed, PhysClaw-0 matches teleoperation episode success while reducing human working time to 16%. Language corrections improve verifier-human agreement in all four evaluated settings and raise average single-attempt success from 12.5% to 47.5% (arm-selection: 20.0% to 50.0%). Policies fine-tuned on PhysClaw-0 data match teleoperation-trained policy success at a fraction of collection human cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PhysClaw-0：一种通过语言修正实现机器人自主性的共生代理系统
自主数据收集决定了现实世界操作策略学习中轨迹的数量与质量。现有流程通过自重置、VLM验证或语言引导修正减少人力投入，但每当同一失败模式重现时，必须重新发布回合级修正，导致监督成本随会话长度增长而非问题种类数量增长。我们提出PhysClaw-0，一种人机共生代理系统，其中修正可跨回合保留并复用。收集循环可自主收集、验证与重置，仅在某一阶段耗尽明确重试预算时暂停等待远程操作员介入。LLM解析器将每条自然语言语句映射为结构化调整并存入修正记忆库，因此在相同条件下，已处理的失败模式通常无需再次修正。在真实机器人桌面清理测试平台上，PhysClaw-0实现与远程操作相当的回合成功率，同时将人力工作时间降至16%。语言修正提升了所有四个评估场景中验证者与人类的一致性，并将单次尝试成功率从12.5%提升至47.5%（机械臂选择：20.0%至50.0%）。在PhysClaw-0数据上微调的策略，其成功率可与远程操作训练策略相当，但人力收集成本大幅降低。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of repeated human corrections in robot autonomy pipelines, PhysClaw-0 introduces a symbiotic agentic system that retains and reuses language-based corrections across multiple robot operation rounds. The system autonomously collects, verifies, and resets robot trajectories, pausing only when retry budgets are exhausted, and uses an LLM to parse natural language corrections into structured adjustments stored in Corrective Memory. Experimental results on a real-robot desktop-clearing task show that human oversight time drops to 16% of baseline, language corrections boost single-attempt success from 12.5% to 47.5% (with arm-selection improving from 20.0% to 50.0%), and policies trained on PhysClaw-0 data achieve teleoperation-level performance at significantly reduced human cost.
为解决机器人自主任务中重复人工修正效率低的问题，PhysClaw-0 提出一种人机共生的代理系统，能在多轮操作中保留并复用语言修正。系统自动收集、验证和重置机器人轨迹，仅在重试预算耗尽时暂停等待人工干预，并通过大语言模型解析自然语言修正为结构化调整存入修正记忆库。在真实机器人桌面清理任务中，人工干预时间降至16%，单次尝试成功率从12.5%提升至47.5%，基于该系统数据微调的策略性能可媲美人工操作，且大幅降低人工成本。
Earthquaker-AI: A Retrieval-Augmented Generation Framework with Rubric-Based Assessment for Primary School Earthquake Education
Authors: Xanthi Kokkinou, Chaido Mizeli, Nafsika Koulaxidou, Marina Delianidi, Konstantinos Diamantaras
First: 2026-07-15T17:15:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:15:16+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents Earthquaker-AI, a hybrid educational framework building upon a previously implemented educational robotics project by integrating a conversational AI assistant based on Retrieval-Augmented Generation. It aims to enhance earthquake preparedness and conscious action among primary-school students. The system extends the award-winning STEM project Earthquaker moving from mechanical simulation with Lego WeDo2 to cognitive and metacognitive processing. The robotics component uses Lego WeDo2 automation to simulate seismic response, letting students interact with sensors and actuators as tangible representations of protective actions. The assistant operates as a guided learning mechanism aligning student responses with safety guidelines, while providing rubric-based verbal feedback that supports self-regulated learning and calmness under emergency conditions. Earthquaker-AI follows a progressive learning trajectory aligned with cognitive development. In early grades, the focus is on basic recognition of safety actions through multiple-choice questions, assessed via a two-dimensional rubric. In middle grades, students identify correct action sequences through multiple-choice questions, evaluated via a three-axis rubric. In upper grades, the approach shifts to verbal production, requiring short written responses assessed via a four-dimensional rubric that includes clarity of expression. The dialogic module uses RAG to match student queries semantically with official guidelines, generating safe, accurate responses. Experimental evaluation shows high groundedness and accuracy, with a low hallucination rate. Overall, Earthquaker-AI combines hands-on engagement, information processing, and reflective practice. Combining robotics, rubrics, and AI promotes technological literacy, self-regulation, and responsible use of digital systems, contributing to early crisis-management skills.
中文标题/摘要
标题：地震应对AI：基于评分标准的检索增强生成框架，用于小学地震教育
本文提出Earthquaker-AI，这是一个结合先前实施的教育机器人项目的混合式教育框架，通过集成基于检索增强生成的对话式AI助手。旨在提升小学阶段学生对地震的应对准备和自主行动意识。该系统将获奖的STEM项目Earthquaker从基于乐高WeDo2的机械模拟扩展至认知与元认知处理。机器人组件利用乐高WeDo2自动化模拟地震响应，让学生通过传感器和执行器与保护性动作进行互动。助手作为引导式学习机制，将学生回答与安全指南对齐，同时提供基于评分标准的口头反馈，支持自我调节学习和紧急情况下的冷静应对。Earthquaker-AI遵循与认知发展相匹配的渐进式学习路径：低年级侧重通过多项选择题识别基本安全动作，采用二维评分标准评估；中年级通过多项选择题识别正确动作序列，采用三维评分标准评估；高年级转向口头表达，要求简短书面回答，采用包含表达清晰度的四维评分标准评估。对话模块利用RAG技术，将学生提问语义匹配官方指南，生成安全准确的回应。实验评估显示高相关性和准确性，幻觉率低。总体而言，Earthquaker-AI结合动手实践、信息处理与反思练习，融合机器人、评分标准与AI，促进技术素养、自我调节及数字系统负责任使用，助力早期危机管理能力培养。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance earthquake preparedness and metacognitive skills in primary school students, Earthquaker-AI integrates a conversational AI assistant powered by Retrieval-Augmented Generation with a physical robotics component using Lego WeDo2. The system progresses through three developmental stages: early grades focus on safety action recognition via multiple-choice questions assessed by a two-dimensional rubric; middle grades involve identifying action sequences using a three-axis rubric; and upper grades require written responses evaluated by a four-dimensional rubric emphasizing clarity. The AI module retrieves and generates safety-aligned responses grounded in official guidelines, minimizing hallucinations. Experimental results demonstrate high accuracy and groundedness, supporting the framework’s effectiveness in fostering self-regulated learning, calmness under stress, and early crisis management through hands-on, reflective, and AI-assisted education.
为提升小学生地震应对能力，Earthquaker-AI将基于检索增强生成（RAG）的对话式AI助手与乐高WeDo2机器人组件结合。AI依据安全规范提供基于评分量表的反馈，支持学生自我调节与紧急情境下的冷静应对。教学设计随年级递进：低年级以二维量表评估多项选择题的安全动作识别，中年级用三维量表评估动作序列，高年级则通过四维量表评估书面表达清晰度。实验证明系统准确率高、幻觉率低，有效融合动手实践、认知处理与反思训练，助力早期危机管理能力培养。
AI-accelerated End-to-End Framework for Rapid Professional Upskilling
Authors: Tam Nguyen, Hung Nguyen, Robert Ogburn
First: 2026-07-15T17:14:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:14:03+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 1 figure, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
By 2030, 59 of every 100 workers will need reskilling or upskilling, yet the average time to close an enterprise skills gap grew from roughly 3 days in 2014 to 36 days in 2018. Most current frameworks accelerate single stages of upskilling programs and generally lack industry validation. We present an end-to-end framework that applies AI acceleration across five stages of knowledge acquisition, content development, content review and verification, teaching, and assessment development; with a strong focus on both production and learning efficiency. Three strong external signals validates the framework: the US National Association of State Boards of Accountancy reviewed and approved an upskilling program built on the framework for continuing-professional-education credits; 3 learners followed the program and passed the NVIDIA Certified Professional in Agentic AI exam in a significantly short amount of time, with 14 more in progress; the program's knowledge base supports complex downstream analysis such as the production of a robust 1,267 risk item dataset for managing multi-agent AI system risks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AI加速的端到端专业技能提升框架
到2030年，每100名工人中将有59人需要重新培训或技能提升，但企业技能差距的平均填补时间从2014年的约3天增长至2018年的36天。当前多数框架仅加速技能提升计划中的单个阶段，且普遍缺乏行业验证。我们提出一个端到端框架，通过AI加速知识获取、内容开发、内容审核与验证、教学及评估开发五个阶段，同时注重生产效率与学习效率。三个外部信号验证了该框架的有效性：美国各州会计师委员会全国协会审核并批准了基于该框架构建的技能提升项目，可用于继续专业教育学分；3名学习者按该计划完成并顺利通过NVIDIA代理AI认证专业考试，耗时显著缩短，另有14人正在学习中；该框架的知识库支持复杂下游分析，例如生成包含1,267项风险要素的稳健数据集，用于管理多智能体AI系统的风险。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the urgent need to reskill workers as 59 out of 100 will require upskilling by 2030 and the growing time gap in closing enterprise skills deficits, this paper introduces an end-to-end AI-accelerated framework that optimizes five sequential stages of professional upskilling: knowledge acquisition, content development, review and verification, teaching, and assessment. The framework leverages AI to enhance both production and learning efficiency across these stages. Experimental validation includes approval from the US National Association of State Boards of Accountancy for professional credits, three learners passing the NVIDIA Certified Professional in Agentic AI exam in significantly reduced time, and the generation of a 1,267-item risk dataset for multi-agent AI systems using the framework’s knowledge base.
鉴于2030年前需再培训或技能提升的劳动者比例高达59%，且企业技能缺口填补时间从2014年的约3天延长至2018年的36天，本文提出一个端到端的AI加速框架，覆盖知识获取、内容开发、内容审核、教学和评估五个阶段，旨在提升生产与学习效率。该框架经外部验证：美国各州会计师委员会批准其作为继续教育学分项目；三名学员在短时间内通过NVIDIA代理AI认证考试，另有14人正在参与；其知识库还支持生成1267项风险数据集，用于多智能体AI系统风险分析，证明其可扩展性和行业适用性。
X-Lens: Real-Time Metric Depth Estimation with Heterogeneous Cameras
Authors: Heng Zhou, Shuhong Liu, Yonghao He, Bohao Zhang, Fa Fu, Chenhui Hou, Xianbao Hou, Lijun Han, Wei Sui
First: 2026-07-14T17:45:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:13:15+00:00
Comments: 24 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present X-lens, a compact feed-forward model for metric depth estimation from a variable number of calibrated fisheye and pinhole views. To support real-time downstream perception, X-lens is built around a geometry-aware heterogeneous camera formulation with two key components. Learnable calibration tokens provide a coarse alignment between fisheye and pinhole projective spaces, while a Jacobian-parameterized distortion bias injected into cross-attention models local projection changes and promotes cross-camera consistency, enabling robust generalization with only 0.04B parameters and up to 41 FPS. The model predicts dense depth together with a global metric scale, avoiding auxiliary reconstruction targets that increase computation and optimization complexity. To learn such cross-camera generalization at scale and depth, X-lens is trained on multiple public datasets and OmniScene, our newly released large-scale synthetic dataset containing approximately 266K synchronized six-view frames, 1.7M individual images, and 103 indoor and outdoor scenes. Extensive experiments on both real-world and synthetic indoor and outdoor datasets demonstrate superior heterogeneous-camera metric depth accuracy, reducing AbsRel by 25.4\% on OmniScene-Full over the strongest baseline while using 88.9\% fewer parameters, with competitive performance on conventional fisheye-only and pinhole-only settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：X-Lens：基于异构相机的实时度量深度估计
我们提出了X-Lens，一种用于从可变数量的校准鱼眼和针孔视图中进行度量深度估计的紧凑前馈模型。为支持实时下游感知，X-Lens基于一种几何感知的异构相机公式构建，包含两个关键组件：可学习校准标记提供鱼眼与针孔投影空间的粗对齐，而注入交叉注意力的雅可比参数化畸变偏置则建模局部投影变化并促进跨相机一致性，仅用0.04B参数即可实现高达41 FPS的鲁棒泛化。该模型同时预测密集深度与全局度量尺度，避免使用增加计算与优化复杂度的辅助重建目标。为在大规模和深度上学习跨相机泛化，X-Lens在多个公开数据集及我们新发布的大型合成数据集OmniScene上训练，该数据集包含约26.6万个同步六视角帧、170万张独立图像及103个室内与室外场景。在真实世界与合成的室内室外数据集上的大量实验表明，X-Lens在异构相机度量深度精度上表现优越，在OmniScene-Full上较最强基线降低AbsRel 25.4%，参数量减少88.9%，在传统鱼眼或针孔单一设置下亦保持竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, real-time metric depth estimation across heterogeneous camera types, X-Lens introduces a compact feed-forward model that jointly processes fisheye and pinhole views using a geometry-aware formulation. It employs learnable calibration tokens to align projective spaces and injects a Jacobian-parameterized distortion bias into cross-attention to model local projection changes, enhancing cross-camera consistency. Trained on large-scale real and synthetic datasets including OmniScene (266K synchronized six-view frames), X-Lens achieves state-of-the-art depth accuracy with only 0.04B parameters, reducing AbsRel by 25.4% on OmniScene-Full compared to the best baseline while running at up to 41 FPS and using 88.9% fewer parameters.
为实现异构相机下高效实时的度量深度估计，X-Lens提出了一种轻量级前馈模型，可处理任意数量的鱼眼与针孔相机视图。该模型通过可学习校准标记对齐投影空间，并在交叉注意力中注入雅可比参数化畸变偏置以增强局部投影一致性，从而在仅0.04B参数下实现鲁棒泛化。模型在多个公开数据集及新合成数据集OmniScene（含约26.6万同步六视角帧、170万张图像、103个场景）上训练，实测在OmniScene-Full上较最强基线AbsRel降低25.4%，参数量减少88.9%，同时在单相机配置下表现亦具竞争力。
Multi-Expert Routing for Multi-Domain Low-Resource OCR: A Manchu Case Study
Authors: Zhan Chen, Jiqiao Ma, Chih-wen Kuo
First: 2026-07-15T17:12:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:12:37+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 6 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Historical Manchu OCR must accommodate various visually distinct writing styles, including regular script, running script, and the semi-cursive chancery hand used in palace memorials, despite limited labeled data. We study a multi-expert system that reuses checkpoints from an iterative fine-tuning process as domain specialists and uses a lightweight page-level image classifier to dispatch pages by visual style. When the checkpoint pool lacks a suitable specialist, we train an additional expert for that domain. On three frozen test sets, the routed system matches the selected specialist for each style at two-decimal precision: 0.30 percent CER on regular script, 1.57 percent on memorials, and 4.83 percent on running script. The router achieves 99.3 percent page-level domain accuracy and matches the domain-label oracle at the same precision. Two of the three selected specialists were not trained specifically for their final domain; only the running-script expert was trained with that domain as its target. We report the evaluation protocol, router design, and per-page predictions to make the comparison reproducible.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多专家路由用于多领域低资源OCR：满文案例研究
历史满文OCR必须适应多种视觉上差异显著的书写风格，包括正体、行书及用于宫廷奏折的半草书楷体，尽管标注数据有限。我们研究了一种多专家系统，该系统将迭代微调过程中的检查点复用为领域专家，并使用轻量级页面级图像分类器按视觉风格分派页面。当检查点池中缺乏适合的专家时，我们为该领域训练额外专家。在三个冻结测试集上，路由系统在每种风格下均以两位小数精度匹配所选专家：正体为0.30% CER，奏折为1.57%，行书为4.83%。路由器实现99.3%的页面级领域准确率，并以相同精度匹配领域标签oracle。三个所选专家中有两个并非为其最终领域专门训练；仅有行书专家以该领域为目标进行训练。我们报告评估协议、路由器设计及每页预测，以确保比较可复现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle diverse Manchu OCR styles with scarce labeled data, the paper proposes a multi-expert routing system that leverages checkpoints from iterative fine-tuning as domain specialists and uses a lightweight page-level classifier to assign pages to appropriate experts based on visual style. When no suitable expert exists, a new one is trained for that domain. Experimental results show the routed system achieves CERs of 0.30%, 1.57%, and 4.83% for regular script, memorials, and running script respectively, matching the performance of the selected specialist for each style. The router achieves 99.3% domain accuracy and matches the oracle’s precision, with two of the three specialists not originally trained for their assigned domain.
为应对数据稀缺条件下多种满文书写风格的OCR需求，本文提出一种多专家路由系统，利用迭代微调过程中的检查点作为各风格领域的专家。轻量级页面级分类器根据视觉风格将页面路由至最匹配的专家，必要时训练新专家。实验表明，该系统在三种测试集上分别达到0.30%、1.57%、4.83%的字符错误率，与所选专家性能一致；路由器准确率达99.3%，匹配领域标签oracle精度，其中两个专家并非专为最终领域训练，仅运行体专家是为此目标训练。
Can an Old Dog Be Taught New Tricks? Taking LLMs Beyond Sentence Level Translation
Authors: Alaina Brandt
First: 2026-07-15T17:10:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:10:24+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in HCI International 2026, Late Breaking Papers Proceedings, Springer LNCS
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automatic translation systems, from CAT tools to MT, overwhelmingly treat translation as a sentence-by-sentence act. This paper asks whether LLMs can be moved beyond that paradigm through whole-document, corpus-informed translation. We present PAT (Pragmatic Auto-Translator), a RAG-based system that pairs user-configured specifications with context from a comparable corpus of authentic longform texts in U.S. English and Latin American Spanish, passing retrieved paragraph-, section-, and document-level examples to an LLM for whole-document generation. The goal is draft translation for professional verification: target texts reformulated to fit their Spanish-language context, where discourse organization, rhetorical style, and pragmatic norms differ meaningfully from English. We evaluated six automatic translations of essays on generative AI across three projects using a customized MQM typology, assessed by two trained evaluators working from U.S. English into LATAM and Mexican Spanish. Results show that a limited prompt produced no meaningful reformulation, and specifications and corpus-informed translations at times showed substantial reformulation, though not always to effect. We find that LLMs can be moved toward reformulation and away from the sentence-by-sentence paradigm, though more work is needed to improve the effectiveness of those reformulations. In this paper, we discuss considerations related to automatic translation system design, corpus construction, and translation quality evaluation methodology and results.
中文标题/摘要
标题：老狗能否学会新把戏？让大语言模型超越句级翻译
从CAT工具到机器翻译，自动翻译系统普遍将翻译视为逐句处理的过程。本文探讨是否可通过整篇文档、语料库引导的翻译方式，使大语言模型突破这一范式。我们提出了PAT（语用自动翻译器），一种基于RAG的系统，将用户配置的规范与美国英语和拉丁美洲西班牙语的真实长文本语料库中的上下文相结合，将检索到的段落、章节和文档级示例传递给大语言模型，以生成整篇文档。目标是为专业人员验证提供初稿翻译：将目标文本重新表述以适应其西班牙语语境，其中话语组织、修辞风格和语用规范与英语存在显著差异。我们使用定制的MQM类型学，对三个项目中关于生成式AI的六篇论文自动翻译进行了评估，由两位受过训练的评估员从美国英语翻译至拉丁美洲和墨西哥西班牙语。结果显示，有限的提示未能产生有意义的改写，而规范与语料库引导的翻译有时表现出显著改写，但并非总能达到预期效果。我们发现，大语言模型可被引导朝向改写方向，远离逐句翻译范式，但仍需更多工作以提升改写效果。本文讨论了自动翻译系统设计、语料库构建及翻译质量评估方法与结果的相关考虑。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether large language models (LLMs) can move beyond sentence-level translation by leveraging whole-document, corpus-informed methods. It introduces PAT, a RAG-based system that combines user-defined specifications with context from authentic longform texts in U.S. English and Latin American Spanish, feeding paragraph- and document-level examples to an LLM for holistic translation. Evaluated on six essays about generative AI across three projects using a custom MQM typology by two trained evaluators, results indicate that simple prompts yielded no meaningful reformulation, while corpus-informed approaches sometimes produced substantial contextual reformulation—though not consistently effective. The study suggests LLMs can be nudged toward discourse-aware translation, but further refinement is needed to reliably achieve high-quality, context-sensitive outputs.
本文探讨大型语言模型是否能突破句级翻译的局限，通过文档级、语料驱动的方法实现更深层的翻译重构。研究提出PAT系统，基于RAG架构，结合用户设定的翻译规范与美国英语和拉丁美洲西班牙语的真实长文本语料，将段落和文档级上下文输入LLM生成改写译文。在六个关于生成式AI的论文样本上，由两位专业译员依据定制MQM体系评估，结果显示简单提示无实质改写，而语料辅助方法有时能实现显著重构，但效果不稳定。研究认为LLM可被引导进行语用层面的调整，但需进一步优化才能稳定产出符合目标语境的译文。
Early Adoption of Agentic Coding Tools by GitHub Projects
Authors: Maliha Noushin Raida, Daqing Hou
Venue: KDD 2026
First: 2026-07-15T17:05:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:05:06+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the KDD 2026 Workshop on Agentic Software Engineering (SE 3.0)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic coding tools are increasingly capable of generating and submitting pull requests (PRs) to software projects, introducing new forms of human-agent collaboration in software development. While prior studies have examined PR-level outcomes of agent-generated contributions, less is known about how agentic coding tools are adopted and managed at the project level. In this paper, we analyze 25,264 agentic PRs from 2,361 popular GitHub repositories to investigate (1) the adoption of agentic coding tools, (2) project-level agentic PR productivity, and (3) human-agent collaboration patterns. Our results show that the median repository generates only one to two agentic PRs during a three-month period, indicating that intensive adoption remains concentrated in a small subset of projects. At the same time, small projects (1-5 contributors) exhibit higher participation ratios and average levels of agentic PR activity than medium-sized and large projects. We also observe substantial variation in project-level agentic PR productivity. While a small number of projects exceed an industry-reported estimate of 36 PRs per participant during the three-month observation period, most projects remain below this threshold. Finally, human-agent collaboration is dominated by a single-human oversight model, in which one developer reviews and/or modifies the agent's contributions, while multi-human collaboration patterns remain uncommon. These findings provide early empirical evidence on how open-source projects organize human oversight around agentic coding tools and suggest that successful integration of agent-generated contributions depends not only on advances in agent capabilities but also on the human and organizational processes that govern their use. Because this study captures an early snapshot of agent adoption, future work should continue to track how adoption patterns evolve over time.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GitHub项目对代理编码工具的早期采用
代理编码工具正日益具备生成并提交拉取请求（PR）的能力，为软件开发引入了新型人机协作形式。尽管已有研究考察了代理生成贡献的PR层面结果，但对代理编码工具在项目层面的采用与管理了解较少。本文分析了2,361个热门GitHub仓库中的25,264个代理PR，探讨（1）代理编码工具的采用情况，（2）项目层面代理PR生产力，以及（3）人机协作模式。结果显示，中位数仓库在三个月内仅生成一至两个代理PR，表明高强度采用仍集中于少数项目。同时，小型项目（1-5名贡献者）的参与率和平均代理PR活动水平高于中型和大型项目。我们还观察到项目层面代理PR生产力存在显著差异：少数项目在三个月观察期内超过行业报告的每位参与者36个PR的估计值，而大多数项目仍低于此阈值。最后，人机协作主要由单人监督模式主导，即一名开发者审查或修改代理的贡献，而多人协作模式仍属罕见。这些发现为开源项目如何围绕代理编码工具组织人工监督提供了早期实证依据，并表明成功整合代理生成贡献不仅依赖于代理能力的提升，还取决于管理其使用的人员与组织流程。由于本研究捕捉的是代理采用的早期快照，未来工作应持续追踪采用模式随时间的演变。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how agentic coding tools are adopted and managed at the project level in open-source software development, motivated by the growing ability of these tools to generate and submit pull requests. Analyzing 25,264 agentic PRs from 2,361 GitHub repositories, the authors find that most projects generate only one to two such PRs over three months, indicating limited widespread adoption. Small projects (1–5 contributors) show higher participation and activity levels compared to larger ones, while productivity varies widely—with only a few projects exceeding 36 PRs per participant. Human-agent collaboration is predominantly handled by a single-human oversight model, with multi-human collaboration being rare. These findings suggest that successful integration of agent-generated code depends not just on tool capabilities but also on human organizational practices.
鉴于自主生成并提交拉取请求的代理编码工具日益普及，本研究旨在探究这些工具在项目层面的采用、生产力及人机协作模式。通过对2361个GitHub仓库中的25264个代理PR进行分析，研究发现大多数项目在三个月内仅产生一到两个代理PR，小项目（1-5名贡献者）参与度和活动水平更高。项目层面的代理PR生产力差异显著，仅有少数项目超过每名参与者36个PR的行业基准，多数项目低于此水平。人类监督主要由单人开发者主导，多人协作模式罕见。研究结果表明，代理工具的成功整合不仅依赖技术能力，更取决于人类与组织层面的协作机制。
JoyAI-Sim: A Simulation-Enabled Interconversion Toolchain for the Embodied Data Pyramid
Authors: Peidong Liu, Yongce Liu, Songyan Guo, Fuyuan Ma, Zhihao Yuan, Ao Li, Zengjue Chen, Wenhao Li, Tianle Zhang, Mingyang Li, Jiale Zhang, Junzhe Xiong, Zhiyuan Xiang, Dafeng Chi, Yuzheng Zhuang, Liyi Luo, Wei Tan, Dongjiang Li, Nan Jiang, Yihang Li, Qingrong He, Jiaming Liang, Chen Cai, Mingxi Luo, Hui Zhang, Peng Hao, Song Wang, Ning Qiao, Yince Gao, Lei Kang, Junwu Xiong, Ruodai Li, Jiawei Li, Hui Shen, Yicheng Gong, Nan Duan, Liang Lin
First: 2026-06-15T14:21:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:00:56+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://joyai-sim.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Generalist robot policies require trustworthy evaluation and robot-usable training data, but both are difficult to scale with physical robots alone. Real-robot trials and demonstrations remain the most faithful source of deployment signals, yet they are slow, costly, and hard to reproduce. We present JoyAI-Sim, a simulation-enabled interconversion toolchain for human-robot aligned model evaluation and data generation, denoted as Robot $\rightleftharpoons$ Simulation $\rightleftharpoons$ Human. On the one hand, the Robot $\rightarrow$ Simulation $\rightarrow$ Human pathway supports human-robot aligned model evaluation by reconstructing real-robot tabletop organization tasks as calibrated digital twins for scalable evaluation, while using human embodied feedback to inspect and refine the naturalness of simulated motions. On the other hand, the Human $\rightarrow$ Simulation $\rightarrow$ Robot pathway supports human-robot aligned data generation: it lifts ego-centric human demonstrations into simulation, checks them under robot physical constraints, and converts them into robot-centered trajectories, annotations, and visual observations. Together, these pathways use the JoySim simulator as both a scalable evaluation layer and a physical consistency filter for robot data generation. We further package the core reconstruction, simulation, rendering, and realism-augmentation modules as cloud services on JD Cloud, turning the system into a reusable and scalable infrastructure for robot data generation and model evaluation.

标题：JoyAI-Sim：面向具身数据金字塔的人机对齐模型评估与数据生成仿真工具链
通用型机器人策略需要可信的评估和机器人可用的训练数据，但仅靠真实机器人难以规模化。真实机器人试验和演示仍是部署信号最忠实的来源，但其速度慢、成本高且难以复现。我们提出JoyAI-Sim，一种支持仿真赋能的人机对齐模型评估与数据生成工具链，记为机器人 ↔ 仿真 ↔ 人类。一方面，机器人 → 仿真 → 人类路径通过将真实机器人桌面组织任务重建为校准的数字孪生体，实现可扩展评估，并利用人类具身反馈检查和优化仿真动作的自然性；另一方面，人类 → 仿真 → 机器人路径支持人机对齐数据生成：将第一人称人类演示提升至仿真环境，验证其在机器人物理约束下的可行性，并转换为机器人中心轨迹、标注与视觉观测。二者共同以JoySim仿真器作为可扩展评估层和机器人数据生成的物理一致性过滤器。我们进一步将核心重建、仿真、渲染与真实感增强模块封装为京东云服务，使系统成为可复用、可扩展的机器人数据生成与模型评估基础设施。
Summary / 总结

The system supports model evaluation by reconstructing real-world robot tasks as calibrated digital twins in simulation, refined using human feedback to ensure motion naturalness, and enables data generation by lifting human ego-centric demonstrations into simulation, filtering them for robot physical constraints, and converting them into robot-centric trajectories and observations.
