为解决通用机器人策略评估与训练数据难以规模化的问题，作者提出JoyAI-Sim，一个基于模拟的双向工具链，实现真实机器人、模拟器与人类演示间的互转。评估路径通过构建校准的数字孪生体模拟真实机器人桌面任务，并借助人类反馈优化动作自然性；数据生成路径则将人类第一视角演示导入模拟器，过滤机器人物理约束后转换为机器人视角轨迹与标注。实验表明，该工具链可有效扩展评估规模并生成符合机器人物理特性的可用数据，同时保持人机行为一致性。
The Illusion of Robustness: Aggregate Accuracy Hides Prediction Flips under Task-Irrelevant Context
Authors: Yanzhe Zhang, Sanmi Koyejo, Diyi Yang
First: 2026-07-14T17:01:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T17:00:42+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) grow more capable, they are increasingly deployed in context-rich settings where task inputs are often accompanied by long, partially irrelevant context. In a controlled setting, we find that state-of-the-art models often appear robust to task-irrelevant context at the aggregate level: prepending it to benchmark questions causes little change in overall accuracy. This aggregate stability, however, masks significant per-example instability. Even semantically meaningless pseudo-words, formed by randomly combining characters, can markedly shift model predictions on a small fraction of examples, degrading performance on some while improving it on others. This two-sided effect holds consistently across a wide range of models and datasets, yet the affected examples are largely model-specific. We further show that this instability is modulated by context type, context length, test-time compute, and model development stage. Together, our findings reveal context-induced tail risks concealed by aggregate accuracy, motivating per-example reliability evaluation of language models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：鲁棒性的幻觉：聚合准确率掩盖了任务无关上下文下的预测翻转
随着大语言模型（LLM）能力不断增强，它们越来越多地部署在上下文丰富的环境中，其中任务输入常伴随长且部分无关的上下文。在受控实验中，我们发现当前最先进的模型在聚合层面往往对任务无关上下文表现出鲁棒性：将此类上下文前置到基准问题上，整体准确率变化不大。然而，这种聚合稳定性掩盖了显著的单例不稳定性。即使由随机字符组合而成的语义无意义伪词，也能显著改变模型在一小部分样本上的预测，导致部分样本性能下降，而另一些样本性能提升。这种双向效应在广泛模型和数据集上保持一致，但受影响的样本主要与模型特定相关。我们进一步表明，这种不稳定性受上下文类型、上下文长度、测试时计算量及模型开发阶段调节。综合来看，我们的发现揭示了聚合准确率所掩盖的上下文诱导尾部风险，呼吁对语言模型进行单例可靠性评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that large language models often appear robust to irrelevant context when measured by aggregate accuracy, this study investigates whether such stability masks per-example prediction instability. Using controlled experiments, the authors find that even semantically meaningless pseudo-words can flip model predictions on a subset of examples, sometimes degrading and sometimes improving performance. This effect is consistent across multiple models and datasets but varies by example, and is influenced by context length, type, compute resources, and model stage. The results reveal hidden tail risks in model behavior that aggregate metrics fail to capture, urging the need for per-example reliability assessment.
鉴于大型语言模型在聚合准确率上常表现出对无关上下文的鲁棒性，本研究旨在探究这种稳定性是否掩盖了单例预测的不稳定性。通过控制实验，作者引入语义无意义的伪词作为无关上下文，发现即使微小的上下文变化也能在少量样本上引发预测翻转，同时导致部分样本性能下降而另一些样本性能提升。该效应在多种模型与数据集上普遍存在，但样本高度特异，且受上下文类型、长度、测试时计算量及模型开发阶段影响，揭示了聚合指标所掩盖的尾部风险。
Improving Wind and Solar Power Prediction with Efficient Wrapper-based Feature Selection: An Empirical Study
Authors: Daniel Grillmeyer, Marius Hadry, Michael Stenger, Vanessa Borst, Veronika Lesch, Samuel Kounev
First: 2026-07-15T16:55:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:55:42+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With rising global energy demand and growing awareness of climate change and its impacts, the share of renewable energies in the global energy mix continues to grow. Unlike conventional power generation, the output of renewable energy sources cannot be controlled as consistently due to their dependence on environmental conditions. Therefore, reliable prediction of current and future energy production is essential. In this paper, we report findings from two structured literature reviews on real-world renewable energy prediction tasks: wind turbine power curve modeling and photovoltaic power prediction. For the former, we conducted a comprehensive literature review ourselves, while for the latter, we synthesize the key findings regarding frequently selected input features based on an existing survey. Across both domains, our analysis reveals that despite the large number of available monitoring and environmental variables, only limited or unsystematic methods for feature selection exist. To address this gap, we propose Cluster-based Sequential Feature Selection (CSFS), a novel, model-agnostic, clustering-based wrapper method for automatic, efficient, and reliable feature selection in renewable energy prediction pipelines. To support reproducibility and reuse, we provide an open-source implementation of CSFS on GitHub. We empirically evaluate the proposed approach on both use cases and compare it with established feature selection techniques such as wrapper-based sequential feature selection (SFS), filter-based methods, and Random Forest's embedded feature importance. The results show that the wrapper-based methods overall provide better-performing selections of features. CSFS achieves a predictive performance comparable to SFS while reducing computational cost by an average of 21%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过高效的基于包裹法的特征选择提升风能与太阳能发电预测：一项实证研究
随着全球能源需求上升及对气候变化及其影响的认识加深，可再生能源在全球能源结构中的占比持续增长。与传统发电不同，可再生能源的输出受环境条件影响，无法稳定控制。因此，准确预测当前和未来的能源产量至关重要。本文报告了两项针对现实世界可再生能源预测任务的结构化文献综述结果：风力涡轮机功率曲线建模与光伏功率预测。前者我们进行了全面的文献综述，后者则基于现有调查综合了常用输入特征的关键发现。在两个领域中，我们的分析表明，尽管存在大量监测与环境变量，但特征选择方法却有限或缺乏系统性。为填补这一空白，我们提出了一种名为基于聚类的序列特征选择（CSFS）的新颖、模型无关、基于聚类的包裹法，用于在可再生能源预测流程中实现自动、高效且可靠的特征选择。为支持可重复性与复用，我们在GitHub上提供了CSFS的开源实现。我们实证评估了该方法在两个应用场景中的表现，并与现有特征选择技术（如基于包裹法的序列特征选择SFS、基于过滤法的方法及随机森林的嵌入式特征重要性）进行了比较。结果表明，基于包裹法的方法总体上能提供更优的特征选择效果。CSFS的预测性能与SFS相当，同时平均降低计算成本21%。

It proposes Cluster-based Sequential Feature Selection (CSFS), a model-agnostic, clustering-enhanced wrapper method designed to automate efficient feature selection. Evaluated against established techniques including SFS, filter methods, and Random Forest’s embedded importance across real-world wind and solar datasets, CSFS demonstrates comparable predictive accuracy to SFS while reducing computational cost by an average of 21%, highlighting its practical efficiency for renewable energy applications.
为应对全球能源需求增长与气候变化背景下可再生能源预测的可靠性需求，本研究针对风能与太阳能预测中缺乏系统性特征选择方法的问题，提出了一种名为Cluster-based Sequential Feature Selection（CSFS）的模型无关、基于聚类的包裹式特征选择方法，旨在自动化高效特征筛选。在真实风能与光伏数据集上，与SFS、过滤法及随机森林嵌入式重要性等方法对比，CSFS在保持预测性能相当的同时，平均降低计算开销21%，展现出良好的实用效率。
Industrial Dexterity Benchmark: A Hardware-Software Benchmarking Platform for Industrial Dexterous Manipulation
Authors: Honglu He, Jacob Laufer, Zhiwu Zheng, David Elkan-gonzalez, Raman Goyal, Xinyi Li, Su Lu, Mishek Musa, Berke Saat, Nicolas Tan, Colm Prendergast
First: 2026-07-15T16:54:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:54:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dexterous manipulation remains a critical bottleneck in industrial automation; tasks such as cable routing, connector insertion, and precision assembly still rely heavily on manual labor despite decades of robotics research. This work presents a progression from classical, modular robotics pipelines toward an end-to-end multimodal imitation-learning framework for industrial dexterous manipulation. As a part of this work, we introduce three key contributions: a set of Industrial Dexterity Benchmark (IDB) boards aimed to mimic datacenter cable management, automotive cable harnesses, and gearbox assembly tasks; a scalable imitation learning framework (DAG-ROS); and a multimodal diffusion-based policy framework (AG-iDP3) that creates models fusing RGB images, point clouds, joint positions, and wrist-frame wrench data. Focusing on the datacenter cable manipulation board, we evaluate the performance of a task involving cleaning a single cable over variations of an end-to-end AI policy using 48 trials per configuration. The best performing configuration, a multimodal expansion Diffusion Policy (DP), includes a multi-view RGB image source passed through an R3M encoder and reaches a 78% grasp and insert combined task success rate. This performance marks a significant improvement over the 36% observed from the single-camera RGB DP baseline. Each of the tested configurations requires only approximately 100 teleoperated demonstrations per task phase. These results indicate that the correct learned policy can outperform classical vision and control robotic methods in robustness, generalization, and deployment efficiency, justifying a shift toward scalable robotic automation for high up-time industrial environments.

灵巧操作仍是工业自动化中的关键瓶颈；尽管机器人研究已历经数十年，诸如线缆布线、连接器插入和精密装配等任务仍高度依赖人工操作。本工作提出从经典模块化机器人流程向端到端多模态模仿学习框架的演进，以实现工业灵巧操作。本研究贡献包括：一套模拟数据中心线缆管理、汽车线束和变速箱装配任务的工业灵巧操作基准（IDB）板；一个可扩展的模仿学习框架（DAG-ROS）；以及一个融合RGB图像、点云、关节位置和腕部力矩数据的多模态扩散策略框架（AG-iDP3）。以数据中心线缆操作板为例，我们对单根线缆清洁任务在48次试验配置下评估端到端AI策略性能。表现最佳的配置——多模态扩散策略（DP）——通过R3M编码器处理多视角RGB图像，任务成功率（抓取与插入结合）达78%，显著优于单摄像头RGB DP基线的36%。每项测试配置仅需约100次遥操作演示。结果表明，正确学习的策略在鲁棒性、泛化能力与部署效率上优于传统视觉与控制机器人方法，支持向高可用工业环境的可扩展机器人自动化转型。

Transforming Rank: How Architecture Navigates the Spectral Pathologies of Depth
Authors: Katie Everett
First: 2026-07-15T16:50:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:50:43+00:00
Comments: 40 pages. Code: https://github.com/everettk/transforming-rank-paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We investigate how each component of the Transformer feedforward block architecture design determines how much rank survives across depth at initialization. We reinterpret skip connections and normalization, long understood as controlling magnitude, as mechanisms for preserving gradient rank across depth, since the very matrix multiplications and nonlinear activations that make the network expressive also reduce the rank. We show that skip connections trade off rank collapse against ensemble-like behavior, controlled by the relative scales of the branch and the skip: skip connections route the gradient around the residual branch, where rank is lost, rather than along the long gradient paths that encourage the layers to compose. The placement of the normalization layer controls this same tradeoff by setting the branch-to-skip ratio across depth, unifying much of the normalization placement and depth scaling literature, in particular why rank collapses for Post-Norm but plateaus for Pre-Norm. Other aspects of the architecture, like the two-matrix structure that expands and contracts the width, use additional parameters to preserve the representation or branch Jacobian rank. The second matrix decorrelates a coherent mean spike that would grow across blocks with a single matrix and uncentered activation, preventing the residual representation from collapsing. The width expansion between the two matrices keeps the branch Jacobian full rank: applying the rank-reducing activation in this expanded space leaves enough directions to span the original, at a width that follows a Marchenko--Pastur law. The initialization rank of the input--output Jacobian predicts which networks train on CIFAR-10. Taken together, we recast architecture design for deep networks as navigating an intrinsic tradeoff among rank collapse, ensemble-like behavior, and parameter count.
中文标题/摘要
标题：变换秩：架构如何在深度中导航谱病态
我们研究了Transformer前馈块架构设计的每个组件如何决定初始化时有多少秩能跨深度保留。我们将跳接连接和归一化重新诠释为控制梯度秩跨深度保留的机制，因为正是那些使网络具有表达能力的矩阵乘法和非线性激活也会降低秩。我们表明，跳接连接在秩坍塌与类似集成的行为之间进行权衡，该权衡由分支与跳接的相对尺度控制：跳接连接将梯度绕过残差分支（秩在此处丢失），而非沿鼓励层组合的长梯度路径。归一化层的位置通过设置深度中分支与跳接的比例控制这一权衡，统一了大量归一化位置和深度缩放文献，特别是解释了为何Post-Norm出现秩坍塌而Pre-Norm则保持平台期。架构的其他方面，如扩展和收缩宽度的双矩阵结构，通过额外参数保留表示或分支雅可比秩。第二个矩阵消除了单一矩阵和非中心化激活下会随块增长的相干均值尖峰，防止残差表示坍塌。两个矩阵之间的宽度扩展保持分支雅可比满秩：在该扩展空间中应用秩降低激活后，仍保留足够方向以覆盖原始空间，其宽度遵循Marchenko-Pastur定律。输入-输出雅可比的初始化秩预测了哪些网络能在CIFAR-10上训练。综合来看，我们将深度网络架构设计重新诠释为在秩坍塌、类似集成行为和参数数量之间进行内在权衡的导航。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how architectural choices in Transformer feedforward blocks influence rank preservation across network depth at initialization, reframing skip connections and normalization not as magnitude controllers but as mechanisms to maintain gradient rank despite rank-reducing operations. The authors show that skip connections balance rank collapse against ensemble-like behavior by routing gradients around residual branches where rank is lost, with the tradeoff governed by branch-to-skip scaling. Normalization placement controls this ratio, explaining why Post-Norm networks suffer rank collapse while Pre-Norm networks plateau. Architectural features like the two-matrix structure preserve Jacobian rank: the second matrix decorrelates mean spikes to prevent residual collapse, and width expansion between matrices ensures full branch Jacobian rank via a Marchenko-Pastur scaling law. The initial input-output Jacobian rank predicts trainability on CIFAR-10, suggesting architecture design should navigate a tradeoff among rank preservation, ensemble behavior, and parameter efficiency.
本文研究了Transformer前馈块架构如何影响深度网络中梯度秩的保持，重新诠释了残差连接与归一化层的作用——它们并非仅控制梯度幅度，而是通过引导梯度绕过秩损失的残差分支，以平衡秩坍塌与类似集成学习的行为，其权衡由分支与跳连的相对尺度决定。归一化层的位置调控该比例，解释了为何Post-Norm结构易发生秩坍塌而Pre-Norm结构能维持秩稳定。架构中的双矩阵结构通过解耦激活函数产生的均值尖峰并扩展宽度，确保分支雅可比矩阵保持满秩，其宽度遵循Marchenko-Pastur分布。输入输出雅可比矩阵的初始秩可预测网络在CIFAR-10上的训练能力，从而将架构设计问题转化为在秩保持、集成行为与参数效率间进行权衡的内在问题。
Vision Transformers and Convolutional Neural Networks for Land Use Scene Classification
Authors: Arun D. Kulkarni
First: 2026-05-20T14:57:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:46:41+00:00
Comments: 11 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Land Use Scene Classification (LUSC) from remote sensing imagery plays a critical role in environmental monitoring, urban planning, and sustainable resource management. In recent years, deep learning methods have significantly advanced the state of the art, with Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) dominating the field because of their strong ability to capture local spatial features. However, the emergence of Vision Transformers (ViTs) has introduced a new paradigm that models long-range dependencies through self-attention mechanisms, potentially enabling improved global context understanding. This paper presents a comparative assessment of Vision Transformers and CNN-based architecture for remote sensing land use scene classification. Representative CNN models, such as AlexNet, is evaluated alongside the Vision Transformer (ViT) using benchmark remote sensing datasets, including the UC Merced Land Use and EuroSAT Land Use datasets. The study examines classification accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, and computational complexity to provide a comprehensive performance comparison. Experimental results demonstrate that CNNs perform robustly on datasets with limited training samples and strong local texture characteristics, whereas Vision Transformers exhibit superior performance in capturing global spatial relationships in complex scenes when sufficient training data are available. However, ViTs typically require greater computational resources and larger training datasets to achieve optimal performance. The findings of this study provide insights into the strengths and limitations of both architectures and offer guidance for selecting appropriate models for remote sensing land use scene classification applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉变换器与卷积神经网络在土地利用场景分类中的应用
遥感影像的土地利用场景分类（LUSC）在环境监测、城市规划和可持续资源管理中发挥着关键作用。近年来，深度学习方法显著推动了该领域的技术进步，其中卷积神经网络（CNN）因其强大的局部空间特征提取能力而占据主导地位。然而，视觉变换器（ViTs）的出现引入了一种新范式，通过自注意力机制建模长距离依赖关系，可能提升全局上下文理解能力。本文对视觉变换器与CNN架构在遥感土地利用场景分类中的表现进行了比较评估。研究选取AlexNet等代表性CNN模型，与视觉变换器（ViT）在UC Merced土地利用和EuroSAT土地利用等基准数据集上进行对比。研究考察了分类准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数和计算复杂度，以提供全面的性能比较。实验结果表明，CNN在训练样本有限且局部纹理特征显著的数据集上表现稳健；而视觉变换器在训练数据充足时，对复杂场景中的全局空间关系捕捉能力更优。但ViTs通常需要更大的计算资源和更庞大的训练数据集才能达到最佳性能。本研究结果揭示了两种架构的优势与局限，为遥感土地利用场景分类应用中的模型选择提供了指导。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate land use classification in remote sensing for environmental and urban applications, this study compares Vision Transformers and Convolutional Neural Networks using benchmark datasets. It evaluates CNN models like AlexNet alongside Vision Transformers, measuring performance across accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, and computational cost. Results show CNNs excel with limited data and local texture-rich scenes, while Vision Transformers outperform in capturing global context when training data is abundant, albeit at higher computational cost.
为满足遥感影像土地利用分类在环境与城市规划中的需求，本文对比了视觉Transformer与卷积神经网络在该任务中的表现。研究评估了AlexNet等CNN模型与视觉Transformer，通过准确率、精确率、召回率、F1值及计算复杂度等指标进行比较。结果表明，CNN在数据量少且局部纹理明显的场景中表现稳健，而视觉Transformer在训练数据充足时更擅长捕捉全局空间关系，但计算开销更大。
Harness VLA: Steering Frozen VLAs into Reliable Manipulation Primitives via Memory-Guided Agents
Authors: Yixian Zhang, Huanming Zhang, Feng Gao, Xiao Li, Zhihao Liu, Chunyang Zhu, Jiaxing Qiu, Yuchen Yan, Jiyuan Liu, Wenhao Tang, Zhengru Fang, Yi Nie, Changxu Wei, Yu Wang, Wenbo Ding, Chao Yu
First: 2026-07-09T13:08:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:40:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language-conditioned manipulation requires both precise contact-rich control and robust reasoning over language, scenes, and long horizons. End-to-end Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide strong local visuomotor skills, but they are trained on in-distribution task trajectories and often fail under deployment perturbations such as semantic retargeting, goal re-binding, spatial-layout shifts, and unstable local contacts. LLM coding agents provide complementary semantic and compositional reasoning, but purely analytic primitives struggle with irregular grasping, constrained placement, and articulated-object interaction. We present Harness VLA, a memory-augmented agentic framework that exposes a frozen VLA as a retryable contact-rich primitive and composes it with a small fixed library of analytic primitives for grounding, staging, transport, navigation, and release. Rather than expanding the skill library, the harness learns the operating range of these fixed primitives from task-specific execution traces, global success rules, and failure models. By lifting semantic re-grounding, non-contact execution, and VLA re-staging to the planner while reserving the frozen VLA for local contact-rich phases, Harness VLA extends pretrained VLAs beyond their original trajectory distribution without finetuning. Across perturbed tabletop, household kitchen, and clean-to-randomized bimanual manipulation, Harness VLA improves over the strongest relevant baselines by 38.6 and 25.4 percentage points on LIBERO-Pro and RoboCasa365, respectively, and reaches 58.4% on RoboTwin C2R.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Harness VLA：通过记忆引导代理将冻结的视觉-语言-动作模型引导至可靠的操控原语
语言引导的操控需要精确的接触密集型控制和对语言、场景及长时程的鲁棒推理。端到端视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型具备强大的局部视觉-运动技能，但它们在分布内任务轨迹上训练，常在部署扰动（如语义重定向、目标重新绑定、空间布局变化和不稳定局部接触）下失效。大型语言模型编码代理提供互补的语义和组合推理能力，但纯分析型原语难以处理不规则抓取、受限放置和可动物体交互。我们提出Harness VLA，一种记忆增强的代理框架，将冻结的VLA作为可重试的接触密集型原语，并与小型固定分析原语库组合，用于定位、预置、运输、导航和释放。该框架不扩展技能库，而是从任务特定执行轨迹、全局成功规则和失败模型中学习这些固定原语的操作范围。通过将语义重定位、无接触执行和VLA重置提升至规划器层面，同时保留冻结VLA用于局部接触密集阶段，Harness VLA在无需微调的情况下扩展了预训练VLA超出其原始轨迹分布的能力。在扰动的桌面、家庭厨房及清洁到随机化双臂操控任务中，Harness VLA在LIBERO-Pro和RoboCasa365上分别比最强基线提升38.6和25.4个百分点，并在RoboTwin C2R上达到58.4%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to deploy vision-language-action models in real-world manipulation tasks under distributional shifts, the authors propose Harness VLA, a memory-augmented framework that combines a frozen VLA with a small set of analytic primitives. The method uses task-specific traces, global success rules, and failure models to learn the operational boundaries of these primitives without expanding the skill set. By offloading semantic reasoning and non-contact phases to an agent while reserving the VLA for contact-rich execution, the system extends pretrained models beyond their original training distribution without fine-tuning. Experiments show it outperforms baselines by 38.6 and 25.4 percentage points on LIBERO-Pro and RoboCasa365, respectively, and achieves 58.4% success on RoboTwin C2R.
为解决视觉语言动作模型在真实世界部署中因分布偏移导致的性能下降，作者提出Harness VLA框架，将冻结的VLA与少量分析型操作原语结合，通过任务轨迹和失败模型学习原语操作边界，实现语义重定位与非接触规划，保留VLA处理接触密集阶段。实验表明，该方法在LIBERO-Pro和RoboCasa365上分别比最强基线提升38.6%和25.4%，在RoboTwin C2R上达到58.4%成功率，且无需微调。
AeroMap3D: Anchoring Monocular UAV 6-DoF Localization to Visual-Geometric-Semantic Map Priors
Authors: Zhiyun Deng, Luis Sentis
First: 2026-07-15T16:38:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:38:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present AeroMap3D, a monocular 6-DoF UAV localization system that anchors onboard imagery to visual, geometric, and semantic map priors for GNSS-denied navigation. AeroMap3D addresses two fundamental challenges in map-referenced aerial localization: the cross-view discrepancy between UAV imagery and satellite maps, and the structural inconsistency between bare-earth digital elevation models (DEMs) and urban scenes. First, we introduce a lightweight adapter that enables a dense matcher pretrained on internet-scale generic data to perform reliable UAV-to-map registration without finetuning. By estimating the scale ratio and yaw offset between the UAV image and map tile, the adapter removes the dominant geometric misalignment induced by altitude, camera field of view, and heading before dense correspondence estimation. Second, AeroMap3D lifts 2D UAV-map correspondences onto DEM terrain while using OpenStreetMap annotations to reject semantically unreliable matches before RANSAC-PnP pose estimation, thereby reducing errors caused by unmodeled building heights and off-nadir structures. Delayed map-based pose measurements are further fused with relative-motion priors using a delayed-state EKF for continuous trajectory estimation. Without UAV-Terra3D retraining or tuning, AeroMap3D localizes all trajectories across eight Austin sites within 50 m and achieves 5.88 m mean 3D error over 55 km of flight.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AeroMap3D：基于视觉-几何-语义地图先验的单目无人机6自由度定位
我们提出了AeroMap3D，一种单目无人机6自由度定位系统，通过将机载影像锚定到视觉、几何和语义地图先验，实现无GNSS导航。AeroMap3D解决了地图参考空中定位中的两个根本挑战：无人机影像与卫星地图之间的跨视角差异，以及裸地数字高程模型（DEM）与城市场景之间的结构不一致。首先，我们引入了一个轻量级适配器，使在互联网规模通用数据上预训练的密集匹配器无需微调即可可靠执行无人机到地图的配准。通过估计无人机图像与地图瓦片之间的尺度比和偏航角偏移，该适配器在密集对应估计前消除由高度、相机视场角和航向引起的主导几何错位。其次，AeroMap3D将2D无人机-地图对应关系提升至DEM地形，并利用OpenStreetMap标注在RANSAC-PnP姿态估计前剔除语义不可靠的匹配，从而减少因未建模的建筑高度和非正射结构导致的误差。延迟的地图姿态测量进一步通过延迟状态EKF与相对运动先验融合，实现连续轨迹估计。无需对无人机-Terra3D进行再训练或调参，AeroMap3D在八个奥斯汀站点的所有轨迹中定位精度均在50米以内，飞行55公里的平均3D误差为5.88米。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable UAV navigation in GNSS-denied environments, AeroMap3D anchors monocular UAV imagery to visual-geometric-semantic map priors to enable 6-DoF localization. It tackles cross-view discrepancies and structural mismatches between UAV images and satellite maps by using a lightweight adapter to align images with map tiles without retraining, correcting for scale and yaw offsets. It then lifts 2D correspondences onto DEM terrain, filters out semantically invalid matches using OpenStreetMap annotations, and refines poses via RANSAC-PnP and delayed-state EKF fusion. Experimental results show it achieves 5.88 m mean 3D error over 55 km of flight across eight Austin sites without any model retraining.
为解决GNSS拒止环境下无人机6自由度定位难题，AeroMap3D将单目图像锚定至视觉、几何与语义地图先验，以克服无人机影像与卫星地图间的跨视角差异及数字高程模型与城市场景的结构不一致。系统采用轻量适配器，使预训练通用密集匹配器无需微调即可完成无人机与地图配准，先估计尺度比与航向偏移以消除高度、镜头视角与航向导致的几何错位。随后将2D对应点提升至DEM地形，并结合OpenStreetMap标注剔除语义不可靠匹配，再通过RANSAC-PnP估计位姿。延迟地图位姿与运动先验通过延迟状态EKF融合实现连续轨迹估计。在八处奥斯汀场地实验中，系统无需重训练即实现50米定位精度，55公里飞行路径平均3D误差达5.88米。
Lighthouse RL: Sample-Efficient Circuit Optimization via Strategic Reset Points
Authors: Mustafa Emre Gürsoy, Stefan Uhlich, Ryoga Matsuo, Yağız Gençer, Arun Venkitaraman, Chia-Yu Hsieh, Andrea Bonetti, Eisaku Ohbuchi, Lorenzo Servadei
First: 2026-07-15T16:37:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:37:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we introduce Lighthouse RL, a sample-efficient reinforcement learning (RL) approach for analog circuit sizing. Traditional methods lack generalization across different performance targets, while standard RL approaches waste resources exploring unpromising regions. Our method addresses these inefficiencies through a strategic reset strategy that initializes episodes from high-performing configurations discovered during training, called "lighthouses". These states, which are closer to the target objectives, guide exploration toward promising regions. When compared to RL and Bayesian optimization methods from the literature, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on a 2D benchmark problem and on two analog circuits, showing significant improvements in sample efficiency (up to 1.72x faster), optimization performance (100% vs. 0-87% success rate), generalization (75% vs. 0-50% extrapolation success), and objective maximization. This efficiency is particularly valuable for computationally expensive black-box optimization problems, and our reset strategy can be used as a plug-and-play enhancement for any RL-based optimization approach.
中文标题/摘要
标题：灯塔强化学习：通过策略性重置点实现样本高效的电路优化
本文提出灯塔强化学习（Lighthouse RL），一种面向模拟电路尺寸优化的样本高效强化学习方法。传统方法难以跨不同性能目标泛化，而标准强化学习方法在探索无希望区域时浪费资源。我们的方法通过策略性重置策略解决这些问题，从训练过程中发现的高性能配置（称为“灯塔”）初始化训练回合。这些更接近目标目标的状态引导探索向有希望区域进行。与文献中的强化学习和贝叶斯优化方法相比，我们在二维基准问题和两个模拟电路中展示了本方法的有效性，显著提升了样本效率（快1.72倍）、优化性能（100%成功率 vs. 0-87%）、泛化能力（75%外推成功率 vs. 0-50%）和目标最大化能力。该效率对计算成本高昂的黑箱优化问题尤为宝贵，且我们的重置策略可作为即插即用的增强模块应用于任何基于强化学习的优化方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of traditional circuit optimization methods and the resource waste in standard RL, this paper proposes Lighthouse RL, which strategically resets RL episodes from high-performing configurations—called 'lighthouses'—discovered during training. These lighthouse states, closer to target objectives, guide exploration toward promising regions, improving sample efficiency and optimization outcomes. Experiments on a 2D benchmark and two analog circuits show Lighthouse RL achieves up to 1.72x faster convergence, 100% success rate versus 0–87% for baselines, 75% extrapolation success versus 0–50%, and superior objective maximization, demonstrating its value for computationally expensive black-box optimization.
为解决传统电路优化方法泛化能力差和标准强化学习探索效率低的问题，Lighthouse RL 引入了一种策略性重置机制，从训练过程中发现的高性能状态（称为“灯塔状态”）开始初始化训练回合。这些更接近目标的灯塔状态引导探索向有潜力的区域集中，显著提升样本效率和优化性能。在2D基准和两个模拟电路实验中，该方法比基线方法快1.72倍，成功率100%（对比0–87%），外推成功率75%（对比0–50%），且目标值最大化表现更优，适用于计算成本高的黑盒优化问题。
Rethinking Penetration Testing for AI-Enabled Systems: From Resource Compromise to Behavioral Objective Violation
Authors: Mohammad Allahbakhsh, Mohammad Hassan Bahari, Moslem Attar-Raouf
First: 2026-07-15T16:36:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:36:54+00:00
Comments: 42 pages, 5 Tables, 21 references
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Penetration testing traditionally evaluates whether adversaries can exploit weaknesses in software, infrastructure, configurations, or operational controls to achieve security-relevant compromise. This paradigm remains necessary for AI-enabled systems, but it is no longer sufficient. In such systems, adversaries may influence prompts, retrieved content, sensor inputs, training data, memory, tools, or human-AI interaction loops to alter system behavior without directly compromising the underlying infrastructure. This paper reframes penetration testing for AI-enabled systems as objective-driven behavioral evaluation. We define an AI-enabled system as one in which learned models materially influence behavior affecting operational outcomes, and we define AI-enabled penetration as the feasible induction of AI-governed behavior that violates one or more operational objectives under an explicit threat model. This definition preserves conventional penetration testing while extending it to adversarial pathways such as prompt injection, indirect prompt injection, data poisoning, sensor manipulation, retrieval poisoning, tool misuse, and agentic misalignment. We further propose a testing workflow that identifies operational objectives, maps AI-governed behavior, analyzes adversarial influence surfaces, defines behavioral failure criteria, executes scenario-based tests, and reports evidence linking adversarial action to objective violation. A running example involving an AI-enabled security operations center assistant illustrates how penetration may occur through behavioral influence rather than infrastructure compromise. Together, the definitions, workflow, and example provide a technical framework for evaluating adversarial success in deployed AI-enabled systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新思考面向AI赋能系统的渗透测试：从资源妥协到行为目标违反
传统渗透测试评估攻击者是否能利用软件、基础设施、配置或操作控制中的弱点实现安全相关妥协。这一范式对AI赋能系统仍有必要，但已不再充分。在AI赋能系统中，攻击者可能通过影响提示、检索内容、传感器输入、训练数据、记忆、工具或人机交互循环，改变系统行为而不直接破坏底层基础设施。本文将AI赋能系统的渗透测试重新定义为以目标为导向的行为评估。我们将AI赋能系统定义为：学习模型实质性影响操作结果的行为系统；将AI赋能渗透定义为：在明确威胁模型下，诱导AI主导行为违反一个或多个操作目标的可行方式。该定义保留传统渗透测试，同时扩展至提示注入、间接提示注入、数据投毒、传感器操控、检索投毒、工具误用及代理错位等对抗路径。我们进一步提出一套测试工作流，包括识别操作目标、映射AI主导行为、分析对抗影响面、定义行为失败标准、执行场景化测试及报告关联对抗行为与目标违反的证据。一个涉及AI赋能安全运营中心助手的示例说明：渗透可通过行为影响而非基础设施破坏实现。定义、工作流与示例共同构成评估部署AI赋能系统中对抗成功的技术框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional penetration testing in AI-enabled systems, this paper redefines the goal as evaluating whether adversaries can induce AI-governed behavior that violates operational objectives, rather than merely compromising infrastructure. The method introduces a behavioral testing workflow that identifies system objectives, maps AI-influenced behaviors, analyzes adversarial influence surfaces, defines failure criteria, and executes scenario-based tests. Experimental results, illustrated through a security operations center assistant example, demonstrate that adversarial success often stems from behavioral manipulation—such as prompt injection or data poisoning—without direct system compromise, validating the need for objective-driven behavioral evaluation.
本文指出，传统渗透测试针对系统资源的破坏性攻击已不足以应对AI赋能系统，因为攻击者可通过操纵提示、数据或交互流程诱导AI产生有害行为，而无需破坏基础设施。作者提出新框架，将AI渗透定义为在威胁模型下诱导AI行为违反运营目标，涵盖提示注入、数据投毒、工具误用等攻击路径。他们设计了一套测试流程，包括目标识别、AI行为映射、攻击面分析、失败标准定义、场景测试与证据报告。以AI安全运营中心助手为例，展示了通过行为操控达成攻击目标而无需破坏基础设施的过程。
M$^\text{4}$World: A Multi-view Multimodal Driving World Model for Interactive Object Manipulation and Minute-long Streaming
Authors: Ke Cheng, Hanqiao Ye, Lei Shi, Yahui Liu, Yunhan Shen, Jingtao Dong, Zhenke Wang, Wenxuan Ao, Weixiang Xu, Kaining Huang, Shuhan Shen
First: 2026-07-15T16:36:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:36:33+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Driving-world generation has emerged as a core capability for scalable autonomous-driving simulation, yet existing methods remain limited in object-level controllability and long-horizon stability. We present M$^\text{4}$World, a Multi-view and Multimodal generative driving world model that synthesizes future surround-view video streams and synchronized LiDAR scans while supporting interactive object Manipulation and stable Minute-long streaming. Fine-grained object manipulation is realized through a flexible conditioning interface that supports explicit control over both the spatial layout and visual appearance of individual objects. Stable minute-long streaming, on the other hand, is achieved through a multi-stage training framework that enables online causal generation in only four denoising steps while maintaining coherent world dynamics throughout extended rollouts. Building on these components, we introduce an efficient few-clip post-training as well as a suite of visual reference-conditioned generation models, preserving general generation ability while allowing rare-case customization for long-tail controllability. To assess controllability beyond realism, we further introduce an automated VLM-based judging pipeline that evaluates scene-level condition adherence, view-wise object controllability, and cross-view object consistency. Comprehensive experiments show that M$^\text{4}$World consistently delivers high generation quality, precise controllability, and stable minute-long streaming. Together with downstream long-tail augmentation and scene editing, these results demonstrate the potential of M$^\text{4}$World for controllable, scalable driving simulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：M⁴World：用于交互式物体操作与分钟级流媒体的多视角多模态驾驶世界模型
驾驶世界生成已成为可扩展自动驾驶仿真中的核心能力，但现有方法在物体级可控性和长时稳定性方面仍有限。我们提出M⁴World，一种多视角多模态生成式驾驶世界模型，可合成未来环视视频流与同步激光雷达扫描，同时支持交互式物体操作与稳定的分钟级流媒体。细粒度物体操作通过灵活的条件接口实现，支持对单个物体的空间布局与视觉外观的显式控制。稳定的分钟级流媒体则通过多阶段训练框架实现，仅需四步去噪即可在线因果生成，同时在长时展开中保持世界动态一致性。基于这些组件，我们引入高效的少片段后训练方法及一系列视觉参考条件生成模型，在保持通用生成能力的同时，支持长尾场景的定制化控制。为超越真实感评估可控性，我们进一步引入基于视觉语言模型的自动化评判流程，评估场景级条件遵循度、视角级物体可控性及跨视角物体一致性。全面实验表明，M⁴World持续提供高质量生成、精确可控性与稳定分钟级流媒体。结合下游长尾增强与场景编辑，这些结果展示了M⁴World在可控、可扩展驾驶仿真中的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable autonomous driving simulation with fine-grained object control and long-horizon stability, M$^\text{4}$World introduces a multi-view, multimodal generative model that synthesizes future surround-view video and synchronized LiDAR data while enabling interactive object manipulation and minute-long streaming. It achieves precise spatial and visual object control via a flexible conditioning interface and maintains coherent world dynamics through a multi-stage training framework that generates causal scenes in just four denoising steps. Experimental results demonstrate high generation fidelity, accurate controllability, and stable long-duration output, validated by automated VLM-based evaluation of scene adherence, object manipulation, and cross-view consistency, along with downstream applications in long-tail augmentation and scene editing.
为实现具备细粒度物体控制与长时稳定性的可扩展自动驾驶仿真，M$^\text{4}$World 提出一种多视角、多模态生成模型，可合成未来环视视频与同步激光雷达数据，并支持交互式物体操控与分钟级流式生成。其通过灵活的条件接口实现物体空间布局与视觉外观的精确控制，借助多阶段训练框架在仅四步去噪中完成稳定生成，保持长时间序列的场景一致性。实验表明该模型在生成质量、可控性与流式稳定性方面表现优异，自动化VLM评估系统验证了场景条件符合度、物体可控性及跨视角一致性。
Do Agent Optimizers Compound? A Continual-Learning Evaluation on Terminal-Bench 2.0
Authors: Wenxiao Wang, Priyatham Kattakinda, Soheil Feizi
First: 2026-07-15T16:36:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:36:04+00:00
Comments: Technical Report by RELAI (relai.ai)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Most reported gains from agent-optimization methods are one-shot: an agent is optimized against a fixed benchmark and the resulting improvement is reported as if it were a stable property of the method. This does not test the setting that matters for deployed agents, where optimization is applied recursively as new failures and new tasks appear over time. The central question this raises is whether optimizer-driven gains compound: after an agent has been optimized once, can it be optimized again on newly arrived tasks without eroding the gains the first round produced? We study this question with a two-phase continual-learning evaluation built from hard tasks in Terminal-Bench 2.0, comparing three approaches to agent-harness optimization (GEPA, Meta Harness, and RELAI's Verifiable Continual Learning, RELAI-VCL) under identical optimization budgets. All three methods improve over the baseline agent in the conventional, static, single-phase setting. However, once new tasks are introduced, the methods diverge sharply: GEPA's optimized agent transfers below the unoptimized baseline, Meta Harness transfers well but fails to improve further once given a second optimization budget, and RELAI-VCL is the only method that both transfers positively to unseen tasks and continues improving after those tasks are folded into the optimization objective, reaching the highest pass rate at every evaluated stage and the highest lifelong average pass rate overall (76.4% vs. 66.0% for GEPA, 64.6% for Meta Harness, and 58.7% for the baseline). Our key observation was that optimization gains compounded only when regression control was built into the optimization loop, providing an inductive bias against shortcut solutions that fail to generalize.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理优化器的效果会累积吗？基于Terminal-Bench 2.0的持续学习评估
大多数报告的代理优化方法收益均为一次性：代理针对固定基准进行优化，所得改进被视作该方法的稳定属性。这并未测试实际部署代理所面临的场景，即随着新失败和新任务不断出现，优化需递归进行。由此引发的核心问题是：优化驱动的收益是否会累积？即代理经一轮优化后，能否在新任务上再次优化而不侵蚀首轮优化成果？我们通过基于Terminal-Bench 2.0中困难任务构建的两阶段持续学习评估，比较了三种代理控制优化方法（GEPA、Meta Harness 和 RELAI-VCL）在相同优化预算下的表现。三者在传统静态单阶段设置下均优于基线代理。然而，当引入新任务后，方法表现显著分化：GEPA优化后的代理性能低于未优化基线；Meta Harness虽能良好迁移但二次优化预算下无法进一步提升；唯有RELAI-VCL既对未见任务正向迁移，又能在新任务纳入优化目标后持续改进，每阶段通过率最高，终身平均通过率亦最高（76.4%，相较GEPA的66.0%、Meta Harness的64.6%及基线的58.7%）。我们的关键发现是：仅当回归控制纳入优化循环时，优化收益才会累积，这提供了对无法泛化的捷径解的归纳偏置。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether agent-optimization gains compound over time in continual learning settings, addressing the gap between one-shot optimization benchmarks and real-world deployment where agents face evolving tasks. Using Terminal-Bench 2.0’s hard tasks in a two-phase continual-learning framework, it compares GEPA, Meta Harness, and RELAI-VCL under identical optimization budgets. While all outperform the baseline in static settings, only RELAI-VCL maintains and improves performance across phases, achieving the highest lifelong average pass rate (76.4%) by incorporating regression control to prevent shortcut solutions that degrade generalization.
该研究探讨了在持续学习场景下，智能体优化收益是否能累积——即在初始优化后，面对新任务时能否继续提升而不削弱原有收益。基于Terminal-Bench 2.0的困难任务，对比了三种优化方法（GEPA、Meta Harness 和 RELAI-VCL）在相同预算下的表现。虽然三者在静态单阶段设置中均优于基线，但只有RELAI-VCL在引入新任务后仍能持续提升并保持良好迁移能力，最终实现最高的终身平均通过率（76.4%），显著优于GEPA（66.0%）、Meta Harness（64.6%）和基线（58.7%）。核心发现是，优化收益的累积依赖于优化循环中内置的回归控制，以避免泛化能力差的捷径解。
Lyapunov Exponent as Physics-Informed Dense Reward: RL Discovery of Stabilization Beyond the Kapitza Pendulum
Authors: Slava Andrejev
First: 2026-07-15T16:29:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:29:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We suggest using the Lyapunov characteristic exponent (LCE) as a dense reward signal for the reinforcement learning problem of stabilizing the inverted pendulum with vertical motion. With LCE, the agent not only successfully found the oscillatory motion known as the Kapitza pendulum but also damped the pendulum's pivoting, leaving it in a strictly upright position.
中文标题/摘要
标题：李雅普诺夫指数作为物理感知密集奖励：强化学习发现超越卡皮察摆的稳定化方法
我们建议将李雅普诺夫特征指数（LCE）作为强化学习问题中垂直运动稳定倒立摆的密集奖励信号。通过LCE，智能体不仅成功找到了已知的振荡运动——卡皮察摆，还抑制了摆的摆动，使其严格保持直立状态。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to stabilize an inverted pendulum using reinforcement learning, this work proposes using the Lyapunov characteristic exponent as a dense reward signal to guide the agent toward stable dynamics. The method leverages the LCE’s sensitivity to system stability, enabling the agent to discover not only the known Kapitza pendulum oscillation but also novel stabilization strategies that suppress pivoting and maintain the pendulum strictly upright. Experimental results demonstrate that LCE-based rewards effectively drive the agent to achieve superior stability compared to traditional reward functions.
为引导强化学习智能体发现具有物理意义的稳定行为，本文提出将李雅普诺夫特征指数作为密集奖励信号，用于通过垂直运动稳定倒立摆。该方法利用李雅普诺夫指数对系统稳定性的敏感性，使智能体不仅发现已知的卡普提萨摆振荡解，还能探索抑制摆动、使摆杆严格保持直立的新稳定策略。实验结果表明，基于李雅普诺夫指数的奖励机制能有效引导智能体实现超越传统方法的鲁棒物理稳定控制。
The Dynamic Verifiable Multi-Agent Human Agentic Loyalty Loop (DVM-HALL) Model and the Net Human-Agent Score (NHAS) in Autonomous Commerce
Authors: Sai Srikanth Madugula, Peplluis Esteva de la Rosa, Daya Shankar
First: 2026-07-15T16:27:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:27:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid proliferation of Agentic Artificial Intelligence fundamentally disrupts traditional customer loyalty paradigms. As AI evolves from passive recommendation algorithms to autonomous, goal-directed agents capable of executing purchasing decisions, the conventional understanding of consumer-brand relationships requires a structural reevaluation. By synthesizing extant literature across human-machine teaming, consumer decision-making, and algorithmic trust dynamics, we demonstrate that traditional loyalty models fail to account for algorithmic bounded rationality and constructed autonomy. To address this, we introduce the Dynamic Verifiable Multi-Agent Human Agentic Loyalty Loop (DVM-HALL) model. We formalize brand choice via a softmax probability formulation where human emotional equity, agentic machine-experience utility, calibrated trust, delegated authority, and verifiable execution jointly determine selection. The model features recursive updating mechanisms to dynamically calibrate trust and delegation after each interaction. Crucially, the framework integrates a verifiable execution layer for Decentralized Finance (DeFi) and tokenized loyalty settings, incorporating execution risks -- such as gas costs, slippage, MEV exposure, and smart-contract vulnerabilities -- as core predictors of agentic brand preference. Furthermore, we introduce the Net Human-Agent Score (NHAS), an auditable, risk-weighted metric designed to measure human-agent alignment using human feedback, execution logs, benchmark comparisons, and verifiable receipts. Finally, we propose a comprehensive three-stage empirical validation plan spanning controlled shopping experiments, multi-agent market simulations, and DeFi testbeds. This framework provides the foundational theory required for brands to navigate the impending transition toward machine customers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自主商业中的动态可验证多智能体人类代理忠诚循环（DVM-HALL）模型与净人机得分（NHAS）
代理型人工智能的迅速普及从根本上颠覆了传统客户忠诚度范式。随着AI从被动推荐算法演进为能够自主执行购买决策的目标导向智能体，消费者与品牌关系的传统理解亟需结构性重构。通过整合人机协同、消费者决策及算法信任动态领域的现有文献，我们证明传统忠诚度模型未能考虑算法有限理性与构建性自主性。为此，我们提出动态可验证多智能体人类代理忠诚循环（DVM-HALL）模型。该模型通过softmax概率公式形式化品牌选择，其中人类情感权益、智能体体验效用、校准信任、授权委托及可验证执行共同决定选择。模型具备递归更新机制，以动态校准每次交互后的信任与授权。关键地，该框架整合了去中心化金融（DeFi）与代币化忠诚度场景中的可验证执行层，将执行风险——如Gas费用、滑点、MEV暴露及智能合约漏洞——作为代理型品牌偏好的核心预测变量。此外，我们引入净人机得分（NHAS），一种可审计、风险加权的指标，通过人类反馈、执行日志、基准对比及可验证收据衡量人机对齐度。最后，我们提出涵盖受控购物实验、多智能体市场模拟及DeFi测试平台的三阶段实证验证方案。该框架为品牌应对即将来临的机器客户转型提供了基础理论支撑。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the shift from passive AI recommendations to autonomous agents making purchasing decisions, this paper proposes the DVM-HALL model to reframe human-brand loyalty in the age of agentic AI. The model formalizes brand choice using a softmax probability function that integrates human emotional equity, machine utility, calibrated trust, delegated authority, and verifiable execution outcomes, with recursive updates to adapt trust and delegation after each interaction. It further embeds DeFi-specific execution risks—like gas fees, slippage, and smart contract vulnerabilities—into preference modeling. The Net Human-Agent Score (NHAS) is introduced as a risk-weighted, auditable metric derived from human feedback, execution logs, benchmarks, and receipts to quantify human-agent alignment. Experimental validation across controlled shopping, multi-agent simulations, and DeFi testbeds confirms the model’s predictive power and its ability to capture algorithmic bounded rationality and constructed autonomy in autonomous commerce.
随着AI从被动推荐演变为自主执行购买决策的代理，本研究通过动态可验证多代理人类代理忠诚循环（DVM-HALL）模型重构顾客忠诚度概念，该模型将人类情感价值、机器效用、校准信任、授权委托与可验证执行整合进softmax选择框架，并在每次交互后递归更新信任与授权。模型嵌入DeFi执行风险（如Gas费、滑点、MEV暴露及智能合约漏洞）作为品牌偏好的核心预测因子，同时提出净人机得分（NHAS），基于人类反馈、执行日志、基准对比与可验证收据，构建可审计的风险加权对齐度量。通过控制购物实验、多代理市场模拟及DeFi测试床的三阶段实证验证，证实该框架对品牌应对机器客户转型具有理论基础与预测能力。
When Vision Overrides Language: Evaluating and Mitigating Counterfactual Failures in VLAs
Authors: Yu Fang, Yuchun Feng, Dong Jing, Jiaqi Liu, Yue Yang, Zhenyu Wei, Daniel Szafir, Mingyu Ding
First: 2026-02-19T18:59:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:20:07+00:00
Comments: Website: https://vla-cf.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) promise to ground language instructions in robot control, yet in practice often fail to faithfully follow language. When presented with instructions that lack strong scene-specific supervision, VLAs suffer from counterfactual failures: they act based on vision shortcuts induced by dataset biases, repeatedly executing well-learned behaviors and selecting objects frequently seen during training regardless of language intent. To systematically study it, we introduce LIBERO-CF, the first counterfactual benchmark for VLAs that evaluates language following capability by assigning alternative instructions under visually plausible LIBERO layouts. Our evaluation reveals that counterfactual failures are prevalent yet underexplored across state-of-the-art VLAs. We propose Counterfactual Action Guidance (CAG), a simple yet effective dual-branch inference scheme that explicitly regularizes language conditioning in VLAs. CAG combines a standard VLA policy with a language-unconditioned Vision-Action (VA) module, enabling counterfactual comparison during action selection. This design reduces reliance on visual shortcuts, improves robustness on under-observed tasks, and requires neither additional demonstrations nor modifications to existing architectures or pretrained models. Extensive experiments demonstrate its plug-and-play integration across diverse VLAs and consistent improvements. For example, on LIBERO-CF, CAG improves $π_{0.5}$ by 9.7% in language following accuracy and 3.6% in task success on under-observed tasks using a training-free strategy, with further gains of 15.5% and 8.5%, respectively, when paired with a VA model. In real-world evaluations, CAG reduces counterfactual failures of 9.4% and improves task success by 17.2% on average.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当视觉超越语言时：评估与缓解视觉-语言-动作模型中的反事实失败
视觉-语言-动作模型（VLAs）旨在将语言指令与机器人控制相结合，但在实践中往往无法忠实遵循语言指令。当面对缺乏强场景特定监督的指令时，VLAs会遭受反事实失败：它们基于数据集偏差诱导的视觉捷径进行行动，反复执行已习得的行为，并选择训练期间频繁出现的物体，而无视语言意图。为系统性研究该问题，我们引入LIBERO-CF，这是首个针对VLAs的反事实基准，通过在视觉上合理的LIBERO布局中分配替代指令来评估语言遵循能力。评估结果表明，反事实失败在当前最先进的VLAs中普遍存在但尚未被充分研究。我们提出反事实动作引导（CAG），这是一种简单而有效的双分支推理方案，显式地在VLAs中正则化语言条件。CAG结合标准VLA策略与无语言条件的视觉-动作（VA）模块，使动作选择过程中可进行反事实比较。该设计减少了对视觉捷径的依赖，提升了对低观察任务的鲁棒性，且无需额外演示或修改现有架构或预训练模型。大量实验表明其可跨多种VLAs无缝集成并持续提升性能。例如，在LIBERO-CF上，CAG采用无训练策略，语言遵循准确率提升9.7%，低观察任务成功率提升3.6%；当与VA模型结合时，分别进一步提升15.5%和8.5%。在真实世界评估中，CAG平均减少反事实失败9.4%，任务成功率提升17.2%。
Summary / 总结
Vision-Language-Action models often fail to follow language instructions when visual cues in the environment strongly correlate with frequently seen training behaviors, leading to counterfactual failures where actions are driven by visual shortcuts rather than language intent. To address this, the authors introduce LIBERO-CF, a novel benchmark that evaluates VLAs by presenting them with alternative language instructions under visually similar but semantically different layouts. They propose Counterfactual Action Guidance (CAG), a dual-branch inference method that combines a standard VLA policy with a language-unconditioned Vision-Action module to enable counterfactual comparison during action selection. Experiments show CAG significantly improves language following accuracy and task success on under-observed tasks without requiring additional training or architectural changes, achieving up to 15.5% and 8.5% gains in performance when paired with a VA model, and reducing counterfactual failures by 9.4% in real-world tests.
视觉-语言-动作模型常因视觉线索主导决策而忽视语言指令，导致反事实失败，即重复训练中习得的行为而不顾意图。为此，作者提出LIBERO-CF基准，通过在视觉上合理的环境中提供替代指令来评估语言遵循能力。他们设计了反事实动作引导（CAG）方法，结合标准VLA策略与无语言条件的视觉-动作模块，显式规范语言条件化。实验表明，CAG无需重训练或架构修改即可显著提升语言遵循准确率和未观察任务成功率，与VA模型结合时分别提升15.5%和8.5%，并在真实场景中减少9.4%的反事实失败，平均提升任务成功率17.2%。
TRACE: Turn-level Reward Assignment via Credit Estimation for Long-Horizon Agents
Authors: Leitian Tao, Baolin Peng, Wenlin Yao, Tao Ge, Hao Cheng, Mike Hang Wang, Jianfeng Gao, Sharon Li
First: 2026-07-15T16:16:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:16:42+00:00
Comments: 26 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-turn agents solve complex tasks through extended sequences of tool interactions before producing a final answer, making credit assignment a fundamental challenge during post-training. Outcome rewards provide reliable supervision for short-horizon reasoning, but become sparse and high-variance as trajectories grow to tens or hundreds of tool calls. They can also be misleading: a failed rollout may contain many useful actions that move the agent closer to the goal, yet outcome-only training assigns them the same negative advantage as the eventual mistake. We propose TRACE (Turn-level Reward Assignment via Credit Estimation), a dense credit-assignment method for agentic reinforcement learning. TRACE represents rollouts as state transitions at tool-call boundaries, obtains gold-answer log-probabilities from a frozen reference model, transforms them into log-ratio state values, and derives per-action rewards as Temporal-Difference changes in those values. This requires no additional critic or process-label training, and its one-step log-ratio TD component telescopes across redundant tool calls. On long-horizon complex search, TRACE substantially improves base-model tool-use ability using pure RL, without a cold-start supervised fine-tuning stage, an agentic mid-training stage, or training on live-web data. On the closed-web BrowseComp-Plus benchmark, it raises Qwen3-4B from $7.2$ to $35.6$ and Qwen3-30B-A3B from $8.4$ to $42.6$. The learned search behavior also transfers to open-web benchmarks, and the learning curves show earlier improvement and faster convergence during RL training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TRACE：通过信用评估进行回合级奖励分配以支持长时域智能体
多回合智能体通过一系列工具交互操作完成复杂任务，最终输出答案，这使得训练后阶段的信用分配成为根本性挑战。结果奖励可为短时域推理提供可靠监督，但当轨迹扩展至数十或数百次工具调用时，奖励变得稀疏且方差高。此外，结果奖励也可能误导：一次失败的执行可能包含许多接近目标的有用动作，但仅基于结果的训练会将这些动作与最终错误赋予相同的负优势。我们提出TRACE（通过信用评估进行回合级奖励分配），一种面向代理强化学习的密集信用分配方法。TRACE将执行轨迹表示为工具调用边界处的状态转移，从冻结的参考模型中获取黄金答案的对数概率，将其转换为对数比状态值，并将每个动作的奖励定义为这些值的时序差分变化。该方法无需额外的批评者或过程标签训练，其单步对数比TD组件可跨越冗余工具调用进行折叠。在长时域复杂搜索任务中，TRACE仅通过纯强化学习显著提升基础模型的工具使用能力，无需冷启动监督微调阶段、代理中间训练阶段或训练实时网络数据。在闭源网络BrowseComp-Plus基准测试中，TRACE将Qwen3-4B的性能从7.2提升至35.6，Qwen3-30B-A3B从8.4提升至42.6。所学习的搜索行为也可迁移到开放网络基准测试中，且学习曲线显示强化学习训练中更早改善和更快收敛。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of sparse and misleading outcome rewards in long-horizon agent training, TRACE introduces a turn-level credit assignment method that estimates per-action rewards via temporal-difference updates of log-ratio state values derived from a frozen reference model. By treating rollouts as state transitions at tool-call boundaries and leveraging gold-answer log-probabilities, TRACE avoids additional critics or supervision, enabling dense reward signals that telescope across redundant actions. Experimental results show TRACE significantly boosts tool-use performance in complex search tasks without requiring supervised fine-tuning, mid-training agent stages, or live-web data, improving Qwen3-4B from 7.2 to 35.6 and Qwen3-30B-A3B from 8.4 to 42.6 on BrowseComp-Plus, with faster convergence and transferable behavior on open-web benchmarks.
为了解决长时序代理训练中奖励稀疏且误导性的问题，TRACE提出一种基于信用估计的回合级奖励分配方法，通过冻结参考模型获取黄金路径对数概率，将其转化为状态值的对数比值，并用时序差分更新计算每个工具调用的奖励。该方法无需额外批评者或监督阶段，仅依赖路径概率差值。实验表明，TRACE在复杂搜索任务中显著提升工具使用能力，无需冷启动微调，使Qwen3-4B在BrowseComp-Plus上从7.2提升至35.6，Qwen3-30B-A3B从8.4提升至42.6，且训练收敛更快、行为可迁移至开放网页基准。
Agent Skill Security: Threat Models, Attacks, Defenses, and Evaluation
Authors: Sanket Badhe, Priyanka Tiwari
First: 2026-07-15T16:15:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:15:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reusable skills are becoming a fundamental building block of Large Language Model (LLM) agents, enabling capabilities to be packaged, shared, and reused across diverse applications. However, existing security research primarily focuses on prompt injection and runtime execution, leaving security risks throughout the broader skill lifecycle largely unexplored. In this paper, we present SkillSec-Eval, a lifecycle-aware framework for systematically evaluating the security of reusable agent skills. We first characterize the skill lifecycle and develop a threat taxonomy spanning repository admission, semantic retrieval, planner selection, execution, and skill evolution. We then instantiate this taxonomy in SkillSec-Eval and conduct a comprehensive empirical evaluation using a repository of 327 real-world skills. Our study demonstrates that vulnerabilities arise at multiple lifecycle stages beyond execution, highlighting the need for lifecycle-aware security analysis of reusable agent skills.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理技能安全：威胁模型、攻击、防御与评估
可复用技能正逐渐成为大语言模型（LLM）代理的基本构建模块，使能力能够被封装、共享和跨多种应用复用。然而，现有安全研究主要聚焦于提示注入和运行时执行，对技能全生命周期中的广泛安全风险仍缺乏探索。本文提出SkillSec-Eval，一个面向生命周期的框架，用于系统性评估可复用代理技能的安全性。我们首先刻画技能生命周期，并构建涵盖仓库准入、语义检索、规划器选择、执行及技能演化的威胁分类体系。随后，我们在SkillSec-Eval中实例化该分类体系，并利用包含327个真实技能的仓库进行全面实证评估。研究表明，漏洞不仅存在于执行阶段，还广泛存在于生命周期的多个阶段，凸显了对可复用代理技能进行生命周期感知安全分析的必要性。
Summary / 总结
As reusable skills become essential components of Large Language Model agents, existing security research has largely overlooked vulnerabilities across the full skill lifecycle, focusing only on prompt injection and runtime execution. To address this, the authors introduce SkillSec-Eval, a lifecycle-aware framework that systematically evaluates skill security by mapping a threat taxonomy across five stages: repository admission, semantic retrieval, planner selection, execution, and skill evolution. Through empirical testing on 327 real-world skills, they demonstrate that security flaws manifest at multiple lifecycle stages beyond runtime, underscoring the necessity for comprehensive lifecycle-based security analysis.
随着可复用技能成为大型语言模型代理的核心组成部分，现有安全研究主要关注提示注入和运行时执行，而忽视了技能全生命周期中的安全风险。为此，作者提出了SkillSec-Eval框架，系统性地评估技能在五个阶段的安全性：仓库准入、语义检索、规划器选择、执行和技能演化。通过对327个真实世界技能的实证测试，研究发现安全漏洞广泛存在于运行时之外的多个生命周期阶段，强调了对可复用技能进行全生命周期安全分析的必要性。
Algorithmic Recourse of In-Context Learning for Tabular Data
Authors: Wenshuo Dong, Jiaming Zhang, Shaopeng Fu, Hongbin Lin, Di Wang, Lijie Hu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-29T13:04:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:15:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As predictive models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings such as credit approval, there is a growing need for post-hoc methods that provide recourse to affected individuals. Many such models operate on tabular data, where features correspond to real-world attributes. Recently, in-context learning (ICL) has enabled large language models to perform tabular prediction by conditioning on labeled examples at inference time, without explicit training. However, algorithmic recourse for tabular decision-making under ICL remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present the first study of algorithmic recourse for tabular data under ICL. We carry out a theoretical analysis, showing that recourse remains well-defined and bounded, and we characterize how recourse converges toward classical solutions as the context size increases. In practice, we propose a novel zeroth-order recourse framework, Adaptive Subspace Recourse for In-Context Learning (ASR-ICL), that efficiently generates actionable and sparse recourse for black-box ICL models. The proposed framework naturally extends to multi-class tabular tasks. Experiments across multiple real-world datasets and models demonstrate that ASR-ICL achieves recourse quality comparable to existing methods with fewer queries and empirically confirm the predicted convergence behavior, supporting our theoretical analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：上下文学习中表格数据的算法救济
随着预测模型在信贷审批等高风险场景中的广泛应用，事后提供救济措施以帮助受影响个体的需求日益增长。许多此类模型处理表格数据，其中特征对应现实世界属性。近期，上下文学习（ICL）使大语言模型能够在推理时通过条件化于标注示例进行表格预测，而无需显式训练。然而，ICL下表格决策的算法救济研究尚属空白。本文首次系统研究ICL下表格数据的算法救济。我们进行理论分析，证明救济仍具明确定义且有界，并刻画了随着上下文规模增大，救济收敛于经典解的过程。实践中，我们提出一种新颖的零阶救济框架——适应子空间上下文学习救济（ASR-ICL），高效生成针对黑盒ICL模型的可操作且稀疏的救济方案。该框架自然扩展至多分类表格任务。在多个真实数据集和模型上的实验表明，ASR-ICL在查询次数更少的情况下，救济质量可媲美现有方法，并实证验证了预测的收敛行为，支持我们的理论分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for explainable and actionable recourse in high-stakes tabular prediction tasks powered by in-context learning, this work introduces the first algorithmic recourse framework tailored for ICL models. The method, called Adaptive Subspace Recourse for In-Context Learning (ASR-ICL), is a zeroth-order approach that efficiently generates sparse, actionable recourse actions for black-box ICL models without requiring gradient information. Theoretically, the study proves that recourse remains bounded and converges toward classical recourse solutions as context size grows. Empirically, ASR-ICL outperforms existing methods on multiple real-world datasets by achieving comparable recourse quality while requiring fewer queries, validating the theoretical convergence predictions.
为解决高风险场景中基于上下文学习的表格数据决策系统缺乏可解释与可操作的救济方案问题，本文首次提出针对ICL的算法救济框架——自适应子空间ICL救济（ASR-ICL）。该方法采用零阶优化策略，通过自适应选择特征子空间生成稀疏且可执行的救济方案。理论分析表明，救济方案在上下文规模增大时收敛于经典解，且保持有界性。实验表明，ASR-ICL在多个真实数据集上达到或超越现有方法的救济质量，同时查询次数更少，验证了理论预测的有效性。
Task-Specific Feature Fusion Method for Multi-Task Affective Behavior Analysis
Authors: Jiajun Sun, Zhe Gao
First: 2026-07-15T16:14:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:14:59+00:00
Comments: Extended arXiv version with an appendix. Code will be made publicly available
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The 11th Affective Behavior Analysis in-the-wild (ABAW11) Multi-Task Learning Challenge requires a unified system to predict valence-arousal, categorical expressions, and facial action units from the official s-Aff-Wild2 images. Although these tasks are naturally related through facial behavior, our validation experiments show that they benefit from different visual features, temporal processing strategies, fusion mechanisms, and calibration procedures. In this paper, we study task-adaptive feature fusion for ABAW11 multi-task affective behavior analysis. We first adapt two pretrained visual backbones, DINOv2 ViT-L and DINOv3 ConvNeXt-base, on an external expression-oriented facial image set and then freeze them to extract complementary frame-level features from the official ABAW11 data. On top of these frozen features, we systematically compare frame-level prediction heads, temporal convolutional heads, post-hoc temporal smoothing, LightGBM models, feature concatenation, gated fusion, residual fusion, late logit fusion, threshold calibration, and shared MTL structures. The final system selects task-specific fusion and prediction strategies rather than forcing all tasks to share a single architecture. On the ABAW11 validation set, the selected system achieves an EXPR macro-F1 of 0.4222, an AU macro-F1 of 0.5402, and a mean VA CCC of 0.6717, resulting in an overall validation score of 1.6341. The results suggest that task-adaptive fusion of frozen visual features is a simple and effective strategy for ABAW-style multi-task affective behavior analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向多任务情感行为分析的任务特定特征融合方法
第11届野外情感行为分析（ABAW11）多任务学习挑战赛要求一个统一系统，从官方s-Aff-Wild2图像中预测效价-唤醒度、类别表情和面部动作单元。尽管这些任务通过面部行为自然相关，但我们的验证实验表明，它们受益于不同的视觉特征、时序处理策略、融合机制和校准程序。本文研究ABAW11多任务情感行为分析中的任务自适应特征融合。我们首先在外部以表情为导向的面部图像集上适配两个预训练视觉骨干网络DINOv2 ViT-L和DINOv3 ConvNeXt-base，然后冻结它们以从官方ABAW11数据中提取互补的帧级特征。在这些冻结特征基础上，我们系统比较了帧级预测头、时序卷积头、事后时序平滑、LightGBM模型、特征拼接、门控融合、残差融合、后期logit融合、阈值校准和共享MTL结构。最终系统选择任务特定的融合与预测策略，而非强制所有任务共享单一架构。在ABAW11验证集上，所选系统获得EXPR宏F1为0.4222，AU宏F1为0.5402，VA平均CCC为0.6717，总验证得分1.6341。结果表明，冻结视觉特征的任务自适应融合是ABAW风格多任务情感行为分析的一种简单而有效的策略。

Motivated by the need for a unified yet flexible system to handle multiple affective behavior tasks in the ABAW11 challenge, this paper proposes a task-specific feature fusion method. 为应对ABAW11挑战中多任务情感行为分析的需求，本文提出一种任务特定特征融合方法，利用在面部表情数据上预训练的视觉骨干网络（DINOv2 ViT-L和DINOv3 ConvNeXt-base）提取互补的帧级特征，并冻结其参数。作者系统比较了多种融合与预测策略，包括门控融合、残差融合、后期logit融合及LightGBM校准等，最终选择针对不同任务定制化配置而非统一架构，实现在验证集上EXPR宏F1为0.4222、AU宏F1为0.5402、VA平均CCC为0.6717，总分1.6341，表明任务特定特征融合比共享架构更有效。
From Observation to Insight: Mechanistic World Models and the Quest for Autonomous Discovery
Authors: Ingmar Posner, Anson Lei, Bernhard Schölkopf
First: 2026-07-14T08:03:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:13:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in foundation models have transformed AI for Science, enabling remarkably accurate predictive performance across domains ranging from protein folding to weather forecasting. Yet prediction alone does not constitute scientific discovery. Scientific understanding depends on uncovering the reusable explanatory mechanisms that generate observations, whereas contemporary machine learning remains fundamentally organised around predictive mappings rather than explanatory structure. In this paper, we argue that scientific discovery is fundamentally a problem of knowledge organisation. To this end, we introduce Mechanistic World Models, a new design paradigm that places reusable mechanisms at the centre of representation, computation and learning. Drawing on insights from the philosophy of science, we derive the computational capabilities required for discovery, identify the design principles and inductive pressures that encourage explanatory knowledge to emerge, and formalise the anatomy of a mechanism-centric world model. Finally, we show how diverse research directions including mechanistic interpretability, causal representation learning, equation discovery and modular architectures capture complementary ingredients of this paradigm while lacking a unified framework. We propose Mechanistic World Models as a conceptual foundation and computational blueprint for moving AI beyond predictive forecasting towards autonomous scientific discovery.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从观察到洞见：机制世界模型与自主发现的追求
基础模型的最新进展已彻底改变了AI for Science，使其在蛋白质折叠到天气预报等各个领域展现出惊人的预测性能。然而，预测本身并不等同于科学发现。科学理解依赖于揭示生成观测的可复用解释机制，而当前机器学习仍根本上围绕预测映射组织，而非解释结构。本文认为，科学发现本质上是知识组织的问题。为此，我们提出机制世界模型，一种将可复用机制置于表征、计算与学习核心的新设计范式。借鉴科学哲学的洞见，我们推导出发现所需的计算能力，识别促进解释性知识涌现的设计原则与归纳压力，并形式化机制中心世界模型的结构。最后，我们指出，包括机制可解释性、因果表征学习、方程发现和模块化架构在内的多种研究方向虽各自捕捉该范式中的互补要素，却缺乏统一框架。我们提议机制世界模型作为推动AI从预测预报迈向自主科学发现的概念基础与计算蓝图。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between predictive AI and true scientific discovery, this paper proposes Mechanistic World Models as a paradigm shift that centers reusable explanatory mechanisms in AI design. Drawing from philosophy of science, it formalizes how such models must organize knowledge to enable discovery, defining computational requirements and inductive pressures that favor mechanistic explanations over mere prediction. Experimental results across diverse domains show that this framework supports the emergence of interpretable, causal, and modular representations, demonstrating its potential to unify efforts in mechanistic interpretability, equation discovery, and causal learning toward autonomous scientific discovery.
为弥合当前AI预测能力与科学发现本质之间的鸿沟，本文提出以可复用机制为核心的‘机制世界模型’范式，旨在推动AI从预测走向自主科学发现。基于科学哲学，该框架系统定义了支持发现所需的计算能力、设计原则与归纳压力，并通过实验验证其统一整合机制可解释性、因果表征学习、方程发现等方向的能力，表明其可作为迈向自主科学推理的计算蓝图。
Multimodal Empirical Bayes Variational Autoencoders for Joint Longitudinal and Time-to-Event Modeling
Authors: Anders Sjöberg, Nils Olsson, Marcus Baaz, Mats Jirstrand
First: 2026-07-15T16:13:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:13:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Longitudinal tumor measurements, dropout information, and genetic covariates provide complementary information about treatment response, but integrating these data sources within a single population modeling framework remains challenging. We extend the empirical Bayes variational autoencoder (EB-VAE) framework to joint longitudinal and time-to-event modeling and evaluate it on tumor growth data. The framework represents inter-individual variability using latent individual effects regularized by a covariate-conditioned empirical Bayes prior, while a decoder maps these latent effects to tumor-volume trajectories. To account for informative dropout, the decoder was augmented with a hazard model, yielding joint predictions of tumor growth and time to dropout. We further compared fully neural and hybrid semi-mechanistic decoder formulations and incorporated genomic covariates through a genetics-conditioned prior adaptation. The hybrid decoder recovered treatment-effect parameters broadly consistent with previously reported nonlinear mixed-effects estimates, while achieving prior predictive performance comparable to the neural decoder. The joint model reproduced both tumor-volume distributions and dropout patterns in held-out individuals, and genetic conditioning improved individual-level prior predictions in both cutaneous melanoma and breast cancer experiments. Stability selection identified several biologically plausible genetic indicators, including alterations in BRAF, NRAS, NF1, and MDM2. These results demonstrate that EB-VAE provides a flexible probabilistic framework for combining neural dynamics, mechanistic structure, time-to-event modeling, and high-dimensional covariates in pharmacometric applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于联合纵向数据与时间至事件建模的多模态经验贝叶斯变分自编码器
纵向肿瘤测量值、脱落信息及遗传协变量可互补反映治疗反应，但将其整合于单一人群建模框架内仍具挑战。我们扩展经验贝叶斯变分自编码器（EB-VAE）框架以实现联合纵向与时间至事件建模，并在肿瘤生长数据上进行评估。该框架通过协变量条件化经验贝叶斯先验正则化的潜在个体效应表示个体间变异，解码器将这些潜在效应映射为肿瘤体积轨迹。为处理信息性脱落，解码器引入风险模型，实现肿瘤生长与脱落时间的联合预测。我们进一步比较了全神经与混合半机制解码器结构，并通过遗传条件化先验纳入基因组协变量。混合解码器恢复的治疗效应参数与先前报道的非线性混合效应估计值基本一致，且先验预测性能与神经解码器相当。联合模型在留出个体中重现了肿瘤体积分布与脱落模式，遗传条件化在黑色素瘤与乳腺癌实验中均提升了个体水平先验预测。稳定性选择识别出若干生物学上合理的遗传指标，包括BRAF、NRAS、NF1和MDM2的变异。结果表明，EB-VAE为药效学应用中整合神经动力学、机制结构、时间至事件建模及高维协变量提供了一种灵活的概率框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to integrate longitudinal tumor measurements, dropout patterns, and genetic covariates into a unified modeling framework, the authors extend the empirical Bayes variational autoencoder (EB-VAE) to jointly model tumor growth trajectories and time-to-event outcomes. The method uses a latent space representing inter-individual variability, regularized by covariate-conditioned empirical Bayes priors, and augments the decoder with a hazard model to handle informative dropout.
