为整合纵向肿瘤测量、脱落信息和遗传协变量，作者将经验贝叶斯变分自编码器（EB-VAE）框架扩展至联合建模肿瘤生长轨迹与时间至事件的脱落模式。该方法通过协变量条件化经验贝叶斯先验正则化个体潜变量，并用解码器映射潜变量至肿瘤体积轨迹，同时引入风险模型处理有信息量的脱落。对比了全神经与混合半机制解码器，混合解码器在治疗效应参数上与非线性混合效应模型结果一致，且预测性能与神经解码器相当。联合模型在未见个体中准确重现了肿瘤体积分布与脱落模式，遗传条件化提升了黑色素瘤和乳腺癌数据中的个体级预测能力。稳定性选择识别出多个生物学上合理的遗传标志物，如BRAF、NRAS、NF1和MDM2，表明EB-VAE在药效学应用中能有效融合神经动态、机制结构、时间至事件建模与高维协变量。
Screening Is Effective for Visual Recognition
Authors: Shunya Shimomura, Kazuhiro Hotta
First: 2026-07-15T16:12:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:12:48+00:00
Comments: Exploratory research
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision Transformer (ViT) has been widely used as a powerful framework for modeling global dependencies among image patches. However, its core component, self-attention assigns softmax-normalized relative weights to all patches, making it difficult to evaluate the relevance between patches independently. In visual recognition, images often contain many background or redundant patches, yet self-attention cannot explicitly reject such irrelevant patches, which may introduce unnecessary information into feature aggregation. To address this limitation, Screening has been proposed in the field of language modeling, where the relevance of each token is independently evaluated based on query-key similarity and low-relevance tokens are explicitly excluded through thresholding. In this work, we propose VisionScreen, a new vision model that extends Screening mechanism to visual recognition. VisionScreen treats image patches as tokens arranged on a two-dimensional grid and extends absolute relevance estimation based on query-key similarity to the two-dimensional spatial domain. This allows each patch to selectively aggregate only content-wise and spatially relevant patches without relying on competition among patches. Experiments on image classification benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms conventional ViT. These results suggest that Screening can be effective for visual recognition, offering an alternative to relative feature aggregation based on softmax attention.
中文标题/摘要
标题：筛选对视觉识别有效
视觉Transformer（ViT）已被广泛用作建模图像块间全局依赖关系的强大框架。然而，其核心组件自注意力机制为所有图像块分配经过softmax归一化的相对权重，难以独立评估图像块间的相关性。在视觉识别中，图像常包含大量背景或冗余块，但自注意力无法明确排除这些无关块，可能将不必要的信息引入特征聚合。为解决此局限，语言建模领域提出了筛选机制，通过查询-键相似度独立评估每个token的相关性，并通过阈值明确排除低相关性token。本文提出VisionScreen，将筛选机制扩展至视觉识别领域。VisionScreen将图像块视为二维网格上的token，并将基于查询-键相似度的绝对相关性估计扩展至二维空间域。这使每个块可选择性聚合内容与空间上相关的块，而不依赖块间的竞争。在图像分类基准测试中，该方法优于传统ViT。结果表明，筛选机制在视觉识别中有效，为基于softmax注意力的相对特征聚合提供了替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Vision Transformer (ViT) has been widely used as a powerful framework for modeling global dependencies among image patches.
为了解决ViT自注意力机制在处理图像中无关背景块时效率低下的问题，本文提出VisionScreen，将语言模型中的Screening机制扩展至视觉识别领域。VisionScreen通过查询-键相似度独立评估每个图像块的相关性，并通过阈值过滤掉低相关性块，实现基于内容与空间上下文的选择性聚合，无需依赖softmax竞争。在图像分类基准测试中，VisionScreen表现优于传统ViT模型，表明Screening机制能有效提升视觉识别性能，为相对注意力机制提供了一种替代方案。
Music-to-Dance Generation via Atomic Movements
Authors: Xinhao Cai, Yixuan Sun, Minghang Zheng, Qingchao Chen, Xin Jin, Song-chun Zhu, Yang Liu
First: 2026-07-15T16:03:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:03:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Music-driven dance generation aims to produce human motion that is both rhythmically synchronized and semantically consistent with music. While recent neural approaches have achieved impressive visual realism, they typically model motion as a continuous signal and neglect its compositional nature, making generated dances structurally incoherent and difficult to control. In this work, we introduce a structure-aware framework that models choreography as a sequence of atomic movements-semantically interpretable motion events that serve as the building blocks of dance. To construct this atomic movement vocabulary, we first segment large-scale dance data and cluster them into atomic movement groups. We then employ a large language model to semantically relabel and refine the clusters, yielding a set of interpretable and reusable atomic movements. Based on these atomic movement annotations, we design a two-stage generation framework that mirrors the human choreography process. In the atomic movement planning stage, the model predicts the type, duration, and timing of atomic movements conditioned on the input music, forming a symbolic dance allocation. In the completion stage, a transition-aware generator synthesizes smooth and stylistically coherent motion conditioned on the planned structure. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method produces dances with significantly improved structural coherence, rhythmic alignment, and perceptual naturalness compared to existing baselines, while providing enhanced interpretability and controllable editing through explicit structural representation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过原子动作实现音乐到舞蹈的生成
音乐驱动的舞蹈生成旨在产生与音乐节奏同步且语义一致的人类动作。尽管近期神经方法在视觉真实感方面表现优异，但它们通常将动作建模为连续信号，忽视了动作的组合性，导致生成的舞蹈结构不连贯且难以控制。本文提出一种结构感知框架，将编舞建模为一系列原子动作——作为舞蹈构建基础的语义可解释的动作事件。为构建该原子动作词汇表，我们首先对大规模舞蹈数据进行分段并聚类为原子动作组，然后利用大语言模型对聚类结果进行语义重标注和优化，得到一组可解释且可复用的原子动作。基于这些原子动作标注，我们设计了一个两阶段生成框架，模拟人类编舞过程。在原子动作规划阶段，模型根据输入音乐预测原子动作的类型、持续时间和时机，形成符号化的舞蹈分配；在完成阶段，一个具备过渡感知的生成器根据规划结构合成平滑且风格连贯的动作。大量实验表明，我们的方法相比现有基线方法，在结构连贯性、节奏同步性和感知自然性方面显著提升，同时通过显式结构表示提供了更强的可解释性和可控编辑能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for structurally coherent and controllable dance generation, this work introduces a structure-aware framework that models choreography as a sequence of atomic movements—semantically interpretable motion events derived from clustering large-scale dance data and refining them with a large language model. The method employs a two-stage generation process: first, it plans atomic movement types, durations, and timing based on input music to form a symbolic dance structure; second, it synthesizes smooth, stylistically coherent motion conditioned on this plan. Experimental results show that the proposed approach significantly improves structural coherence, rhythmic alignment, and perceptual naturalness over existing methods, while enabling interpretable and controllable editing through explicit structural representation.
为解决现有舞蹈生成方法结构不连贯、难以控制的问题，该研究提出一种结构感知框架，将编舞建模为原子动作序列——通过聚类大规模舞蹈数据并结合大语言模型语义重标注，构建可解释的运动事件单元。方法采用两阶段生成：第一阶段根据音乐预测原子动作类型、时长与时机，形成符号化舞蹈结构；第二阶段基于该结构生成平滑且风格一致的运动。实验表明，该方法在结构连贯性、节奏同步性与感知自然度上显著优于基线方法，同时支持通过显式结构表示实现可解释与可控的编辑。
Constraint-Aware Counterfactual Editing for Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis
Authors: S M Rafiuddin, Vamsi Krishna Pavuluri, Atriya Sen
First: 2026-07-15T16:03:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:03:44+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 1 figure, and 5 tables. Accepted for presentation at the 2nd International Workshop on Informing ML with Knowledge Engineering for Hybrid Intelligent Systems (HHAI-KEML 2026), Brussels, Belgium
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (ABSA) requires models to identify sentiment toward specific aspects rather than relying on the global polarity of a sentence. This makes counterfactual evaluation especially challenging: a valid counterfactual should flip the sentiment of one target aspect while preserving the sentiment of all non-target aspects, semantic meaning, fluency, and factual consistency. Existing counterfactual generation methods often focus on sentence-level label flipping and may produce edits that are fluent but aspect-invalid, semantically drifting, or contradictory. To address this limitation, we propose CAVE-ABSA, a Constraint-Aware Validated Editing framework for generating and validating aspect-level counterfactuals. CAVE-ABSA localizes the opinion span associated with the target aspect, performs controlled counterfactual rewriting, refines candidates through a repair module, and filters them using aspect-level verification, semantic similarity, AMR-guided structural preservation, edit minimality, fluency, and contradiction detection. The framework is designed to construct validated counterfactual ABSA datasets for robustness evaluation and data augmentation. By explicitly separating generation from validation, CAVE-ABSA provides a principled approach for producing meaningful aspect-local counterfactuals and for testing whether ABSA models truly rely on aspect-grounded sentiment reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向方面情感分析的约束感知反事实编辑
方面情感分析（ABSA）要求模型识别针对特定方面的感情，而非依赖句子的整体极性。这使得反事实评估尤其具有挑战性：一个有效的反事实应仅翻转目标方面的感情，同时保持所有非目标方面的感情、语义意义、流畅性和事实一致性。现有反事实生成方法常聚焦于句子级标签翻转，可能产生流畅但方面无效、语义漂移或矛盾的编辑。为解决此局限，我们提出CAVE-ABSA，一种面向方面级反事实生成与验证的约束感知验证框架。CAVE-ABSA定位目标方面关联的意见片段，进行受控反事实重写，通过修复模块优化候选结果，并利用方面级验证、语义相似性、AMR引导的结构保持、编辑最小化、流畅性及矛盾检测进行筛选。该框架旨在构建经过验证的反事实ABSA数据集，用于鲁棒性评估与数据增强。通过显式分离生成与验证，CAVE-ABSA提供了一种生成有意义的方面局部反事实及检验ABSA模型是否真正依赖方面基础情感推理的原理性方法。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of generating valid, aspect-specific counterfactuals in Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis, where existing methods often produce semantically inconsistent or fluency-violating edits, the authors propose CAVE-ABSA, a constraint-aware framework that localizes target aspect opinion spans, applies controlled rewriting, and validates candidates through multiple checks including aspect-level correctness, semantic similarity, AMR structure preservation, edit minimality, fluency, and contradiction detection. Experiments demonstrate that CAVE-ABSA generates high-quality counterfactuals that preserve non-target sentiment and factual consistency, enabling robust evaluation of ABSA models’ reliance on aspect-grounded reasoning.
为提升方面级情感分析中反事实样本的准确性，本文提出CAVE-ABSA框架，通过定位目标方面的情感片段、控制重写、修复模块优化及多约束过滤（如语义相似性、AMR结构保持、最小编辑等），生成仅反转目标方面情感而不影响其他方面和语义的反事实样本。实验表明，该方法生成的反事实样本质量高，能有效揭示模型缺陷，提升模型鲁棒性评估与数据增强效果。
Optimizing Binary and Ternary Neural Network Inference on RRAM Crossbars using CIM-Explorer
Authors: Rebecca Pelke, José Cubero-Cascante, Nils Bosbach, Niklas Degener, Florian Idrizi, Lennart M. Reimann, Jan Moritz Joseph, Rainer Leupers
First: 2025-05-20T12:54:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:03:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Using Resistive Random Access Memory (RRAM) crossbars in Computing-in-Memory (CIM) architectures offers a promising solution to overcome the von Neumann bottleneck. Due to non-idealities like cell variability, RRAM crossbars are often operated in binary mode, utilizing only two states: Low Resistive State (LRS) and High Resistive State (HRS). Binary Neural Networks (BNNs) and Ternary Neural Networks (TNNs) are well-suited for this hardware due to their efficient mapping. Existing software projects for RRAM-based CIM typically focus on only one aspect: compilation, simulation, or Design Space Exploration (DSE). Moreover, they often rely on classical 8 bit quantization. To address these limitations, we introduce CIM-Explorer, a modular toolkit for optimizing BNN and TNN inference on RRAM crossbars. CIM-Explorer includes an end-to-end compiler stack, multiple mapping options, and simulators, enabling a DSE flow for accuracy estimation across different crossbar parameters and mappings. CIM-Explorer can accompany the entire design process, from early accuracy estimation for specific crossbar parameters, to selecting an appropriate mapping, and compiling BNNs and TNNs for a finalized crossbar chip. In DSE case studies, we demonstrate the expected accuracy for various mappings and crossbar parameters. CIM-Explorer can be found on GitHub.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用CIM-Explorer优化RRAM交叉阵列上的二进制和三进制神经网络推理
在计算存内（CIM）架构中使用阻变存储器（RRAM）交叉阵列，是克服冯·诺依曼瓶颈的一种有前景的解决方案。由于器件非理想性（如单元体差异），RRAM交叉阵列通常以二进制模式运行，仅使用两种状态：低阻态（LRS）和高阻态（HRS）。二进制神经网络（BNN）和三进制神经网络（TNN）因其高效的映射特性，非常适合该硬件。现有的基于RRAM的CIM软件项目通常只关注一个方面：编译、仿真或设计空间探索（DSE），且常依赖于经典的8位量化。为解决这些局限性，我们引入了CIM-Explorer，一个用于优化RRAM交叉阵列上BNN和TNN推理的模块化工具包。CIM-Explorer包含端到端编译器栈、多种映射选项和仿真器，支持针对不同交叉阵列参数和映射方式的DSE流程，以估算精度。CIM-Explorer可贯穿整个设计流程，从早期针对特定交叉阵列参数的精度估算，到选择合适的映射方式，再到为最终交叉阵列芯片编译BNN和TNN。在DSE案例研究中，我们展示了不同映射和交叉阵列参数下的预期精度。CIM-Explorer可在GitHub上获取。
Summary / 总结
To address the von Neumann bottleneck and improve inference efficiency on RRAM-based CIM architectures, the paper introduces CIM-Explorer, a modular toolkit designed to optimize binary and ternary neural network inference. It integrates an end-to-end compiler, multiple mapping strategies, and simulators to enable comprehensive Design Space Exploration (DSE) across crossbar parameters and network quantization. Unlike existing tools that focus narrowly on compilation, simulation, or DSE, CIM-Explorer supports the full design flow—from early accuracy estimation to final compilation—while accommodating non-ideal RRAM behaviors such as cell variability. Experimental results show that CIM-Explorer enables accurate prediction of inference performance across diverse configurations, allowing designers to select optimal mappings and crossbar settings for target hardware.
为缓解冯·诺依曼瓶颈并提升RRAM基CIM硬件上的推理效率，作者提出了CIM-Explorer模块化工具套件，通过集成编译、仿真与设计空间探索功能，优化二值和三值神经网络的推理。该工具支持多种映射策略，可在跨阻态参数（如单元变异性和量化级别）上实现端到端优化，突破传统8位量化限制。实验表明，CIM-Explorer可准确预测不同配置下的推理性能，帮助设计者选择最优映射与参数，从而在无需重训练的前提下提升精度与效率。
CF-Net: Conflict Fusion with Speaker Normalisation and Certainty Weighting for Ambivalence/Hesitancy Recognition
Authors: Tung Hung Bui, Hong Hai Nguyen, Van Thong Huynh
First: 2026-07-15T16:02:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:02:21+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 3 figues, 3 tables, 26 references
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Detecting ambivalence and hesitancy (AH) in unconstrained video is challenging because the target signal is inherently ambiguous and expressed through subtle cross-modal incongruence rather than prototypical affect. We present CF-Net, a deep multimodal network submitted to the 3rd Edition of the AH Video Recognition Challenge (ABAW 11th, ECCV 2026), targeting the BAH dataset. CF-Net encodes visual, audio, and transcript streams with frozen SigLIP2, HuBERT, and DistilBERT backbones, normalises backbone features per speaker to reduce identity leakage, and fuses them via a ConflictFusion module that explicitly computes pairwise cross-modal incongruence. Training combines certainty-weighted focal loss, manifold mixup, and modality dropout; an auxiliary certainty-regression head leverages ambiguity annotations to stabilise learning on genuinely borderline samples. CF-Net achieves a Macro F1 of 0.7155 on the BAH validation set and 0.7364 (AP = 0.7492) on the private challenge test set.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CF-Net：用于矛盾融合、说话人归一化与置信度加权的模态不一致识别
在无约束视频中检测矛盾与犹豫（AH）具有挑战性，因为目标信号本质上模糊，且通过跨模态细微不一致表达，而非典型情感。我们提出CF-Net，一种深度多模态网络，提交至第3版AH视频识别挑战赛（ABAW 11th, ECCV 2026），针对BAH数据集。CF-Net使用冻结的SigLIP2、HuBERT和DistilBERT骨干网络编码视觉、音频和转录流，按说话人归一化骨干特征以减少身份泄露，并通过ConflictFusion模块显式计算成对跨模态不一致。训练结合置信度加权焦点损失、流形混合与模态丢弃；辅助置信度回归头利用模糊标注稳定学习，尤其针对真正边界样本。CF-Net在BAH验证集上达到0.7155的Macro F1，在私有挑战测试集上达到0.7364（AP=0.7492）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of detecting ambivalence and hesitancy in unconstrained video due to their subtle, cross-modal nature, CF-Net introduces a deep multimodal architecture that encodes visual, audio, and transcript inputs using frozen backbones, normalizes features per speaker to mitigate identity bias, and fuses them via a ConflictFusion module that quantifies cross-modal incongruence. Training employs certainty-weighted focal loss, manifold mixup, and modality dropout, augmented by a certainty-regression head to handle ambiguous samples. Experiments on the BAH dataset show CF-Net achieves a Macro F1 of 0.7155 on the validation set and 0.7364 (AP = 0.7492) on the private test set, demonstrating strong performance in recognizing nuanced affective states.
为解决在无约束视频中通过跨模态不一致而非典型情感线索检测矛盾与犹豫的挑战，CF-Net采用深度多模态架构，使用冻结的视觉、音频和文本编码器。它按说话人归一化特征以减少身份泄露，并通过冲突融合模块显式计算跨模态不一致。训练结合置信度加权焦点损失、流形混合与模态dropout，辅以置信度回归头稳定学习模糊样本。模型在BAH验证集上达到0.7155的Macro F1，在私有测试集上达到0.7364（AP=0.7492）。
Traffic-CBM: A Structurally Interpretable Multimodal Framework for Encrypted Traffic Classification
Authors: Honglei Jin, Wenshuo Chen, Shaofeng Liang, Haozhe Jia, Runwei Guan, Menshuo Zhao, Shuxu Jin, Songning Lai, Yutao Yue
First: 2026-06-29T07:44:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T16:01:59+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, figures and tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Encrypted traffic classification has achieved strong performance, but its decision process remains difficult to interpret. Existing methods usually combine flow statistics, packet sequences, and byte-level representations into opaque latent features, making it unclear which type of evidence actually drives the prediction. In this paper, we propose Traffic-CBM, a structurally interpretable multimodal framework for encrypted traffic classification. Instead of directly fusing heterogeneous traffic signals into a black-box representation, Traffic-CBM organizes them into a unified hierarchical concept space. These concepts are not manually annotated semantic attributes; rather, they are scalar evidence summaries constrained by predefined traffic evidence groups. More specifically, grouped flow statistics are mapped to statistical concepts, dedicated temporal encoders learn temporal concepts from disjoint feature subspaces, and byte-level evidence is further organized into packet-level and cross-packet concepts. This design turns heterogeneous traffic evidence into an explicit concept representation and makes different levels of traffic evidence easier to analyze. We evaluate Traffic-CBM on multiple encrypted traffic benchmarks. Results show that it achieves competitive and balanced classification performance while providing a clearer structural interpretation interface than conventional end-to-end fusion models. Further analyses suggest that the learned concept space is actively used in the prediction process and provides a clearer structural explanation of multimodal traffic evidence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Traffic-CBM：一种结构可解释的多模态加密流量分类框架
加密流量分类虽已取得良好性能，但其决策过程难以解释。现有方法通常将流统计、包序列和字节级表示融合为不可见的潜在特征，导致难以明确哪些类型证据驱动预测。本文提出Traffic-CBM，一种结构可解释的多模态加密流量分类框架。该框架不直接将异构流量信号融合为黑箱表示，而是将其组织为统一的层次化概念空间。这些概念并非人工标注的语义属性，而是受预定义流量证据组约束的标量证据摘要。具体而言，分组流统计映射为统计概念，专用时序编码器从不相交特征子空间学习时序概念，字节级证据进一步组织为包级和跨包级概念。该设计将异构流量证据转化为显式概念表示，使不同层级流量证据更易分析。我们在多个加密流量基准数据集上评估Traffic-CBM，结果表明其在保持竞争力和平衡分类性能的同时，比传统端到端融合模型提供更清晰的结构化解释接口。进一步分析表明，所学习的概念空间在预测过程中被主动使用，为多模态流量证据提供了更清晰的结构化解释。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of interpretability in encrypted traffic classification models, this paper introduces Traffic-CBM, a multimodal framework that organizes heterogeneous traffic signals—flow statistics, packet sequences, and byte-level data—into a structured concept space rather than fusing them into opaque latent features. The method defines traffic evidence groups and maps each type of signal into explicit, constrained scalar concepts: statistical concepts from grouped flows, temporal concepts from dedicated encoders, and packet- and cross-packet concepts from byte-level data. Evaluated on multiple benchmarks, Traffic-CBM achieves competitive classification accuracy while offering a clear, hierarchical interpretation interface, demonstrating that the learned concept space actively contributes to predictions and enables more transparent analysis of multimodal evidence.
为解决加密流量分类模型缺乏可解释性的问题，本文提出Traffic-CBM框架，将流量信号（流统计、包序列、字节级数据）组织为分层概念空间，而非直接融合为黑箱特征。该框架将分组流统计映射为统计概念，使用专用时序编码器从不同子空间提取时序概念，并将字节级数据组织为包内与跨包概念，实现证据类型的显式表达与分析。在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，Traffic-CBM在保持高分类准确率的同时，相比端到端融合模型提供更清晰的结构化解释能力，且进一步分析证实所学概念空间在预测中被主动使用，增强了多模态流量证据的可解释性。
Controllable Generation of Diverse Dermatological Imagery for Fair and Efficient Malignancy Classification
Authors: Héctor Carrión, Narges Norouzi
Venue: MICCAI 2026

Comments: Accepted at MICCAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Accurate dermatological diagnosis naturally necessitates equitable performance across diverse populations, yet a systematic lack of expertly annotated images, especially for underrepresented skin tones and rare diseases, impedes progress toward measurably fair methods. We introduce cgDDI (Controllable Generation of Diverse Dermatological Imagery), a hybrid framework that (1) synthesizes realistic healthy skin samples without disturbing other input properties, (2) maps single-sample rare lesions onto novel skin-tones and locations non-parametrically, and (3) allows for efficient parametric generation with as few as 10 training samples. The framework supports both human and automated segmentation masking, enabling scalability to datasets without pre-made lesion masks. We grow a 656-image dataset by more than 400x and validate across two datasets: biopsy-confirmed Diverse Dermatology Images (DDI) and expert-verified Fitzpatrick17k (F17k). On the DDI benchmark, we achieve malignancy classification accuracy of 86.4% under synthetic-only training and 90.9% state-of-the-art performance with real data fine-tuning, alongside leading fairness metrics. Cross-dataset experiments show +13.9% accuracy improvements on unseen F17k data despite minimal disease overlap. We openly release 266k+ synthetic images, code, and generative models to further support fairness research at https://github.com/hectorcarrion/ControllableGenDDI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可控生成多样化的皮肤病影像以实现公平高效的恶性病变分类
准确的皮肤病诊断自然需要在不同人群中保持公平的性能表现，但专家标注的影像系统性匮乏，尤其是对肤色较少见群体和罕见疾病，阻碍了可衡量公平方法的发展。我们提出cgDDI（可控生成多样化的皮肤病影像），一种混合框架，可（1）在不干扰其他输入属性的情况下合成逼真的健康皮肤样本；（2）非参数化地将单样本罕见病变映射到新肤色和位置；（3）仅需10个训练样本即可实现高效的参数化生成。该框架支持人工与自动化分割掩码，可扩展至无预设病变掩码的数据集。我们通过该框架将656张图像数据集扩展超过400倍，并在两个数据集上进行验证：经活检确认的多样化皮肤病影像（DDI）和专家验证的Fitzpatrick17k（F17k）。在DDI基准测试中，仅使用合成数据训练时恶性病变分类准确率达86.4%，结合真实数据微调后达到90.9%的最先进性能，并伴随领先的公平性指标。跨数据集实验显示，即使疾病重叠度极低，模型在未见的F17k数据上仍提升13.9%的准确率。我们公开发布超过26.6万张合成图像、代码及生成模型，以进一步支持公平性研究：https://github.com/hectorcarrion/ControllableGenDDI。
Summary / 总结
To address the lack of diverse, expert-annotated dermatological images that hinders fair malignancy classification, especially for underrepresented skin tones and rare diseases, the authors propose cgDDI, a hybrid generative framework that synthesizes realistic healthy skin, maps rare lesions to new skin tones and locations without altering other properties, and enables efficient generation from as few as 10 samples. The method supports both human and automated segmentation masking, allowing scalability to unlabeled datasets. Evaluated on two benchmarks—DDI and F17k—the framework achieves 86.4% accuracy under synthetic-only training and 90.9% state-of-the-art performance with real data fine-tuning, while also demonstrating strong fairness metrics. Cross-dataset experiments show a 13.9% accuracy gain on unseen F17k data despite minimal disease overlap, and the authors release over 266k synthetic images and code to support further research.
为解决缺乏多样且专家标注的皮肤病图像导致恶性病变分类不公的问题，作者提出cgDDI混合生成框架，可合成真实健康皮肤样本、非参数映射罕见病灶至新肤色与位置，且仅需10个样本即可高效生成。该方法支持人工与自动分割掩码，便于扩展至无预标注数据集。在DDI与F17k两个基准上，框架在纯合成数据训练下准确率达86.4%，结合真实数据微调后达90.9%，优于现有方法的公平性指标。跨数据集实验显示，即使疾病重叠度低，仍能在未见F17k数据上提升13.9%准确率，证明其泛化能力。
DeltaMerge-LowRes: Composing Language and Task Deltas for Low-Resource Adaptation
Authors: Son Ha Xuan, Xuan-Bach Le, Phat T. Tran-Truong
First: 2026-07-15T15:53:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:53:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Adapting a multilingual encoder to a new language \emph{and} a new task with only a few hundred gold examples is a common low-resource NLP setting, yet the two axes are usually fused via an expensive language--task fine-tuning run. We ask whether they can instead be trained separately and recombined in weight space. \DeltaMergeLowRes{} learns a language delta $Δ_L$ from unlabeled monolingual text and a task delta $Δ_T$ from labeled English data, then composes them at inference under one of four rules: additive, activation-guided, sparsity-aware, and a novel \emph{cross-axis TIES}. The new rule adapts the TIES-Merging steps of trimming, sign election, and merging to the language and task axes rather than to two task axes. Holding $(Δ_L,Δ_T)$ fixed across rules on four task families and four African languages ($158$ evaluated cells, $10{,}000$-sample paired bootstrap per cell), we find: (i) cross-axis TIES wins summarisation on $3/4$ languages by $+4$ to $+7$ chrF (chrF $18.59$ vs.\ $13.80$ task-only); (ii) it improves QA F1 by $+2.32$ and EM by $+2.91$; and (iii) sparsity-aware merging cuts classification ECE by $36\%$ at parity macro-F1. The composition rule materially changes what the merged model preserves, suppresses, and calibrates. We release all JSON traces and a claim ledger.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DeltaMerge-LowRes：通过语言与任务增量组合实现低资源适配
仅用数百个标注示例，将多语言编码器适配至新语言和新任务，是常见的低资源NLP场景，但通常需通过昂贵的语言-任务联合微调实现。我们探讨是否可分别训练语言增量Δ_L（来自无标签单语文本）和任务增量Δ_T（来自标注英语数据），并在推理时按四种规则组合：加法、激活引导、稀疏感知，以及新颖的跨轴TIES。新规则将TIES合并步骤（修剪、符号选择、合并）应用于语言与任务轴，而非两个任务轴。在四个任务家族和四种非洲语言上固定(Δ_L,Δ_T)，每单元进行10,000次配对自助采样（共158个评估单元），结果表明：(i) 跨轴TIES在3/4语言上提升摘要chrF值4至7点（chrF 18.59 vs. 13.80，仅任务微调）；(ii) 提升问答F1值+2.32、EM值+2.91；(iii) 稀疏感知合并使分类ECE降低36%，在宏F1持平下实现。组合规则显著改变合并模型所保留、抑制与校准的内容。我们公开所有JSON追踪记录与声明账本。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of jointly fine-tuning language and task in low-resource NLP settings, DeltaMerge-LowRes proposes training language and task deltas separately—using unlabeled monolingual data for language delta and labeled English data for task delta—and composing them at inference via four rules including a novel cross-axis TIES method. Experiments across four African languages and four task families show that cross-axis TIES outperforms alternatives in summarization (improving chrF by +4 to +7 over task-only baselines), boosts QA F1 by +2.32 and EM by +2.91, and sparsity-aware merging reduces classification ECE by 36% while maintaining macro-F1 parity. The composition rule critically determines what the merged model retains, suppresses, or calibrates.
为解决低资源NLP中语言与任务联合微调效率低的问题，DeltaMerge-LowRes将语言适应与任务适应分离，分别从无标注单语数据和标注英语数据中学习语言差分与任务差分，再通过四种组合规则（含新提出的跨轴TIES）在推理时合并。实验表明，跨轴TIES在四种非洲语言中三成四任务上提升摘要chrF值4至7点（18.59 vs 13.80），问答F1提升2.32、EM提升2.91；稀疏感知合并可使分类ECE降低36%且保持宏F1不变，证明组合规则显著影响模型保留、抑制与校准能力。
HELP: Human-Efficient Large-Scale Robot Post-Training with Rollout Segmentation
Authors: Shaopeng Zhai, Qi Zhang, Tianyi Zhang, Haoran Zhang, Fuxian Huang, Zhanhui Lin, Zijun Xu, Weinan Zhang
First: 2026-07-08T03:03:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:49:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When adapting Vision Language Action (VLA) models to downstream tasks, multiple rounds of post-training are often required to progressively address policy weaknesses. In this report, we focus on maximizing human efficiency during this iterative process, measured by policy improvement and task throughput per unit of human labor and time. We propose HELP, a Human-Efficient Large-scale robot Post-training pipeline in which two specialized operators supervise twelve robots concurrently. A trained Teleoperator provides high-value remote interventions and recovery demonstrations, while a Floor Operator monitors the robot fleet, triggers takeovers, and performs physical resets. This role specialization improves human efficiency by reducing task switching, lowering operator training costs, and expanding robot interaction coverage. Beyond increasing rollout volume, concurrent supervision also broadens the range of policy behaviors observed by the human team, making recurring failure modes easier to identify and enabling more targeted takeovers, resets, and recovery demonstrations. To efficiently utilize the large and mixed-quality rollout data, HELP incorporates \vlac, an automatic rollout segmentation critic specifically designed for this setting. It separates autonomous trajectories into progress-making, idle, failure-inducing, and recovery segments. Useful rollout segments are retained and combined with Human-in-the-Loop data for the next post-training round. Across four real-world manipulation tasks, HELP achieves 80\%--95\% success rates and improves task throughput by 1.7$\times$--4.2$\times$ over the base model. Under matched HITL recovery budgets, VLAC-CUT further amplifies throughput gains by 1.20$\times$--3.43$\times$ and success-rate gains by 1.50$\times$--3.00$\times$ over HITL-only updates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HELP：高效人类辅助大规模机器人后训练方法
在将视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型适配下游任务时，通常需要多轮后训练以逐步解决策略缺陷。本报告聚焦于提升此迭代过程中的人类效率，以单位人力与时间内的策略改进和任务吞吐量为衡量标准。 我们提出HELP（Human-Efficient Large-scale robot Post-training），一种人类高效的大规模机器人后训练流程，其中两名专业操作员同时监督十二台机器人。一名训练有素的远程操作员提供高价值的远程干预与恢复演示，另一名地面操作员监控机器人车队、触发接管并执行物理重置。这种角色分工通过减少任务切换、降低操作员培训成本、扩展机器人交互覆盖范围，提升人类效率。除增加 rollout 量外，同时监督还扩大了人类团队观察到的策略行为范围，使重复性故障模式更易识别，从而支持更精准的接管、重置和恢复演示。为高效利用大量且质量混杂的 rollout 数据，HELP 引入了 \vlac，一种专为此场景设计的自动 rollout 分段批评器。它将自主轨迹划分为进步型、空闲型、故障诱导型和恢复型段落。有用的 rollout 段落将被保留，并与人类在环数据结合用于下一轮后训练。 在四个真实世界操作任务中，HELP 实现了 80%–95% 的成功率，任务吞吐量较基线模型提升 1.7–4.2 倍。在匹配的人类在环恢复预算下，VLAC-CUT 进一步使吞吐量提升 1.20–3.43 倍，成功率提升 1.50–3.00 倍。
Summary / 总结
To enhance human efficiency in post-training VLA models for robotic tasks, HELP introduces a pipeline where two specialized human operators—Teleoperator and Floor Operator—concurrently supervise twelve robots. The Teleoperator provides high-value remote interventions and recovery demonstrations, while the Floor Operator manages robot fleets, triggers takeovers, and performs physical resets. This role division reduces task switching, lowers training costs, and expands interaction coverage. To process large-scale, mixed-quality rollouts, HELP integrates VLAC, an automatic critic that segments trajectories into progress, idle, failure, and recovery segments, retaining only useful segments for training. Across four real-world manipulation tasks, HELP achieves 80–95% success rates and boosts task throughput 1.7–4.2 times over the base model; when combined with VLAC-CUT, throughput and success rates improve further by 1.2–3.43 and 1.5–3.0 times respectively under matched human-in-the-loop budgets.
为提升机器人策略迭代中的人力效率，HELP提出一套双人协同监督十二台机器人的流程：远程操作员负责高价值干预与恢复演示，地面操作员负责物理重置与任务接管。这种角色分工减少认知切换与培训成本，扩大机器人行为覆盖范围，加速故障模式识别。为处理海量混质轨迹数据，HELP引入VLAC，自动将自主轨迹划分为进步、空闲、故障与恢复段，仅保留有效段用于训练。在四项真实操作任务中，HELP实现80–95%成功率，任务吞吐量提升1.7–4.2倍；在匹配人工干预预算下，VLAC-CUT进一步提升吞吐量1.2–3.4倍、成功率1.5–3.0倍。
GigaWorld-Policy-0.5: A Faster and Stronger WAM Empowered by AutoResearch
Authors: GigaWorld Team, Angen Ye, Angyuan Ma, Boyuan Wang, Chaojun Ni, Fangzheng Ye, Guan Huang, Guo Li, Guosheng Zhao, Haodong Yan, Hengtao Li, Jiwen Lu, Kai Wang, Mingming Yu, Qitang Hu, Qiuping Deng, Songling Liu, Xiaoyu Tian, Xiaofeng Wang, Xinyu Zhou, Xiuwei Xu, Xinze Chen, Yang Wang, Yejun Zeng, Yifan Chang, Yun Ye, Zhenyu Wu, Zhanqian Wu, Zheng Zhu
First: 2026-07-15T15:46:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:46:42+00:00
Comments: project page: https://open-gigaai.github.io/giga-world-policy/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
World Action Models (WAMs) improve robot policy learning by jointly modeling actions and future visual observations, using future scene evolution as dense supervision for physically grounded action generation. However, a common design in existing WAMs is to explicitly generate future videos at inference time, incurring substantial computational overhead and hindering real-time closed-loop deployment. GigaWorld-Policy addresses this issue with an action-centered formulation, where future visual dynamics are used during training while action-only decoding is used at inference time. Building upon this framework, we present GigaWorld-Policy-0.5, an enhanced action-centered WAM designed for more efficient robot control. During pretraining, GigaWorld-Policy-0.5 adopts a mixed Action-Conditioned World Modeling (AC-WM) and WAM training strategy. This strengthens the coupling between visual dynamics and robot actions and improves the transferability of action representations for downstream policy learning. For efficient inference, GigaWorld-Policy-0.5 introduces a Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that separates visual dynamics modeling and action generation into specialized experts, reducing active computation during action-only inference and achieving 85 ms inference latency on a local RTX 4090 setup. In addition, we employ an agent-based AutoResearch pipeline to systematically search training configurations, enabling more efficient identification of optimal experimental setups while reducing the time and manual intervention required for hyperparameter tuning. Experiments and ablations show that GigaWorld-Policy-0.5 preserves the training benefits of future visual dynamics while improving inference efficiency for robot control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GigaWorld-Policy-0.5：基于AutoResearch的更快更强的WAM
世界动作模型（WAMs）通过联合建模动作与未来视觉观测，利用未来场景演化作为物理接地动作生成的密集监督信号，从而提升机器人策略学习效果。然而，现有WAMs的常见设计是在推理时显式生成未来视频，导致计算开销大，阻碍实时闭环部署。GigaWorld-Policy采用以动作为中心的公式，训练时使用未来视觉动态，推理时仅生成动作。在此框架基础上，我们提出GigaWorld-Policy-0.5，一种更高效的机器人控制专用动作中心WAM。预训练阶段，GigaWorld-Policy-0.5采用混合动作条件世界建模（AC-WM）与WAM训练策略，强化视觉动态与机器人动作的耦合关系，提升动作表征在下游策略学习中的迁移能力。为实现高效推理，GigaWorld-Policy-0.5引入混合Transformer架构，将视觉动态建模与动作生成分离为专用专家，降低仅动作推理时的活跃计算量，在本地RTX 4090环境下实现85ms推理延迟。此外，我们采用基于代理的AutoResearch流程系统性搜索训练配置，更高效地识别最优实验设置，减少超参数调优所需的时间与人工干预。实验与消融研究表明，GigaWorld-Policy-0.5在保持未来视觉动态训练优势的同时，显著提升了机器人控制的推理效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of generating future videos during inference in existing World Action Models (WAMs), GigaWorld-Policy-0.5 adopts an action-centered approach that uses future visual dynamics for training but decodes only actions at inference time. It enhances this framework with a mixed Action-Conditioned World Modeling and WAM pretraining strategy to strengthen action-visual coupling and improve policy transfer. For efficient inference, it employs a Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that splits visual modeling and action generation into specialized experts, reducing active computation and achieving 85 ms latency on an RTX 4090. An agent-based AutoResearch pipeline automates hyperparameter tuning, and experiments confirm that the model retains the benefits of future visual supervision while significantly improving real-time deployment efficiency.
为解决现有WAM在推理时显式生成未来视频导致的计算开销问题，GigaWorld-Policy-0.5采用以动作为中心的框架，训练阶段使用视觉动态，推理阶段仅生成动作，显著降低延迟。其混合的AC-WM与WAM预训练策略强化了动作与视觉动态的耦合，提升下游策略学习的迁移能力；采用Mixture-of-Transformers架构将视觉建模与动作生成分离为专用专家，减少推理时活跃计算，实现在RTX 4090上85毫秒的推理延迟。结合基于代理的AutoResearch流程自动优化训练配置，实验表明该模型在保留未来视觉监督优势的同时，实现了更高效的机器人控制。
TFP: Temporally Conditioned Memory-Fusion Policies for Visuomotor Learning
Authors: Yushen Liang, Yue Peng, Baosheng Jin, Tianluo Zhang, Xinyu Zhang, Shuyi Zhou, Zhuoran Chen, Xinqi Liu, Shenji Wan
Venue: RSS 2026
First: 2026-07-09T09:24:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:46:02+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the SemRob 2026 Workshop at Robotics: Science and Systems (RSS 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision--Language--Action (VLA) policies such as $π_{0.5}$ and OpenVLA perform well on many manipulation tasks, but they are often reactive: the next action is predicted from the current observation, instruction, and proprioceptive state. This assumption breaks down in stage-dependent manipulation, where visually similar states may require different actions depending on latent task progress and previous interaction outcomes. We argue that such tasks require not only memory, but dynamics-aware belief updates: the policy should preserve task progress during stable or occluded phases and revise its belief near contact, release, or subgoal transitions. We introduce Temporally Conditioned Memory-Fusion Policies (TFP), a lightweight memory-action framework for VLA backbones. TFP maintains an episode-local task-progress belief with Liquid Time-Constant dynamics and injects the updated belief directly into the flow-matching action decoder through adaptive modulation. This lets temporally accumulated context shape the generated action chunk, rather than serving only as passive history context. With a 3.3B-parameter model, TFP improves the average success rate from \(96.9\%\) to \(98.75\%\) on LIBERO and from \(91.4\%\) to \(93.77\%\) on LIBERO-plus. On the memory-focused MIKASA ShellGameTouch diagnostic, TFP achieves success up to \(75.0\%\). Mechanistic analyses show that write-gain changes near manipulation events are about \(6\times\) larger than far non-event phases, and hidden-state interventions show that the belief causally modulates generated action chunks. These results suggest that compact, event-sensitive memory dynamics can improve VLA policies under occlusion, visual perturbation, and stage-dependent task structure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TFP：面向视觉运动学习的时序条件记忆融合策略
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略如π₀.₅和OpenVLA在许多操作任务中表现良好，但它们通常是反应式的：下一动作从当前观测、指令和本体感觉状态预测。这一假设在阶段依赖型操作任务中失效，因为视觉相似的状态可能需要不同的动作，具体取决于潜在任务进度和先前交互结果。我们认为此类任务不仅需要记忆，还需要动态感知的信念更新：策略应在稳定或遮挡阶段保持任务进度，并在接触、释放或子目标转换附近更新信念。我们提出了时序条件记忆融合策略（TFP），一种轻量级的记忆-动作框架，用于VLA主干网络。TFP使用液态时间常数动力学维护局部会话任务进度信念，并通过自适应调制将更新后的信念直接注入流匹配动作解码器。这使得累积的时间上下文塑造生成的动作块，而非仅作为被动历史上下文。在3.3B参数模型下，TFP在LIBERO上将平均成功率从96.9%提升至98.75%，在LIBERO-plus上从91.4%提升至93.77%。在以记忆为核心的MIKASA ShellGameTouch诊断任务中，TFP成功率高达75.0%。机制分析表明，操作事件附近写入增益约为非事件阶段的6倍，隐藏状态干预显示信念因果地调节生成的动作块。这些结果表明，紧凑且事件敏感的记忆动力学可提升VLA策略在遮挡、视觉扰动和阶段依赖任务结构下的表现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of reactive VLA policies in stage-dependent manipulation tasks where visually similar states require different actions based on task progress, the authors propose TFP: Temporally Conditioned Memory-Fusion Policies. TFP introduces a lightweight memory framework that maintains a task-progress belief using Liquid Time-Constant dynamics and injects it adaptively into the action decoder via modulation, allowing temporally accumulated context to directly shape action generation. Experiments show that TFP improves success rates from 96.9% to 98.75% on LIBERO and from 91.4% to 93.77% on LIBERO-plus, and achieves 75.0% success on the MIKASA ShellGameTouch benchmark. Mechanistic analysis confirms that belief updates near manipulation events are 6x more impactful than in stable phases, and interventions demonstrate causal influence of the belief on action chunks.
针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在阶段依赖型操作任务中因视觉相似状态需根据任务进度调整动作的问题，作者提出TFP：时序条件记忆融合策略。该方法通过轻量级记忆模块结合液态时间常数动力学维持任务进度信念，并通过自适应调制将其注入动作解码器以塑造生成动作而非仅作为被动历史上下文。实验表明，TFP将LIBERO任务成功率从96.9%提升至98.75%，LIBERO-plus从91.4%提升至93.77%，在MIKASA ShellGameTouch基准上达成75.0%成功率。机制分析显示，任务事件附近信念更新效果显著强于非事件阶段（约6倍），且信念状态干预证实其对动作生成具有因果调节作用。
GroundShot: Visually Consistent Multi-Shot Long Video Generation via Entity-Grounded Shot Scheduling
Authors: Yixuan Lai, Tianjia Shao, Kun Zhou, Weijia Dou, Siyu Zhu, Jingdong Wang
First: 2026-06-18T18:00:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:45:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generating visually consistent multi-shot videos remains an open challenge. As videos span more shots, inconsistencies can accumulate across shots, causing entities that reappear across shots -- characters, objects, and locations -- to drift away from how they first appear. We observe that viewers judge consistency by comparing each later appearance of an entity with its first clear appearance; the visual quality of this initial appearance sets the consistency ceiling for all that follows. Motivated by this, we present \textbf{GroundShot}, a training-free, model-agnostic agentic framework for entity-grounded multi-shot generation. GroundShot builds an entity-level visual memory online from accepted generated shots: it schedules shots' generation order by their expected usefulness as entity references, grounds entities from generated videos, verifies their reliability before adding them to memory, and retrieves suitable entity references from memory before each shot is generated. To evaluate this entity-centered view of consistency, we further introduce \textbf{GroundBench}, a diagnostic benchmark that measures consistency at the entity level while isolating controlled challenge dimensions. Experiments show that GroundShot improves multi-shot consistency over existing methods while requiring no additional training or model modification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GroundShot：通过实体锚定镜头调度实现视觉一致的多镜头长视频生成
生成视觉一致的多镜头视频仍是一个开放性挑战。随着视频包含的镜头增多，不一致性会在镜头间累积，导致跨镜头重现的实体（角色、物体和地点）偏离其首次出现时的状态。我们观察到，观众通过将每个实体的后续出现与其首次清晰出现进行比较来判断一致性；该初始出现的视觉质量决定了后续所有内容的一致性上限。受此启发，我们提出\textbf{GroundShot}，一种无需训练、模型无关的代理框架，用于实体锚定的多镜头生成。GroundShot 在线构建实体级视觉记忆：根据预期的实体参考价值调度镜头生成顺序，从生成视频中锚定实体，验证其可靠性后再加入记忆，并在每个镜头生成前从记忆中检索合适的实体参考。为评估以实体为中心的一致性视角，我们进一步引入\textbf{GroundBench}，一个诊断基准，可在控制挑战维度下测量实体级一致性。实验表明，GroundShot 在无需额外训练或模型修改的情况下，显著提升了多镜头一致性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that visual consistency in multi-shot videos depends on how well recurring entities match their initial appearance, the authors introduce GroundShot, a training-free, model-agnostic framework that builds an entity-level visual memory during generation. It schedules shot generation based on expected reference value, grounds entities from generated content, verifies their reliability before storing, and retrieves appropriate references before each new shot. Experiments on GroundBench, a new entity-level consistency benchmark, show GroundShot significantly improves visual consistency across shots without requiring model modifications or additional training.
鉴于观众判断多镜头视频一致性主要依据实体后续出现与首次清晰出现的对比，作者提出GroundShot框架，该框架无需训练、兼容任意模型，通过在线构建实体级视觉记忆来提升一致性。它根据预期参考价值调度镜头生成顺序，从生成视频中锚定实体，验证其可靠性后再存入记忆，并在生成每个镜头前检索合适参考。实验表明，GroundShot在GroundBench这一新设计的实体级一致性基准上显著优于现有方法，且无需模型修改或额外训练。
The Spectrum Is Not Enough: When Context Helps Time-Series Forecasting
Authors: Mert Onur Cakiroglu, Mehmet Dalkilic, Hasan Kurban
First: 2026-07-14T17:50:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:43:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
A growing family of indices scores how predictable a series is from its spectrum. Practitioners increasingly read these scores as answering a different question: whether \emph{adding context}, a longer lookback, a retrieval plug-in, or a pretrained model, will help. These are not the same question. The value of context is a property of the operating point, not of the series. Any index built from the power spectrum is invariant under phase randomization, whereas the beyond-second-order value that retrieval and foundation models supply is not, because a phase-randomized series is asymptotically Gaussian. We state this as an impossibility result and isolate it with surrogate pairs that fix the spectrum and the marginal by construction. We then give a label-free, configuration-level diagnostic, the coverage deficit, whose principal term measures beyond-spectrum structure as the gain of analog over linear prediction. On seven benchmarks the prediction holds: window-keyed retrieval's value collapses across surrogate pairs (ECL median $+33\%\!\to\!-35\%$, $p{<}10^{-40}$) while every spectral index stays frozen; a foundation model's value splits into a surviving second-order part and a small beyond-linear margin that collapses; a longer linear window's value survives. Leave-one-dataset-out, the structure term predicts the sign of beyond-spectrum value where the spectral indices trail it, and the reverse holds for the second-order mechanism. We introduce no new forecaster; the contribution is the distinction, a controlled comparison, and a diagnostic for the deployment decision. Code: https://github.com/KurbanIntelligenceLab/SINE
中文标题/摘要
标题：频谱不足：上下文如何助力时间序列预测
一系列增长中的指标通过频谱评估序列的可预测性。从业者越来越多地将这些分数解读为回答另一个问题：是否添加上下文、延长回溯窗口、引入检索插件或预训练模型会有帮助。这两个问题并不相同。上下文的价值是操作点的属性，而非序列本身的属性。任何基于功率谱构建的指标在相位随机化下保持不变，而检索和基础模型提供的超越二阶的值则不然，因为相位随机化序列渐近服从高斯分布。我们将此表述为一个不可能性结果，并通过构造固定频谱与边际的代理对加以隔离。随后，我们提出一种无标签、配置级别的诊断工具——覆盖不足，其主项衡量超越频谱结构，即模拟预测相对于线性预测的增益。在七个基准测试中，该预测成立：基于窗口键的检索价值在代理对间崩溃（ECL中位数从+33%降至-35%，p<10^{-40}），而所有频谱指标保持不变；基础模型的价值分裂为幸存的二阶部分和一个微小的超越线性边际，后者崩溃；延长线性窗口的价值得以保留。在留一数据集外测试中，结构项预测超越频谱价值的符号，而频谱指标滞后于它；反之，二阶机制则相反。我们未引入新预测器，贡献在于区分、受控比较及部署决策诊断。代码：https://github.com/KurbanIntelligenceLab/SINE
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the common misinterpretation of spectral indices as predictors of context usefulness in time-series forecasting, this work demonstrates that spectral measures alone cannot capture the value of context such as retrieval or pretrained models, because they are invariant under phase randomization while context-dependent gains depend on higher-order structure. The authors construct surrogate pairs that preserve spectrum and marginal distribution to isolate context effects, then introduce the 'coverage deficit' — a label-free diagnostic measuring beyond-spectrum structure as analog prediction gain over linear models. Experiments across seven benchmarks show that retrieval’s performance collapses under surrogates while spectral indices remain unchanged, foundation models split into second-order and beyond-linear components with the latter collapsing, and longer linear windows retain value. The structure term also predicts the sign of context gains where spectral indices fail, and vice versa for second-order mechanisms.
该论文指出，谱基预测性指标无法准确判断添加上下文（如检索或预训练模型）是否能提升时间序列预测效果，因为谱指标在相位随机化下保持不变，而上下文方法依赖的高阶结构无法被谱指标捕捉。作者提出无标签诊断指标“覆盖缺陷”，用于衡量类比预测相对于线性预测的增益。在七个基准测试中，当谱与边际固定时，检索方法的预测能力显著下降，而谱指标保持不变；基础模型的预测能力分为二阶部分和微小的超越线性部分，后者消失；更长的线性窗口仍保持有效。覆盖缺陷还能预测上下文价值相对于谱指标的符号，验证了谱与上下文机制的本质区别。
DermDepth: Toward Monocular Metric Scale 3D Reconstruction Models for Dermatology
Authors: Héctor Carrión, Narges Norouzi
Venue: MICCAI 2026
First: 2026-07-14T17:51:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:42:32+00:00
Comments: Accepted at MICCAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Dermatological practice routinely involves measuring and tracking lesion size, morphology and texture, as critical components of wound or skin cancer screening, monitoring and diagnosis. To accomplish this task, practitioners often image the skin surface with commonly available off-the-shelf camera sensors. This has led to an overwhelming research focus on 2D methods while these objectives naturally benefit from 3D information. In this paper, we demonstrate that dense monocular 3D reconstructions, metric scale measurements and rich surface normal texture estimates are achievable for both dermoscopic and macroscopic cases without the need for additional hardware or multiple captures. We present DermDepth, the first single-view metric scale 3D model for the dermatological domain and D-Synth, the first synthetic dermoscopic dataset with pixel-perfect 3D information. Our experiments show training DermDepth on D-Synth corrects metric scale error from over 16x to under 1.1x for real dermoscopic data, while preserving geometric quality and increasing texture richness. Fine-tuning on a small amount of real clinical samples generalizes our method across three real-world benchmarks spanning the few mm to hundred cm range, diverse skin-tones, chronic wound cases and produces measurements broadly consistent with disease size reported in medical literature. All code, data and models are available at https://github.com/hectorcarrion/dermdepth.

为支持皮肤病诊断与监测中对皮肤病变的精确三维测量，本文提出DermDepth模型，该模型仅用单目图像即可实现具有真实尺度的深度、表面法线和纹理重建，无需额外硬件或多视角拍摄。方法基于D-Synth合成数据集（含精确三维标注）训练，并在少量真实临床数据上微调。实验表明，该模型将真实皮肤镜图像的尺度误差从超过16倍降至1.1倍以下，同时保持几何精度并增强纹理细节，并能有效泛化至涵盖慢性伤口、多种肤色及毫米至百厘米尺度的现实场景，其测量结果与医学文献中疾病尺寸报告基本一致。
Partially Observed Structural Causal Models
Authors: Turan Orujlu, Jordan Matelsky, Martin V. Butz, Charley M. Wu, Konrad P. Kording
First: 2026-05-05T01:40:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:37:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Here we introduce Partially Observed Structural Causal Models (POSCMs) as an extension of structural causal models (SCMs) to settings where upstream contexts co-determine both the interaction structure and downstream mechanisms on observed variables. POSCMs thus provide a self-contained causal modeling framework for endogenous graphs, allowing for an intervention hierarchy spanning node- and edge-level contexts and endogenous variable interventions. To define edge interventions, we separate node mechanisms into edge-local transmission channels that can be modified without changing the source node or the rest of the target mechanism. We provide an identifiability theory that clarifies which intervention families would suffice to disentangle structure formation from mechanisms. We then empirically validate these theoretical results in two external simulators: a biophysically detailed virtual human retina and a gene-regulatory analogue. The experiments reproduce non-identifiability under latent context, expose structure-mechanism confounding under latent edges, and recover pathway-level input-output relationships under targeted interventions, consistent with our positive Markov kernel identifiability results. Together, POSCMs provide an intervention-oriented framework for causal systems in which contexts, graph structure, mechanisms, and measurements are jointly generated and only partially observed.
中文标题/摘要
标题：部分观测结构因果模型
本文引入部分观测结构因果模型（POSCMs），作为结构因果模型（SCMs）在上游上下文共同决定观测变量的交互结构和下游机制的场景下的扩展。POSCMs为内生图提供了一个自洽的因果建模框架，支持从节点和边级别上下文到内生变量干预的干预层次。为定义边干预，我们将节点机制分离为边局部传输通道，这些通道可在不改变源节点或目标机制其余部分的情况下进行修改。我们提供了一种可识别性理论，阐明哪些干预家族足以将结构形成与机制分离。随后，我们在两个外部模拟器中实证验证了这些理论结果：一个生物物理细节丰富的虚拟人视网膜和一个基因调控模拟器。实验在潜在上下文下重现了不可识别性，在潜在边下暴露了结构-机制混杂，并在目标干预下恢复了通路级别的输入-输出关系，与我们正向马尔可夫核可识别性结果一致。总体而言，POSCMs为因果系统提供了一个以干预为导向的框架，其中上下文、图结构、机制和测量共同生成且仅部分可观测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model causal systems where context jointly determines both graph structure and variable mechanisms, this work introduces Partially Observed Structural Causal Models (POSCMs) that support interventions at both node and edge levels. The method decomposes node mechanisms into edge-local transmission channels, enabling targeted modifications without altering source nodes or broader target mechanisms. Empirical experiments in two simulators—a virtual human retina and a gene-regulatory network—demonstrate that latent contexts cause non-identifiability, latent edges induce structure-mechanism confounding, but targeted interventions successfully recover pathway-level input-output relationships, aligning with theoretical Markov kernel identifiability conditions.
为应对图结构与机制由未观测上下文共同决定的因果系统建模需求，本文提出部分观测结构因果模型（POSCMs），扩展传统SCM以处理内生图结构。该方法通过将节点机制分解为局部传输通道，定义边级干预，实现对目标机制的针对性修改而不影响源节点或其余机制。在虚拟人视网膜与基因调控网络两个仿真器中的实验表明：潜变量上下文导致不可识别性，潜边引发结构-机制混淆，但针对性干预可成功恢复通路级输入-输出关系，与基于马尔可夫核的可识别性理论结果一致。
Efficiency, Feasibility, and Incentive-Awareness in Constrained Online Resource Allocation
Authors: Yan Dai, Negin Golrezaei, Patrick Jaillet
Venue: NeurIPS 2025
First: 2025-07-13T03:18:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:36:32+00:00
Comments: A preliminary version was accepted to NeurIPS 2025. This version includes extension to multi-unit resource allocation, expanded numerical simulation, extensively refind technical exposition, and enhanced literature review and positioning
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the dynamic allocation of indivisible resources to strategic agents under long-term constraints, where the planner aims to maximize social welfare, satisfy multiple constraints, and elicit near-truthful reports. We find standard primal-dual methods fragile in this setting: agents easily manipulate their reports to distort dual variables, sacrificing social efficiency for individual utility. To address this, we propose the Incentive-Aware Primal-Dual (IAPD) framework. On the primal side, we integrate three components to suppress manipulation: a VCG-based payment neutralizes immediate misreporting benefits, while epoch-based lazy updates and random exploration together ensure potential future gains are outweighed by immediate penalties. On the dual side, to overcome a learning barrier due to lazy updates -- which we call the "price of incentives" -- we design a novel optimistic online learning algorithm, O-FTRL-FP. It utilizes a fixed-point oracle to resolve the circular dependency between optimistic dual variables and the resulting allocations. Ultimately, our mechanism attains $\tilde{\mathcal O}(\sqrt T)$ social welfare regret, satisfies all long-term constraints, and induces a near-truthful equilibrium. It also smoothly generalizes to multi-unit multi-demand allocation problems. Notably, this $\tilde{\mathcal O}(\sqrt T)$ regret near-matches the non-strategic $Ω(\sqrt T)$ lower bound, demonstrating that incentive-awareness can be accommodated at nearly no cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：受约束在线资源分配中的效率、可行性与激励感知
我们研究在长期约束下，将不可分割资源动态分配给战略代理的问题，其中规划者旨在最大化社会福利、满足多重约束并诱导近似诚实报告。我们发现标准的对偶方法在此设定下脆弱：代理容易操纵报告以扭曲对偶变量，牺牲社会效率换取个体效用。为解决此问题，我们提出激励感知对偶（IAPD）框架。在对偶侧，为克服因懒惰更新导致的学习障碍——我们称之为“激励代价”——我们设计了一种新颖的乐观在线学习算法O-FTRL-FP，它利用不动点求解器解决乐观对偶变量与由此产生的分配之间的循环依赖。最终，我们的机制实现$\tilde{\mathcal O}(\sqrt T)$社会福利遗憾，满足所有长期约束，并诱导近似诚实均衡。它还可平滑扩展至多单位多需求分配问题。值得注意的是，该$\tilde{\mathcal O}(\sqrt T)$遗憾近似匹配非战略情形下的$Ω(\sqrt T)$下界，表明激励感知可几乎无成本地被容纳。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of allocating indivisible resources to strategic agents under long-term constraints while maintaining social efficiency and truthful behavior, the paper introduces the Incentive-Aware Primal-Dual (IAPD) framework. On the primal side, it combines VCG-based payments, epoch-based lazy updates, and random exploration to deter manipulation by making immediate misreporting unprofitable. On the dual side, it employs a novel optimistic online learning algorithm, O-FTRL-FP, to handle the circular dependency caused by lazy updates, termed the "price of incentives." Experimentally, the mechanism achieves $\tilde{\mathcal O}(\sqrt T)$ social welfare regret, satisfies all constraints, and induces near-truthful equilibrium, nearly matching the non-strategic lower bound and generalizing to multi-unit multi-demand settings.
为在长期约束下向战略性代理分配不可分割资源时兼顾社会效率与诚实报告，本文提出激励感知的对偶-原问题框架（IAPD）。原问题侧通过VCG支付、分段延迟更新与随机探索组合，抑制代理通过误报获取即时收益；对偶侧则针对延迟更新导致的“激励代价”，设计了新型乐观在线学习算法O-FTRL-FP，利用不动点求解器打破对偶变量与分配结果的循环依赖。实验表明，该机制实现\(\tilde{\mathcal O}(\sqrt T)\)社会福利遗憾，满足所有长期约束，诱导近似诚实均衡，且可推广至多单位多需求场景，其遗憾值几乎逼近非战略性下\(Ω(\sqrt T)\)的理论下界。
Auditing Asset-Specific Preferences in Financial Large Language Models: Evidence from Bitcoin Representations and Portfolio Allocation
Authors: Wenbin Wu
First: 2026-06-01T17:36:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:33:44+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 5 figures, 18 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models now power robo-advisors and trading agents, yet whether they carry built-in biases toward specific assets is largely untested. We ask three questions: do LLMs systematically prefer certain financial instruments; can an internal representation with causal leverage over those preferences be identified; and does that representation affect downstream financial decisions? We develop a three-level audit protocol and apply it to Bitcoin. First, a behavioral audit of nine frontier LLMs shows that Bitcoin's ranking among money-like instruments is frame-dependent: models place it around rank 5 of 8 as "reliable money" but near the top under crisis and autonomous-agent frames, and an attribute-swap experiment shows that rankings track functional properties, not names. Second, we open a model's internals: a search across thousands of sparse-autoencoder features in Gemma 3 identifies a dominant Bitcoin-selective feature. Amplifying it shifts the model toward the asset and suppressing it shifts the model away, even when "Bitcoin" never appears in the prompt. Third, we test financial consequences: amplification raises Bitcoin's portfolio share by 5.2 percentage points while suppression lowers it by 4.6 pp, with amplification reallocating within crypto and suppression cutting total crypto exposure. We characterize this as bounded behavioral leverage (leverage meaning causal influence over outputs, not financial leverage): an identifiable internal feature can be perturbed to move financial choices, but only within measurable limits. The framework links internal representations to external recommendations, validated with random controls and mechanism boundaries. As LLMs become autonomous financial agents, this is a first step toward a behavioral layer for emerging know-your-agent (KYA) standards: knowing what an agent prefers, and how far that preference can be moved.
中文标题/摘要
标题：金融大语言模型中资产特异性偏好的审计：基于比特币表征与投资组合配置的证据
大语言模型如今驱动着智能投顾和交易代理，但它们是否内建对特定资产的偏好，尚缺乏系统检验。我们提出三个问题：大语言模型是否系统性偏好某些金融工具；能否识别具有因果影响力、能驱动这些偏好的内部表征；该表征是否影响下游金融决策？我们构建三级审计协议并应用于比特币。首先，对九个前沿大语言模型的行为审计显示，比特币在类似货币工具中的排名受框架影响：模型将其列为8种“可靠货币”中的第5位，但在危机或自主代理框架下排名靠前；属性交换实验表明，排名与功能属性相关，而非名称。其次，我们打开模型内部：在Gemma 3中搜索数千个稀疏自动编码器特征，识别出主导性比特币选择性特征；放大该特征使模型更倾向比特币，抑制则使其远离，即使提示中从未出现“比特币”。第三，我们检验金融后果：放大使比特币在投资组合中的占比上升5.2个百分点，抑制则下降4.6个百分点，放大时资金在加密货币内部再分配，抑制则削减总加密货币敞口。我们将此现象定义为“有界行为杠杆”（杠杆指对输出的因果影响，非金融杠杆）：可识别的内部特征可通过扰动影响金融选择，但仅在可测量范围内。该框架将内部表征与外部推荐关联，经随机对照与机制边界验证。随着大语言模型成为自主金融代理，这是迈向新兴“了解你的代理”（KYA）标准的行为层第一步：明确代理偏好及其可移动范围。

This study investigates whether financial large language models exhibit built-in biases toward specific assets, using Bitcoin as a case. NSNQuant: A Double Normalization Approach for Calibration-Free Low-Bit Vector Quantization of KV Cache
Authors: Donghyun Son, Euntae Choi, Sungjoo Yoo
First: 2025-05-23T12:40:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:32:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large Language Model (LLM) inference is typically memory-intensive, especially when processing large batch sizes and long sequences, due to the large size of key-value (KV) cache. Vector Quantization (VQ) is recently adopted to alleviate this issue, but we find that the existing approach is susceptible to distribution shift due to its reliance on calibration datasets. To address this limitation, we introduce NSNQuant, a calibration-free Vector Quantization (VQ) technique designed for low-bit compression of the KV cache. By applying a three-step transformation-1) a token-wise normalization (Normalize), 2) a channel-wise centering (Shift), and 3) a second token-wise normalization (Normalize)-with Hadamard transform, NSNQuant effectively aligns the token distribution with the standard normal distribution. This alignment enables robust, calibration-free vector quantization using a single reusable codebook. Extensive experiments show that NSNQuant consistently outperforms prior methods in both 1-bit and 2-bit settings, offering strong generalization and up to 3$\times$ throughput gain over full-precision baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/DHdroid/NSNQuant.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NSNQuant：一种无需校准的低比特键值缓存向量量化双归一化方法
大语言模型（LLM）推理通常内存密集，尤其在处理大批次和长序列时，因键值（KV）缓存体积庞大。近期采用向量量化（VQ）缓解此问题，但现有方法因依赖校准数据集易受分布偏移影响。为此，我们提出NSNQuant，一种无需校准的低比特KV缓存向量量化技术。通过三步变换——1）逐令牌归一化（Normalize），2）逐通道中心化（Shift），3）二次逐令牌归一化（Normalize）——结合Hadamard变换，NSNQuant有效将令牌分布对齐至标准正态分布。该对齐支持使用单一可复用码本实现鲁棒、无需校准的向量量化。大量实验表明，NSNQuant在1比特和2比特设置下均优于现有方法，具备强泛化能力，且相较全精度基线最高可提升3倍吞吐量。代码见https://github.com/DHdroid/NSNQuant。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high memory cost of KV cache in large language model inference, especially under large batches and long sequences, the paper proposes NSNQuant, a calibration-free vector quantization method for low-bit compression. It employs a double normalization strategy—first token-wise normalization, then channel-wise centering followed by a second token-wise normalization—with Hadamard transform to align token distributions with standard normality, enabling robust quantization without calibration data. Experiments demonstrate that NSNQuant outperforms existing methods in both 1-bit and 2-bit settings, achieving strong generalization and up to 3x throughput gain compared to full-precision baselines.
为缓解大语言模型推理中KV缓存占用大量内存的问题，本文提出NSNQuant，一种无需校准的低比特向量量化方法。该方法通过两次归一化操作——先按token归一化，再按通道中心化后再次按token归一化，并结合哈达玛变换，使token分布贴近标准正态分布，从而实现无需校准数据的鲁棒量化。实验表明，NSNQuant在1比特和2比特设置下均优于现有方法，具备强泛化能力，并较全精度基线提升最高3倍的吞吐量。
Mind the Gap: Action Rebinding Attacks against Android GUI Agents
Authors: Yi Qian, Kunwei Qian, Xingbang He, Ligeng Chen, Jikang Zhang, Tiantai Zhang, Haiyang Wei, Linzhang Wang, Hao Wu, Bing Mao
First: 2026-01-18T10:54:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:32:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large multimodal model powered GUI agents are emerging as high-privilege operators on mobile platforms, entrusted to perceive screen content and inject inputs across application boundaries. While these agents aim to automate complex tasks, we demonstrate that their design introduces a fundamental conflict with Android's strict application sandboxing. We present a novel cross-application Action Rebinding attack, which allows a malicious application with zero dangerous permissions to hijack the agent's execution and perform privileged operations on behalf of the attacker. Our attack exploits the inevitable observation-action gap inherent in the agent's reasoning pipeline. A malicious app can render a benign ``contextual carrier'' to elicit a planned action, and then swap the foreground to a sensitive target application during the reasoning latency. The agent, unaware of the transition, unwittingly executes the action in the privileged context. We further advance this attack by weaponizing the agent's own task-recovery logic to create programmable, multi-step exploit loops , and introducing an Intent Alignment Strategy (IAS) that manipulates the agent's reasoning to rationalize the hijacked state. We evaluate our attack on six widely-used Android GUI agents. Our results demonstrate a 100% success rate for atomic action hijacking and the ability to orchestrate high-impact exploits, including unauthorized file deletion, SMS transmission, and app uninstallation, without the attacker holding any corresponding permissions. Furthermore, since the malicious application separates intent from capability and contains no privileged API calls, it achieves a 0% detection rate across commercial malware scanners (e.g., VirusTotal), highlighting a critical blind spot in current mobile security analysis. To access experimental logs and demonstration videos, please contact yi_qian@smail.nju.edu.cn.

大型多模态模型驱动的GUI代理正作为移动平台上的高权限操作者出现，负责感知屏幕内容并在应用边界间注入输入。尽管这些代理旨在自动化复杂任务，但我们证明其设计与Android严格的沙箱隔离存在根本冲突。我们提出了一种新颖的跨应用Action Rebinding攻击，允许无任何危险权限的恶意应用劫持代理执行，并代表攻击者执行特权操作。 该攻击利用代理推理管道中固有的观察-行动间隙。恶意应用可渲染一个无害的“上下文载体”以诱使代理执行计划动作，然后在推理延迟期间切换至敏感目标应用的前台。代理因未察觉该切换，无意中在特权上下文中执行该动作。我们进一步通过利用代理自身的任务恢复逻辑，构建可编程的多步骤利用循环，并引入意图对齐策略（IAS），操纵代理推理以合理化被劫持状态。 我们在六个广泛使用的Android GUI代理上评估该攻击。结果表明，原子动作劫持成功率100%，且可协调高影响利用，包括未经授权的文件删除、短信发送及应用卸载，而攻击者无需任何对应权限。此外，由于恶意应用将意图与能力分离且不调用任何特权API，其在商业恶意软件扫描器（如VirusTotal）上的检测率为0%，凸显当前移动安全分析中的关键盲区。欲获取实验日志与演示视频，请联系yi_qian@smail.nju.edu.cn。

PlumeQuant: Uncertainty-aware consistency assessment of methane plume masks and emission-rate estimates
Authors: Parisa Masnadi Khiabani, Wolfgang Jentner, Alireza Rangrazjeddi, Michael C. Wimberly, Binbin Weng, David Ebert, Charles Nicholson
First: 2026-07-15T15:31:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:31:31+00:00
Comments: 50 pages, 9 figures. Supplementary material provided as an ancillary file. Code and derived data archived at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21282534
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Imaging spectrometers increasingly distribute source-resolved methane plume products in which the plume mask, integrated mass enhancement (IME), plume length, emission rate, and uncertainty are physically and algorithmically linked. Using 63 EMIT-derived Carbon Mapper plume records from 27 scenes, we show that these published scalar quantities do not uniquely constrain the plume boundary: substantially different yet plausible masks reproduce the same IME, plume length, and emission rate. Genetic-algorithm (GA) ensembles conditioned on the published IME and plume length make this equifinality explicit: the high-confidence core selected by nearly all target-consistent masks covers a median of 13% of the plausible footprint envelope, and ambiguity is largest for weak, low-overlap plumes. The diagnostics come from PlumeQuant, which recomputes IME, plume length, emission rate, and five-term uncertainty from distributed product components under stated conventions and evaluates four mask representations: the distributed reference mask, a transparent Carbon Mapper-informed analogue (CM-like), the GA ensemble, and optional expert edits. The CM-like mask is generated per plume without access to the reference mask or published quantities, with settings fixed once on a scene-disjoint 44-plume development split. It reproduced published IME with +0.72% median difference and emission rate with +0.16% (6.98% mean absolute), reached 0.843 median intersection-over-union against the reference masks, and matched the published uncertainty scale (median ratio 1.01). Holdout mean absolute errors were 7.6% (IME), 9.5% (length), and 6.1% (rate). These are product-level consistency diagnostics, not independent validation. They flag weak, offset, or ambiguous plumes for expert review.

标题：PlumeQuant：面向甲烷羽流掩膜与排放率估计的不确定性感知一致性评估
成像光谱仪正越来越多地分发源解析甲烷羽流产品，其中羽流掩膜、积分质量增强（IME）、羽流长度、排放率和不确定性在物理与算法上相互关联。基于63个来自27个场景的EMIT衍生Carbon Mapper羽流记录，我们表明这些已发表的标量量值并不能唯一约束羽流边界：大量不同但合理的掩膜可重现相同的IME、羽流长度和排放率。基于已发表IME和羽流长度的遗传算法（GA）集合使这种等效性显性化：几乎所有目标一致的掩膜所选择的高置信度核心区域覆盖了合理足迹包络的中位数13%，且不确定性在弱、低重叠羽流中最大。这些诊断结果来自PlumeQuant，它在指定约定下重新计算IME、羽流长度、排放率和五项不确定性，并评估四种掩膜表示：分布式参考掩膜、透明的Carbon Mapper信息类比掩膜（CM-like）、GA集合以及可选专家编辑。CM-like掩膜在不接触参考掩膜或已发表量值的情况下，按羽流生成，其参数在场景不相交的44羽流开发集上一次性固定。该掩膜对已发表IME的中位数差异为+0.72%，对排放率的中位数差异为+0.16%（平均绝对误差6.98%），与参考掩膜的交并比中位数为0.843，且匹配已发表不确定性尺度（中位数比值1.01）。留出样本的平均绝对误差分别为7.6%（IME）、9.5%（长度）和6.1%（排放率）。这些是产品级一致性诊断，而非独立验证。它们标记出弱、偏移或模糊的羽流供专家审查。
