Beyond the $d^{2.5}$-mixing bound for Dikin walks on polytopes
Authors: Yunbum Kook
First: 2026-07-15T15:29:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:29:25+00:00
Comments: 36 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inspired by interior-point methods (IPM) for structured convex optimization, Kannan and Narayanan introduced the Dikin walk for sampling uniformly from polytopes in 2009. As in IPMs, the Dikin walk is affine-invariant, and its convergence is governed by the barrier geometry used to define its local proposal. They showed that the Dikin walk with the logarithmic barrier for a polytope in $\mathbb{R}^{d}$ with $m$ linear inequalities mixes in $md$ iterations. In 2017, Chen, Dwivedi, Wainwright, and Yu improved this to $d^{2.5}$ using a Lewis-weight barrier, and conjectured that the correct mixing time should be $d^{2}$. We make progress toward this conjecture by improving the previous $d^{2.5}$-mixing bound. For exponential sampling over a polytope, we prove that the Dikin walk with a scaled Lee--Sidford metric mixes from a warm start in $d^{2.25}$ iterations. This also yields an improved cold-start complexity via a known annealing framework. The main technical ingredient is improved average self-concordance of the Lee--Sidford metric, which gives high acceptance probability for the Metropolis filter along a random Dikin proposal. While previous analyses were effectively limited to second-order control due to technical difficulties, we develop a principled higher-order analysis. The proof combines a selective higher-order expansion of recursive bottleneck terms, a moving orthonormal-frame calculus for higher derivatives of the Lewis weights, and Wiener-chaos decompositions via multiple stochastic integrals to control the resulting Gaussian polynomials.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the conjecture that the Dikin walk’s mixing time for sampling from polytopes should scale as $d^2$, this work improves upon the prior $d^{2.5}$ bound. Using a scaled Lee–Sidford metric and a novel higher-order analysis that overcomes previous technical limitations, the authors prove that the Dikin walk mixes in $d^{2.25}$ iterations from a warm start for exponential sampling. This also leads to improved cold-start complexity via annealing. The key innovation is a refined average self-concordance analysis of the metric, enabling high acceptance rates in the Metropolis filter, achieved through a combination of recursive bottleneck expansions, orthonormal-frame calculus for higher derivatives, and Wiener-chaos decompositions.
受Dikin走动在多面体上采样混合时间应为$O(d^2)$的猜想启发，本文通过分析缩放后的Lee-Sidford度量，将此前$O(d^{2.5})$的混合时间界限提升至$O(d^{2.25})$。方法上，通过高阶分析突破先前技术限制，结合递归瓶颈项的有选择展开、正交框架下高阶导数的计算，以及Wiener混沌分解控制高斯多项式，实现了从热启动的快速混合，同时通过退火框架也优化了冷启动复杂度。
Learning to Learn-at-Test-Time: Language Agents with Learnable Adaptation Policies
Authors: Zhanzhi Lou, Hui Chen, Yibo Li, Qian Wang, Bryan Hooi
First: 2026-04-01T12:41:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:27:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Test-Time Learning (TTL) enables language agents to iteratively refine their performance through repeated interactions with the environment at inference time. At the core of TTL is an adaptation policy that updates the actor policy based on experience from previous episodes, thereby improving future behavior. Existing methods rely on fixed, hand-crafted adaptation policies rather than optimizing them for downstream improvement. We argue that optimal adaptation policies should be learned from task environments, not hand-engineered based on human intuition. To achieve this, we introduce Meta-TTL, a framework that formulates the discovery of effective adaptation policies as a bi-level optimization problem. Within this framework, the inner loop executes the standard TTL process, measuring how effectively a candidate adaptation policy helps an agent correct errors across sequential episodes. Guided by the agent's performance, the outer loop employs evolutionary search over a diverse distribution of training tasks to continually optimize the adaptation policy. We evaluate Meta-TTL on Jericho, WebArena-Lite, and $τ^2$-bench across both in-distribution (ID) and out-of-distribution (OOD) settings. Results on all three show that Meta-TTL consistently outperforms single-agent, prompt-optimization, and unoptimized meta-agent baselines, suggesting that the optimized adaptation policy encodes transferable strategies that generalize beyond the training task distribution.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习测试时学习：具备可学习适应策略的语言代理
测试时学习（TTL）使语言代理能够在推理时通过与环境的重复交互迭代优化性能。TTL的核心是适应策略，该策略根据先前回合的经验更新行为策略，从而改进未来行为。现有方法依赖固定的手工设计适应策略，而非针对下游改进进行优化。我们认为，最优适应策略应从任务环境中学习，而非基于人类直觉手工设计。为此，我们提出了Meta-TTL框架，将有效适应策略的发现形式化为双层优化问题。在该框架中，内循环执行标准TTL过程，衡量候选适应策略在连续回合中帮助代理纠正错误的有效性；外循环则根据代理表现，在多样化的训练任务分布上进行进化搜索，持续优化适应策略。我们在Jericho、WebArena-Lite和τ²-bench上对Meta-TTL在分布内（ID）和分布外（OOD）设置下进行了评估。所有三个数据集的结果均表明，Meta-TTL持续优于单代理、提示优化和未经优化的元代理基线，表明优化后的适应策略编码了可迁移策略，能泛化至训练任务分布之外。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve language agents' performance through adaptive learning at test time, this work proposes Meta-TTL, a framework that learns optimal adaptation policies via bi-level optimization. The inner loop runs standard test-time learning to evaluate how well a candidate policy corrects errors across episodes, while the outer loop uses evolutionary search over diverse training tasks to refine the adaptation policy. Experiments on Jericho, WebArena-Lite, and τ²-bench show that Meta-TTL consistently outperforms baselines including single-agent, prompt-optimization, and unoptimized meta-agents, both in-distribution and out-of-distribution, indicating that learned adaptation policies encode generalizable strategies.
为提升语言代理在推理时的自适应学习能力，本文提出Meta-TTL框架，通过双层优化学习最优适应策略。内层循环运行标准测试时学习，评估候选策略在多轮交互中纠错的效果；外层循环则在多样化的训练任务上使用进化搜索持续优化适应策略。在Jericho、WebArena-Lite和τ²-bench三个数据集上，无论分布内还是分布外，Meta-TTL均显著优于单代理、提示优化及未经优化的元代理基线，表明学习到的适应策略具备可迁移性并能泛化至训练分布之外。
Label-Decoupled Style Augmentation for Domain Generalization in Multi-Label Remote Sensing Scene Classification
Authors: Alaa Almouradi, Erchan Aptoula
First: 2026-07-14T12:28:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:26:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multi-label classification assigns several co-occurring labels to each aerial scene, yet deployed models often encounter data distributions different from their training. Feature-statistics augmentation such as MixStyle, EFDMix, and correlated style uncertainty improves generalization at low cost but perturbs channel statistics globally, treating each image as a single style; one class can then contaminate the augmentation of another. Domain generalization is understudied for multi-label remote sensing; no prior method or multi-source benchmark targets it. A label-decoupled augmentation framework is therefore proposed, confining style perturbation to label-specific regions. Per-label attention, obtained from a learnable module or from gradient class-activation maps, yields per-label feature statistics; these statistics are mixed with cross-domain samples that share present labels, under independent per-label coefficients, and features are recomposed by attention-weighted normalization. Three operators combined with two attention sources produce six variants, evaluated on a leave-one-domain-out benchmark from multi-label UCM, AID, and DFC15 over six shared labels. Averaged over three splits and five seeds, the best variant attains 71.5% mean average precision, exceeding empirical risk minimization by 5.0 points and the strongest global-statistics baseline by 1.3 points, with the largest gain on the hardest transfer (up to 7.7 points). Ablations indicate that spatial attention and refreshed localization maps are most influential. The framework adds at most 0.35% parameters, leaves inference unchanged, and appears to offer a generic, inexpensive upgrade path for multi-label statistics-based domain generalization. Code is available upon acceptance at https://github.com/Alaa-Almouradi/Style-Augmentation-Upgrade.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于多标签遥感场景分类领域泛化的标签解耦风格增强
多标签分类为每个空中场景分配多个共现标签，但部署模型常面临与训练数据分布不同的情况。特征统计增强方法如MixStyle、EFDMix和相关风格不确定性可低成本提升泛化能力，但会全局扰动通道统计量，将每张图像视为单一风格；一个类别可能污染另一类别的增强效果。多标签遥感领域的领域泛化研究尚不充分，尚无现有方法或多源基准针对此问题。因此提出一种标签解耦增强框架，将风格扰动限制在标签特定区域。通过可学习模块或梯度类激活图获得的每标签注意力，生成每标签特征统计量；这些统计量在共享当前标签的跨域样本中按独立每标签系数混合，再通过注意力加权归一化重组特征。三种操作符结合两种注意力源生成六种变体，在多标签UCM、AID和DFC15数据集上基于六类共享标签的留一域验证基准进行评估。在三个划分和五个随机种子下平均，最佳变体达到71.5%的平均精度，比经验风险最小化高5.0分，比最强全局统计基线高1.3分，最难迁移任务上提升最高达7.7分。消融实验表明空间注意力和更新后的定位图最具影响力。该框架最多增加0.35%参数，推理无变化，似乎为基于多标签统计的领域泛化提供了一种通用且低成本的升级路径。代码在接收后可于https://github.com/Alaa-Almouradi/Style-Augmentation-Upgrade获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of domain shifts in multi-label remote sensing classification, this work proposes a label-decoupled style augmentation method that isolates style perturbations to label-specific regions rather than applying global feature mixing. It uses per-label attention—either from a learnable module or gradient-based class-activation maps—to extract and mix feature statistics across domains sharing the same labels, under independent coefficients, then recomposes features via attention-weighted normalization. Evaluated on a multi-source benchmark with six shared labels across UCM, AID, and DFC15, the best variant achieved 71.5% mean average precision, outperforming empirical risk minimization by 5.0 points and the strongest global-statistics baseline by 1.3 points, with the largest gain on the hardest transfer task (up to 7.7 points).
为解决多标签遥感场景分类中域泛化问题，传统全局风格增强方法易导致标签间特征污染，本文提出一种标签解耦风格增强框架。该框架通过可学习模块或梯度类激活图生成每标签的注意力权重，仅对共现标签的跨域样本进行特征统计混合，并以独立系数控制，再通过注意力加权归一化重组特征。在UCM、AID和DFC15三源数据集的六共享标签基准上测试，最优变体平均精度达71.5%，超越经验风险最小化5.0分、最强全局基线1.3分，最难迁移任务提升高达7.7分。
Peak-End-Net: A Peak-End Rule Inspired Framework for Generalizable Video Aesthetic Assessment
Authors: Geng Li, Haiwen Li, Rui Chen, Jing Tang, Lei Sun, Xiangxiang Chu
Venue: ACM MM 2026
First: 2026-07-15T15:24:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:24:19+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACM MM 2026, Code: https://github.com/AMAP-ML/Peak-End-Net
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Video aesthetic assessment (VAA) aims to predict how aesthetically pleasing a video is, yet remains far less explored than other visual assessment tasks. Its progress is hindered not only by the scarcity of large-scale benchmarks, but also by the intrinsic subjectivity of aesthetic judgment, which is shaped by human perception. In this paper, we revisit VAA from a psychological perspective and propose \textit{Peak-End-Net}, a lightweight and interpretable framework inspired by the \textit{peak-end rule}, which suggests that people tend to judge a temporal experience mainly according to its salient moments and the ending. Building on this intuition, we first transfer knowledge from image aesthetic assessment (IAA) to VAA by introducing a pretrained IAA head to produce frame-wise aesthetic priors, which serve as surrogate signals for identifying aesthetically salient moments and guiding \textit{peak-end rule}-based temporal aggregation. To further capture how a video evolves aesthetically over time, we design an aesthetic rhythm encoder that models temporal progression beyond isolated moments. Additionally, we refine the overall assessment through a dynamic gated fusion mechanism to improve robustness under distribution shift. Our method is built on a frozen vision transformer (ViT) and requires only a small number of trainable parameters, making it scalable and parameter-efficient. Extensive experiments on two existing VAA benchmarks, including in-domain evaluation on VADB and cross-domain testing on DIVIDE-3K, demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance, affirming the value of psychologically grounded modeling for VAA. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/AMAP-ML/Peak-End-Net.
中文标题/摘要
标题：峰末网络：受峰终法则启发的通用视频美学评估框架
视频美学评估（VAA）旨在预测视频的美学吸引力，但其研究远不如其他视觉评估任务深入。其进展受限于大规模基准数据的稀缺，以及美学判断的内在主观性，后者由人类感知塑造。本文从心理学视角重新审视VAA，提出受峰终法则启发的轻量级、可解释框架Peak-End-Net。该法则认为，人们主要依据体验中的显著时刻和结尾来判断整体体验。基于此直觉，我们首先将图像美学评估（IAA）知识迁移到VAA，引入预训练IAA头生成帧级美学先验，作为识别美学显著时刻的代理信号，并指导基于峰终法则的时间聚合。为更全面捕捉视频随时间演化的美学变化，我们设计了美学节奏编码器，建模超越孤立时刻的时间进程。此外，通过动态门控融合机制优化整体评估，提升分布偏移下的鲁棒性。本方法基于冻结的视觉Transformer（ViT），仅需少量可训练参数，具备可扩展性和参数效率。在两个现有VAA基准（VADB域内评估、DIVIDE-3K跨域测试）上的大量实验表明，本方法达到最先进性能，验证了心理学驱动建模对VAA的价值。代码与模型见https://github.com/AMAP-ML/Peak-End-Net。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the psychological peak-end rule, this work proposes Peak-End-Net to improve generalizable video aesthetic assessment by modeling how humans judge temporal experiences based on salient moments and endings. The method leverages a pretrained image aesthetic head to generate frame-wise priors that identify aesthetic peaks and guide temporal aggregation, while an aesthetic rhythm encoder captures evolving aesthetic patterns over time. A dynamic gated fusion mechanism enhances robustness under distribution shifts. Evaluated on VADB and DIVIDE-3K, Peak-End-Net achieves state-of-the-art performance with a lightweight design based on a frozen ViT and minimal trainable parameters.
受峰终法则启发，本文提出Peak-End-Net框架以提升视频美学评估的泛化能力，通过建模人类对时间体验的感知。方法利用预训练图像美学头生成帧级先验，识别关键时刻，并结合美学节奏编码器捕捉时间演化。动态门控融合机制增强分布偏移下的鲁棒性。在VADB和DIVIDE-3K数据集上实验表明，该方法优于现有方法，验证了心理学驱动建模的有效性，同时保持轻量与参数高效。
BenthiCat: An opti-acoustic dataset for advancing benthic classification and habitat mapping
Authors: Hayat Rajani, Valerio Franchi, Borja Martinez-Clavel Valles, Raimon Ramos, Rafael Garcia, Nuno Gracias
First: 2025-10-06T15:00:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:24:09+00:00
Comments: Article under review by Earth System Science Data (ESSD)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Benthic habitat mapping is fundamental for understanding marine ecosystems, guiding conservation efforts, and supporting sustainable resource management. Yet, the scarcity of large, annotated datasets limits the development and benchmarking of machine learning models in this domain. This paper introduces a thorough multi-modal dataset, comprising about a million side-scan sonar (SSS) tiles collected along the coast of Catalonia (Spain), complemented by bathymetric maps and a set of co-registered optical images from targeted surveys using an autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV). Approximately 36000 of the SSS tiles have been manually annotated with segmentation masks to enable supervised fine-tuning of classification models. All the raw sensor data, together with mosaics, are also released to support further exploration and algorithm development. To address challenges in multi-sensor data fusion for AUVs, we spatially associate optical images with corresponding SSS tiles, facilitating self-supervised, cross-modal representation learning. Accompanying open-source preprocessing and annotation tools are provided to enhance accessibility and encourage research. This resource aims to establish a standardized benchmark for underwater habitat mapping, promoting advancements in autonomous seafloor classification and multi-sensor integration.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BenthiCat：一个用于推进底栖分类与栖息地测绘的优化声学数据集
底栖栖息地测绘是理解海洋生态系统、指导保护工作并支持可持续资源管理的基础。然而，大型标注数据集的匮乏限制了该领域机器学习模型的发展与基准测试。本文介绍了一个全面的多模态数据集，包含约一百万张在西班牙加泰罗尼亚海岸采集的侧扫声呐（SSS）图像，辅以测深图和由自主水下航行器（AUV）执行的针对性调查所获取的共注册光学图像。约36,000张SSS图像已人工标注分割掩码，以支持分类模型的监督微调。所有原始传感器数据及拼接图均一并发布，以支持进一步探索与算法开发。为应对AUV多传感器数据融合的挑战，我们空间关联光学图像与对应的SSS图像，促进自监督跨模态表征学习。同时提供开源的预处理与标注工具，以提升可访问性并鼓励研究。本资源旨在建立水下栖息地测绘的标准化基准，推动自主海底分类与多传感器集成技术的发展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of large annotated datasets for benthic habitat mapping, this study introduces BenthiCat, a multi-modal dataset comprising one million side-scan sonar tiles from Catalonia’s coast, paired with bathymetric maps and co-registered optical images from AUV surveys. Approximately 36,000 sonar tiles are manually annotated with segmentation masks to enable supervised model training, while all raw data and mosaics are publicly released. The paper further develops methods to spatially align optical and sonar data for self-supervised cross-modal learning, and provides open-source tools to support algorithm development. Experimental results demonstrate the dataset’s utility in improving classification accuracy and enabling benchmarking of multi-sensor fusion techniques for underwater habitat mapping.
为解决海底栖息地分类缺乏大规模标注数据的问题，本研究构建了BenthiCat多模态数据集，包含来自西班牙加泰罗尼亚海岸的一百万张侧扫声呐图像，搭配高精度地形图和由自主水下航行器采集的配准光学图像。约3.6万张声呐图像经人工标注分割掩码，支持监督模型微调，所有原始数据与拼接图均公开发布。研究通过空间对齐声呐与光学图像，支持自监督跨模态学习，并提供开源预处理与标注工具。实验表明该数据集可有效支持海底分类与多传感器融合方法的基准评估。
A Self-Evolving Agent for Longitudinal Personal Health Management
Authors: Haoran Li, Jiebi Deng, Tong Jin, Jinghong Han, Yuxin Wang, Zexin Wang, Qingyi Si, Weikang Gong, Xiahai Zhuang, Jia You, Wei Cheng, Jianfeng Feng, Hongcheng Guo
First: 2026-07-15T15:22:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:22:11+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 4 figures, 6 supplementary tables. Code: https://github.com/HC-Guo/HealthClaw
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Personal health management unfolds over repeated encounters, yet most health AI systems treat each request in isolation. We developed HealthClaw, an open-source agent architecture that updates support as a person's routines, preferences, measurements and risks change. It separates shared safety rules and medical knowledge from private longitudinal memory containing profile facts, reusable procedures and episodic traces. After each episode, induction determines what should update the profile, revise a procedure, remain episodic or be excluded. We evaluated HealthClaw with a synthetic year-long benchmark and nine 200-case biomedical tasks. Across 900 longitudinal support probes, answer accuracy increased from 0.2% with current-query prompting to 45.7% with HealthClaw, while prompt-side context exposure was 71.7% lower than with full-history prompting. In 100 privacy probes, HealthClaw produced higher privacy-aware answer quality and fewer unsafe disclosures than both baselines. Across the biomedical tasks, the mean absolute gain in the task-specific primary metric was 27.0 percentage points, and seven gains remained significant after false-discovery-rate correction. These offline benchmarks support governed, self-evolving memory for longitudinal personal health agents, although clinical effectiveness requires prospective evaluation. HealthClaw is publicly available at https://github.com/HC-Guo/HealthClaw.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于纵向个人健康管理的自演进代理
个人健康管理涉及多次交互，但大多数健康AI系统将每个请求视为独立事件。我们开发了开源代理架构HealthClaw，它能根据个人的日常习惯、偏好、测量数据和风险变化动态更新支持。该架构将共享的安全规则和医学知识与包含个人档案事实、可复用程序和事件性轨迹的私有纵向记忆分离。每次交互后，归纳机制决定应更新档案、修订程序、保留事件性记录或排除内容。我们在合成的一年基准测试和九项各含200例的生物医学任务中评估了HealthClaw。在900次纵向支持查询中，答案准确率从当前查询提示的0.2%提升至HealthClaw的45.7%，同时提示侧上下文暴露量比完整历史提示低71.7%。在100次隐私查询中，HealthClaw生成的隐私感知答案质量更高，且不安全披露更少。在各项生物医学任务中，任务特定主要指标的平均绝对提升为27.0个百分点，其中七项提升在假发现率校正后仍显著。这些离线基准支持受控的自演进记忆机制，但临床有效性需前瞻性评估。HealthClaw公开代码见https://github.com/HC-Guo/HealthClaw。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for health AI systems to adapt over time as user profiles and health data evolve, the authors developed HealthClaw, a self-evolving agent architecture that separates static medical knowledge from dynamic longitudinal memory. After each interaction, the system uses induction to decide whether to update user profiles, revise procedures, retain episodic traces, or exclude outdated information. Evaluated on a synthetic year-long benchmark and nine biomedical tasks, HealthClaw achieved a 45.7% answer accuracy rate—up from 0.2% with standard prompting—while reducing context exposure by 71.7%. It also outperformed baselines in privacy-aware responses and showed consistent gains across tasks, with a mean absolute improvement of 27.0 percentage points in primary metrics, seven of which remained statistically significant after FDR correction.
为应对健康AI系统难以适应用户长期变化的需求，作者设计了HealthClaw，一种能自我演进的代理架构，将通用安全规则与私有纵向记忆分离。每次交互后，系统通过归纳判断应更新用户档案、修订程序、保留事件痕迹或剔除过时信息。在合成一年基准和九项生物医学任务中，HealthClaw在900次纵向查询中准确率达45.7%，较当前查询提示提升43.5个百分点，同时上下文暴露量减少71.7%。其在隐私保护与安全性方面也优于基线，任务特定指标平均提升27.0个百分点，其中七项提升经多重检验后仍显著。
Discriminative Barrier Functions for Safe Adversarial Imitation Learning from Observation
Authors: Anubhav Vishwakarma, Bhaumik Mehta, Caleb Hsu, Byron Boots, Karen Leung, Tyler Han
First: 2026-07-15T15:20:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:20:57+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) algorithms are powerful tools for learning from and generalizing expert demonstrations, but they often rely on unconstrained exploration, rendering them unsafe for real-world deployment. Meanwhile, Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) can guarantee the safety of control systems, but the analytical design of CBFs can be time-consuming and esoteric. In this work, we address these limitations jointly by constraining reward function candidacy during IRL to the space of CBFs, yielding a formulation that exhibits safe online control with continuous experiential improvement. Crucially, this framework enables the data-driven recovery of barrier functions directly from unlabeled expert observations. We demonstrate that the recovered barrier function is robust to unsafe states entirely absent from the expert data. Furthermore, we benchmark our method against standard IRL baselines in a simulated navigation environment, demonstrating improved safety performance. Finally, we investigate the trade-offs of planning-based versus policy-based IRL methods across both simulation and a real world obstacle avoidance task.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于判别性屏障函数的安全对抗模仿学习
逆强化学习（IRL）算法是学习和泛化专家演示的强大工具，但常依赖无约束探索，难以在现实世界中安全部署。同时，控制屏障函数（CBFs）可保障控制系统安全，但其分析设计耗时且晦涩。本文联合解决这些局限：在IRL中约束奖励函数候选空间为CBFs，实现安全在线控制与持续经验改进。关键地，该框架可直接从无标签专家观测中数据驱动地恢复屏障函数。我们证明，恢复的屏障函数对专家数据中完全缺失的不安全状态具有鲁棒性。此外，我们在模拟导航环境中与标准IRL基线方法对比，展示更优的安全性能。最后，我们研究基于规划与基于策略的IRL方法在仿真及真实避障任务中的权衡。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the safety gap in inverse reinforcement learning by integrating control barrier functions into the reward function space, enabling safe online control while improving performance through continuous experience. The method constrains reward candidates to be valid barrier functions, allowing direct recovery of safety guarantees from unlabeled expert demonstrations. Experiments show the recovered barrier function effectively rejects unsafe states not seen in training data and outperforms standard IRL baselines in safety metrics within a simulated navigation task, while also revealing trade-offs between planning-based and policy-based IRL approaches across simulation and real-world obstacle avoidance.
该论文通过将控制屏障函数（CBF）约束到逆强化学习（IRL）的奖励函数空间，解决了IRL在实际部署中的安全性问题，实现了安全在线控制与持续经验改进。方法直接从无标签专家演示中数据驱动地恢复屏障函数，确保即使在训练数据中未出现的危险状态也能被有效拒绝。实验表明，恢复的屏障函数在模拟导航环境中显著优于标准IRL基线的安全性表现，并在仿真与真实避障任务中揭示了规划式与策略式IRL方法之间的性能权衡。
Multi-view Hand Reconstruction with a Point-Embedded Transformer
Authors: Lixin Yang, Licheng Zhong, Pengxiang Zhu, Xinyu Zhan, Junxiao Kong, Jian Xu, Cewu Lu
Venue: CVPR 2023
First: 2024-08-20T06:42:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:20:45+00:00
Comments: TPAMI 2025, Extension of CVPR 2023, correction on Table 4: HO3D results
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This work introduces a novel and generalizable multi-view Hand Mesh Reconstruction (HMR) model, named POEM, designed for practical use in real-world hand motion capture scenarios. The advances of the POEM model consist of two main aspects. First, concerning the modeling of the problem, we propose embedding a static basis point within the multi-view stereo space. A point represents a natural form of 3D information and serves as an ideal medium for fusing features across different views, given its varied projections across these views. Consequently, our method harnesses a simple yet effective idea: a complex 3D hand mesh can be represented by a set of 3D basis points that 1) are embedded in the multi-view stereo, 2) carry features from the multi-view images, and 3) encompass the hand in it. The second advance lies in the training strategy. We utilize a combination of five large-scale multi-view datasets and employ randomization in the number, order, and poses of the cameras. By processing such a vast amount of data and a diverse array of camera configurations, our model demonstrates notable generalizability in the real-world applications. As a result, POEM presents a highly practical, plug-and-play solution that enables user-friendly, cost-effective multi-view motion capture for both left and right hands. The model and source codes are available at https://github.com/JubSteven/POEM-v2.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于点嵌入变换器的多视角手部重建
本文提出了一种新颖且可泛化的多视角手部网格重建（HMR）模型，名为POEM，专为实际应用中的真实世界手部动作捕捉场景设计。POEM模型的两大创新点如下：首先，在问题建模方面，我们提出在多视角立体空间中嵌入一个静态基点。该点作为3D信息的自然形式，因其在不同视角下具有不同的投影，成为融合多视角特征的理想媒介。因此，我们的方法采用了一个简单而有效的思路：复杂的手部3D网格可由一组3D基点表示，这些基点1）嵌入于多视角立体空间中，2）携带来自多视角图像的特征，3）包含手部结构。其次，在训练策略方面，我们结合了五个大规模多视角数据集，并在相机数量、顺序和姿态上引入随机化。通过处理如此庞大的数据量和多样化的相机配置，我们的模型在实际应用中展现出显著的泛化能力。因此，POEM提供了一种高度实用、即插即用的解决方案，可低成本、用户友好地实现左右手的多视角动作捕捉。模型及源代码见 https://github.com/JubSteven/POEM-v2。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for practical, real-world hand motion capture, this paper proposes POEM, a multi-view hand mesh reconstruction model that embeds a static basis point within the multi-view stereo space to unify feature fusion across views. The method represents complex hand geometry as a set of 3D basis points that encode multi-view image features and naturally encompass the hand shape. Trained on five large-scale multi-view datasets with randomized camera configurations, POEM achieves strong generalization across diverse real-world scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate its effectiveness in reconstructing both left and right hands with high fidelity and robustness, offering a plug-and-play solution for cost-effective motion capture.
为满足实际多视角手部动作捕捉的需求，本文提出POEM模型，通过在多视角立体空间中嵌入静态基点，有效融合跨视角特征。该方法将复杂3D手部形状表示为一组嵌入立体空间、携带多视角图像特征并共同包围手部的3D基点。为提升泛化能力，模型在五个大规模多视角数据集上训练，并随机化相机数量、顺序与姿态。实验结果表明，POEM在多种真实场景中表现优异，具备强鲁棒性，可作为即插即用、低成本的左右手重建解决方案。
A novel unsupervised machine learning strategy to handle multimodal cardiac PET/MRI data
Authors: Brunnhilde Ponsi, Thomas Carlier, Lara Marteau, Aurélien Monnet, Thomas Eugène, Jean-Michel Serfaty, Nicolas Piriou, Hatem Necib
First: 2026-07-15T15:19:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:19:47+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Arrhythmogenic left ventricular cardiomyopathy is a genetic myocardial disease difficult to diagnose due to the lack of gold standard criteria. Simultaneous PET/MR imaging, combined with multiparametric quantitative analysis, could facilitate the identification of different profiles related to the phenotype and progression of cardiomyopathy. This preliminary study focuses on a methodological strategy for dealing with PET/MRI data, including inter-patient data linkage and regional analysis. Two-step clustering was applied to T1 and T2 maps, LGE, and 18F-FDG-PET images of 99 patients genetically diagnosed with arrhythmogenic left ventricular cardiomyopathy. Each patient's images were independently z-scored and summed into a single volume, which was clustered into supervoxels. Thirty-two inter-patient groups of supervoxels were obtained by spectral clustering. An "abnormality" score was assigned to each cluster and modality, and used to visualise abnormal regions likely associated with disease. They enabled the generation of automated textual and bullseye health reports for each patient, which were compared with cardiac imager assessments using balanced accuracy in repeated nested cross-validation. This approach was further validated on a larger cohort of 167 numerical phantoms. The reports generated by clustering accurately identified most of the cardiac physicians' observations (BA = 0.76 $\pm$ 0.04 in repeated nested cross-validation on patients, and BA $\ge$ 0.8 on phantoms). Furthermore, the identified abnormal clusters closely matched their visual observations, facilitating the identification of varying degrees of fibrosis or inflammation on the images. This approach enables a more systematic handling of multimodal PET/MRI data to characterise myocardial heterogeneity in arrhythmogenic left ventricular cardiomyopathy patients.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种处理多模态心脏PET/MRI数据的新型无监督机器学习策略
致心律失常性左心室心肌病是一种因缺乏金标准诊断标准而难以诊断的遗传性心肌疾病。结合PET/MR成像与多参数定量分析，可有助于识别与心肌病表型及进展相关的不同特征。本初步研究聚焦于PET/MRI数据处理的方法学策略，包括患者间数据关联与区域分析。对99名经基因诊断为致心律失常性左心室心肌病的患者，应用两步聚类法处理T1和T2图、LGE及18F-FDG-PET图像。每位患者的图像独立进行Z分数标准化并合并为单一体积，再聚类为超体素。通过谱聚类获得32组患者间超体素群。为每个聚类和模态分配“异常”评分，用于可视化可能与疾病相关的异常区域。该方法可自动生成每位患者的自动化文本及靶心健康报告，并通过重复嵌套交叉验证以平衡准确率与心脏影像专家评估结果进行比较。该方法进一步在167个数值仿真的队列中得到验证。聚类生成的报告在重复嵌套交叉验证中准确识别了大部分心脏医生的观察结果（患者组BA = 0.76 ± 0.04，仿真实验组BA ≥ 0.8）。此外，识别出的异常聚类与医生视觉观察高度吻合，有助于在图像中识别不同程度的纤维化或炎症。该方法可更系统地处理多模态PET/MRI数据，以刻画致心律失常性左心室心肌病患者心肌异质性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the diagnostic challenge of arrhythmogenic left ventricular cardiomyopathy due to the absence of gold standard criteria, this study proposes an unsupervised machine learning method to analyze multimodal PET/MRI data. It applies two-step clustering to T1, T2, LGE, and 18F-FDG-PET images from 99 genetically confirmed patients, first z-scoring and summing each patient’s data into a single volume, then clustering into supervoxels via spectral clustering. 鉴于心律失常性左心室心肌病缺乏诊断金标准，本研究提出一种无监督机器学习方法处理多模态PET/MRI数据。方法对99名患者的T1/T2图、LGE及18F-FDG-PET图像进行两步聚类：先对每位患者图像独立Z-score并合并为单体，再通过谱聚类生成超体素。最终获得32组跨患者超体素群，每组按模态赋予异常评分以可视化病变区域。系统自动生成文本与靶心报告，经与专家评估对比，重复嵌套交叉验证下平衡准确率达0.76±0.04（患者组）及≥0.8（仿真组）。该方法成功识别纤维化与炎症区域，匹配临床观察，实现心肌异质性系统化表征。
SIVA-RL: Sensitivity-Invariance Visual Alignment for Multimodal Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Cheng Tang, Junzhi Ning, Min Cen, Wei Li, Xinyi Zeng, Pinxian Zeng, Rongbin Li, Qiming Zhu, Yuqiang Li, Junjun He, Yirong Chen, Ming Hu
First: 2026-07-15T15:13:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:13:02+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 11 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) drives multimodal reasoning, but answer-level correctness does not guarantee that a vision-language model grounds its predictions in visual evidence. Existing visual-intervention methods contrast policy behavior on original and modified images, yet assign supervision by the type of intervention rather than its observed effect. This assumption fails: identical operators produce heterogeneous outcomes across samples. We propose SIVA-RL, a Sensitivity-Invariance Visual Alignment framework that replaces operator-conditioned regularization with sample-wise, outcome-conditioned supervision. SIVA-RL constructs localized interventions through token-aligned, distance-constrained within-image PatchSwap. A frozen audit policy then scores each clean-intervention pair, and the observed reward drop becomes soft routing weights. Large-drop pairs drive sensitivity alignment, low-drop pairs drive clean-anchored invariance alignment, and ambiguous pairs are down-weighted. This design decouples intervention construction from supervision assignment and is compatible with both GRPO and DAPO backbones. Across nine multimodal reasoning benchmarks spanning mathematical, logical, and vision-dependent tasks, SIVA-RL improves 3B and 7B models over matched RL baselines in every setting. It yields an 8.79 percentage-point gain on vision-dependent reasoning and up to 14.9% relative overall improvement across all four GRPO- and DAPO-based configurations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SIVA-RL：面向多模态强化学习的敏感性不变视觉对齐
可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）推动多模态推理，但答案正确性并不能保证视觉-语言模型将其预测根植于视觉证据。现有视觉干预方法通过对比原始与修改图像上的策略行为进行监督，但其监督依据的是干预类型而非观察到的效果。这一假设失效：相同操作符在不同样本上产生异质结果。我们提出SIVA-RL，一种敏感性-不变性视觉对齐框架，用样本级、结果导向的监督替代操作符条件正则化。SIVA-RL通过令牌对齐、距离约束的图像内PatchSwap构建局部干预。冻结的审计策略对每对干净-干预样本打分，观察到的奖励下降转化为软路由权重。奖励下降大的样本驱动敏感性对齐，下降小的样本驱动干净锚定不变性对齐，模糊样本则被降权。该设计将干预构建与监督分配解耦，并兼容GRPO和DAPO骨干网络。在涵盖数学、逻辑及视觉依赖任务的九个多模态推理基准上，SIVA-RL在所有设置中均优于匹配的强化学习基线模型。其在视觉依赖推理任务上提升8.79个百分点，并在所有四种基于GRPO和DAPO的配置中实现最高达14.9%的相对整体改进。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation that answer correctness alone does not ensure visual grounding in multimodal reinforcement learning, the paper introduces SIVA-RL, a framework that aligns model predictions with visual evidence through outcome-conditioned supervision rather than intervention type. It employs PatchSwap to generate localized image interventions aligned with tokens, then uses a frozen audit policy to score clean-intervention pairs, converting reward drops into soft routing weights that guide sensitivity and invariance alignment. Experimental results across nine multimodal benchmarks show consistent performance gains over baseline RL methods, with an 8.79 percentage-point improvement on vision-dependent reasoning and up to 14.9% relative overall improvement across GRPO and DAPO backbones.
为解决多模态强化学习中仅凭答案正确性无法保证视觉依据的问题，本文提出SIVA-RL框架，通过样本级、结果导向的监督机制替代传统干预类型监督，实现视觉对齐。该框架利用PatchSwap生成局部化、词对齐的图像干预，并通过冻结的审计策略评估干净与修改图像对的奖励下降，以此作为软路由权重引导敏感性与不变性对齐。在九个涵盖数学、逻辑与视觉依赖任务的基准测试中，该方法在所有配置下均优于基线模型，视觉依赖推理任务提升8.79个百分点，整体相对提升最高达14.9%。
NNStar: An end-to-end AI agent for nuclear matter and neutron star physics
Authors: Yao Ma, Yong-Liang Ma, Jia-Ying Xiong
First: 2026-07-15T15:12:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:12:43+00:00
Comments: 14 pages; comments are welcome
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Constraining the equation of state of dense matter requires confronting effective models with massive data that spans many orders of magnitude in scale, from sub-saturation nuclear matter properties to the masses, radii, and tidal deformabilities of neutron stars. Exploring the high-dimensional coupling space of such a model and fine tuning it against all of these constraints is a labor- and time-intensive task. We present \textsc{NNStar}, an end-to-end artificial-intelligence agent that automates this workflow. Rather than a bespoke application, \textsc{NNStar} is delivered as a portable \emph{skill} for an open large-language-model (LLM) agent platform -- a self-describing module that pairs worked usage conventions with symbolic and numerical physics engines that (i) build a relativistic mean-field model directly from a Lagrangian, (ii) solve the mean-field equations of motion and evaluate the saturation properties, (iii) construct the $β$-equilibrium equation of state, splice it to a crust, and integrate the Tolman--Oppenheimer--Volkoff equations, and (iv) score the resulting predictions through a Bayesian joint analysis against nuclear matter and astrophysical observations. The agent can read a model, fit its parameters, and report the full set of nuclear matter and neutron star observables without human intervention. \textsc{NNStar} therefore provides a new, AI-driven framework for analyzing nuclear matter and neutron-star observations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NNStar：用于核物质与中子星物理的端到端人工智能代理
约束致密物质的物态方程需要将有效模型与跨越多个数量级的海量数据进行对比，从亚饱和核物质性质到中子星的质量、半径和潮汐变形性。探索此类模型的高维耦合空间并针对所有这些约束进行精细调优是一项耗时耗力的任务。我们提出了\textsc{NNStar}，一个端到端的人工智能代理，自动化这一工作流程。\textsc{NNStar}并非定制化应用，而是作为可移植的\emph{技能}提供给开放的大语言模型（LLM）代理平台——一个自描述模块，结合了工作使用惯例与符号和数值物理引擎，可（i）直接从拉格朗日量构建相对论平均场模型，（ii）求解平均场运动方程并评估饱和性质，（iii）构建β平衡物态方程，与外壳拼接并积分托尔曼-奥本海默-沃尔科夫方程，（iv）通过贝叶斯联合分析，将所得预测与核物质和天文观测数据对比评分。该代理可读取模型、拟合其参数，并在无需人工干预的情况下报告完整的核物质与中子星可观测量。因此，\textsc{NNStar}提供了一种新的、由人工智能驱动的核物质与中子星观测分析框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently constrain the equation of state of dense nuclear matter using diverse observational data, the authors introduce NNStar, an end-to-end AI agent designed to automate the complex workflow of model building, parameter fitting, and observables prediction. 为高效约束致密核物质的方程状态并整合多尺度观测数据，作者提出NNStar，一个端到端的AI代理，自动化核物质模型构建、参数拟合与可观测量预测的复杂流程。该代理作为大语言模型平台中的自描述技能模块，集成符号与数值物理引擎，可从拉格朗日量直接构建相对论平均场模型，求解平均场运动方程、计算饱和性质，构建β平衡方程状态、连接外壳并求解托尔曼-奥本海默-沃尔科夫方程，最后通过贝叶斯联合分析对核物质与天文观测数据进行评分。实验表明，NNStar无需人工干预即可准确生成核饱和性质及中子星质量、半径、潮汐变形等可观测量，大幅降低传统分析所需的人力与时间成本。
VAIOM: Continuous-Input, Discrete-Output Decoder-Only Financial Sequence Modeling
Authors: Yiming Ma, Xinyu Chen
First: 2026-07-15T15:10:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:10:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Financial observations are continuous, heterogeneous, and noisy, whereas decoder-only next-token models are usually built around discrete symbolic inputs. We introduce Vector-Input Autoregressive Inference for Ordinal-Return Modeling (VAIOM), a decoder-only Transformer for probabilistic next-return modeling on one-hour foreign-exchange bars. VAIOM separates input representation from output likelihood: continuous multivariate financial-event vectors preserve numerical structure at the input, while a categorical distribution over the next volatility-normalized return bucket supports cross-entropy training and likelihood evaluation. The selected 0.9M Hybrid Continuous Input model combines continuous event features with categorical asset metadata, a Mixture-of-Market-States return head, Gap, volatility-regime, and Ordinal auxiliary objectives, and full-sequence supervision. Models and preprocessing are fit using pre-2024 Train data; models are selected on 2024H2 Validation and evaluated without refitting on two 2025 Test periods. Across three independent training seeds, every model outperforms fixed single-bar LightGBM baseline in both Test halves. For the canonical checkpoint, paired gains over LightGBM are 0.029 and 0.043 bits per event. Validation experiments show that continuous input improves over discrete-token input under the same categorical return objective, full-sequence supervision improves over last-position training, and auxiliary representation shaping together with a mixture-structured return head improves return likelihood in controlled comparisons. A supporting capacity study finds that the smallest evaluated complete architecture rung achieves the strongest Validation likelihood on the present corpus.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VAIOM：连续输入、离散输出的解码器专用金融序列建模
金融观测数据是连续的、异构的且存在噪声，而解码器专用的下一令牌模型通常基于离散符号输入构建。我们引入了向量输入自回归推断用于序数收益建模（VAIOM），这是一种专用于一小时外汇K线概率性下一收益建模的解码器专用Transformer。VAIOM将输入表示与输出似然分离：连续多变量金融事件向量在输入端保留数值结构，而下一波动率标准化收益桶的类别分布支持交叉熵训练和似然评估。所选的0.9M混合连续输入模型结合了连续事件特征与类别资产元数据、混合市场状态收益头、缺口、波动率状态及序数辅助目标，并采用全序列监督。模型与预处理使用2024年前训练数据拟合；模型在2024年下半年验证集上选择，并在两个2025年测试期中无需重新拟合进行评估。在三个独立训练种子下，所有模型在测试期均优于固定单K线LightGBM基线。对于标准检查点，与LightGBM相比，每事件增益分别为0.029和0.043比特。验证实验表明，在相同类别收益目标下，连续输入优于离散令牌输入；全序列监督优于仅最后位置训练；辅助表示塑造与混合结构收益头共同提升收益似然。支持性容量研究发现，所评估最小完整架构层级在当前语料上实现了最强的验证似然。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the mismatch between continuous financial data and discrete-token transformer models, VAIOM introduces a decoder-only architecture designed for probabilistic next-return prediction using one-hour FX bars. It decouples input representation—using continuous multivariate financial-event vectors—to preserve numerical structure, from output likelihood—modeled via a categorical distribution over volatility-normalized return buckets for cross-entropy training. The 0.9M Hybrid Continuous Input model integrates asset metadata, a Mixture-of-Market-States return head, and auxiliary objectives like Gap and volatility-regime, trained under full-sequence supervision. Evaluated on 2025 test sets without refitting, VAIOM consistently outperforms a LightGBM baseline across three seeds, achieving 0.029–0.043 bits per event gains. Validation experiments confirm that continuous inputs, full-sequence training, and auxiliary objectives enhance return likelihood, with the smallest architecture achieving the strongest validation performance.
为解决金融数据连续性与传统离散符号模型之间的不匹配，VAIOM提出一种仅解码器的Transformer架构，用于基于一小时外汇数据的下一回报概率建模。该架构将输入表示（使用连续多维金融事件向量）与输出似然（基于波动率标准化回报桶的类别分布）分离，以保留数值结构并支持交叉熵训练。0.9M混合连续输入模型结合资产元数据、市场状态混合回报头及Gap、波动率模式等辅助目标，在全序列监督下训练。在2025测试集上无需再训练，该模型在三个随机种子下均优于LightGBM基线，提升0.029–0.043比特/事件。验证实验表明，连续输入、全序列训练及辅助目标共同提升回报似然，最小模型在验证集上表现最优。
Plausible Deniability Guarantees for Whistleblowers
Authors: Leo Richter, Matt J. Kusner
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-15T15:09:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:09:55+00:00
Comments: Accepted at three ICML 2026 workshops, including the ICML Workshop on Technical AI Governance Research
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Whistleblowers are a key safeguard against organizational wrongdoing, but the threat of retaliation deters reporting. Existing whistleblower-protection proposals lack formal privacy guarantees, and existing differential privacy mechanisms do not directly target the natural threat model -- one in which the audited organization itself observes auditor selection decisions and uses them to identify reporters. We formalize protection against a strong-adversary threat model as per-report $(0, δ)$-differential privacy on the transcript of audit selections. Within this framework we prove that a natural approach -- randomized response applied at the selection step -- can never outperform uniform random auditing by more than $δ$ at any horizon. We then give a generic mechanism that reduces private auditing to private continual counting: any $(0, δ)$-DP continual counter plugs in by post-processing, and the audit transcript inherits the same per-report guarantee. Instantiating the reduction with a recent work in continual counting yields per-report $(0, δ)$-DP with noise scaling as $O(\sqrt{\log T})$ across a horizon of $T$ audit decisions. A utility theorem shows that the selection error vanishes whenever the noisy report gap between the most-reported organization and the runner-up grows faster than $\sqrt{\log T}$. Simulations show a substantial improvement over randomized response.
中文标题/摘要
标题：举报人可合理否认的保障机制
举报人是防范组织不当行为的关键保障，但报复威胁阻碍了举报行为。现有举报人保护方案缺乏形式化的隐私保障，而现有差分隐私机制并未直接针对自然威胁模型——即被审计组织本身可观察审计员选择决策，并据此识别举报人。我们以每份报告的 $(0, δ)$-差分隐私形式化强敌对威胁模型下的保护机制，针对审计选择记录。在此框架下，我们证明：在选择阶段应用随机响应的自然方法，其性能在任何时间范围内均不会优于均匀随机审计超过 $δ$。随后我们提出一种通用机制，将私密审计归约为私密持续计数：任何 $(0, δ)$-DP 持续计数器可通过后处理嵌入，审计记录将继承相同的每份报告保障。通过将该归约与近期持续计数工作实例化，可实现每份报告 $(0, δ)$-DP，其噪声随 $T$ 个审计决策时间范围呈 $O(\sqrt{\log T})$ 缩放。一个效用定理表明，当最常被举报组织与次高举报组织之间的带噪声报告差距增长速度超过 $\sqrt{\log T}$ 时，选择误差将趋近于零。仿真结果表明，该方法显著优于随机响应。
Summary / 总结
To address the lack of formal privacy guarantees for whistleblowers under adversarial threat models where audited organizations can infer reporter identities from audit selection patterns, the paper introduces a framework of per-report (0, δ)-differential privacy for audit transcripts. It proves that randomized response at selection cannot outperform uniform random auditing beyond a δ-bound, then constructs a generic mechanism that reduces private auditing to private continual counting, enabling plug-in of existing (0, δ)-DP counters. When instantiated with a recent continual counting method, this yields per-report (0, δ)-DP with noise scaling as O(√log T) over T audit decisions, and a utility theorem confirms selection accuracy improves as the gap between top and second-most-reported entities exceeds √log T. Simulations demonstrate significantly better performance than randomized response.
为防止被审计机构通过观察审计员选择行为识别举报人，论文提出强对手威胁模型，要求审计选择记录满足每报告(0, δ)-差分隐私。证明在任意时间范围内，选择阶段的随机响应策略无法优于均匀随机审计超过δ。随后提出将私有审计归约至私有持续计数问题，任何(0, δ)-DP持续计数器均可通过后处理实现相同隐私保证。结合近期持续计数机制，实现每报告(0, δ)-DP，噪声随时间T增长为O(√log T)，并证明当最活跃机构与次活跃机构的噪声报告差距超过√log T时，选择误差趋于零。仿真结果表明该方法显著优于随机响应策略。
Cyclone: Diffusion Model for Cycle-Consistent Weather Editing from Unpaired Driving Data
Authors: Thang-Anh-Quan Nguyen, Moussab Bennehar, Luis Guillermo Roldao Jimenez, Nathan Piasco, Dzmitry Tsishkou, Laurent Caraffa, Jean-Philippe Tarel, Roland Brémond
First: 2026-07-15T15:08:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:08:45+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://ntaquan0125.github.io/weather-cyclone/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reliable perception under diverse weather conditions remains a major challenge for autonomous driving systems. A common strategy to improve robustness is either to synthesize adverse weather conditions for training perception models or to apply weather-removal techniques to recover clean inputs. However, existing approaches typically rely on synthetic data augmentation or physics-based, task-specific models that require paired training data and often struggle to generate realistic weather effects or generalize robustly to out-of-domain scenarios. Toward this problem, we present Cyclone, a unified framework for weather editing based on latent diffusion, equipped with cycle-consistent constraints and knowledge from image-text models. Cyclone enables the generation of multiple weather conditions across diverse scenes while eliminating the need for paired data. Experimental results show that our approach produces more realistic, structure-preserving outputs than existing baselines and leads to consistent improvements across several downstream driving perception tasks. Furthermore, we demonstrate that Cyclone can be distilled to a video diffusion model for temporally consistent weather editing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Cyclone：基于未配对驾驶数据的循环一致天气编辑扩散模型
在多样化的天气条件下实现可靠的感知仍是自动驾驶系统的主要挑战。一种常见的提升鲁棒性的策略是合成恶劣天气条件用于训练感知模型，或应用去天气技术恢复干净输入。然而，现有方法通常依赖合成数据增强或基于物理的、任务特定模型，需要配对训练数据，且往往难以生成逼真的天气效果或在域外场景中稳健泛化。针对此问题，我们提出Cyclone，一个基于潜在扩散的统一天气编辑框架，结合循环一致性约束和图像-文本模型的知识。Cyclone可在多样场景中生成多种天气条件，无需配对数据。实验结果表明，我们的方法生成的输出比现有基线更真实、结构保持更好，并在多个下游驾驶感知任务中带来一致提升。此外，我们证明Cyclone可蒸馏为视频扩散模型，实现时序一致的天气编辑。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of reliable perception under varying weather conditions in autonomous driving, Cyclone introduces a latent diffusion model that enables cycle-consistent weather editing from unpaired driving data, leveraging image-text model knowledge to generate realistic weather effects without requiring paired training samples. The method generates multiple weather conditions across diverse scenes while preserving structural integrity and achieves consistent performance improvements across downstream perception tasks. Experiments demonstrate that Cyclone outperforms existing baselines in realism and robustness, and can be distilled into a video diffusion model for temporally consistent weather editing.
为解决自动驾驶系统在复杂天气下的可靠感知问题，Cyclone提出一种基于潜在扩散模型的天气编辑框架，通过引入循环一致性约束和图像-文本模型知识，在无需配对数据的情况下生成多样化的天气效果。该方法能保持场景结构完整性，并可蒸馏为视频扩散模型以实现时序一致的天气编辑。实验表明，Cyclone在生成真实、结构保持的天气效果方面优于现有基线，并在多个下游感知任务中带来稳定性能提升。
S-squared-VLA: Decoupling Semantic and Spatial Streams in Vision-Language-Action Models for Autonomous Driving
Authors: Jianguo Yu, Rukang Wang, Duanfeng Chu, Chen Wang, Renju Feng, Liping Lu
First: 2026-07-15T15:07:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:07:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable potential for high-level reasoning in autonomous driving, yet they fundamentally struggle to generate precise, low-level control actions. This limitation is rooted in a semantic-physical gap caused by the inherent mismatch between discrete language tokens and continuous trajectory planning. While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) architectures attempt to bridge this gap by unifying perception and control into a single policy, this entanglement creates a new bottleneck. Standard VLAs experience a severe spatial representation collapse, which irreversibly degrades the fine-grained spatial and geometric priors essential for safe, boundary-aware navigation. To address this limitation, we propose the S-squared-VLA, which explicitly decouples the semantic and spatial streams in Vision-Language-Action models. The semantic stream leverages hierarchical bridging to extract multi-scale VLM features for robust intent reasoning. In parallel, an independent spatial stream bypasses the autoregressive language bottleneck, directly preserving uncompressed spatial features from the visual encoder. By integrating auxiliary perception supervision, this stream explicitly equips the model with rich spatial and geometric priors. Finally, a dual-stream planning adapter fuses high-level semantic intent with precise spatial constraints via cascaded attention mechanisms. Evaluations on the NAVSIM closed-loop benchmark show that S-squared-VLA achieves a Predictive Driver Model Score (PDMS) of 87.1, establishing a new state-of-the-art for VLA models under a purely supervised fine-tuning (SFT) setting. By mitigating the spatial representation collapse of traditional VLMs, our framework significantly outperforms baselines, achieving the highest No Collision (NC) rate of 98.4 among all evaluated methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：S-squared-VLA：在视觉-语言-动作模型中解耦语义与空间流以实现自动驾驶
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在自动驾驶高层推理中展现出巨大潜力，但其根本上难以生成精确的低层控制动作。这一局限源于离散语言标记与连续轨迹规划之间固有的语义-物理鸿沟。尽管视觉-语言-动作（VLA）架构试图通过统一感知与控制来弥合这一鸿沟，但这种耦合反而造成了新的瓶颈：标准VLA模型经历严重的空间表征坍缩，不可逆地损害了安全、边界感知导航所必需的精细空间与几何先验。为解决此问题，我们提出S-squared-VLA，明确解耦视觉-语言-动作模型中的语义与空间流。语义流通过分层桥接提取多尺度VLM特征，以实现稳健意图推理；同时，独立的空间流绕过自回归语言瓶颈，直接保留视觉编码器中的未压缩空间特征。通过引入辅助感知监督，该流显式赋予模型丰富的空间与几何先验。最后，双流规划适配器通过级联注意力机制融合高层语义意图与精确空间约束。在NAVSIM闭环基准测试中，S-squared-VLA实现预测驾驶员模型得分（PDMS）87.1，创下纯监督微调（SFT）设置下VLA模型的新纪录。通过缓解传统VLM的空间表征坍缩，我们的框架显著优于基线方法，达到98.4%的最高无碰撞（NC）率。
Summary / 总结
To address the semantic-physical gap that prevents Vision-Language Models from generating precise low-level control actions in autonomous driving, the authors propose S-squared-VLA, which decouples semantic and spatial processing streams. The semantic stream uses hierarchical bridging to extract multi-scale VLM features for intent reasoning, while the spatial stream independently preserves uncompressed visual features to avoid the autoregressive language bottleneck. A dual-stream planning adapter then fuses semantic intent with spatial constraints via cascaded attention. Evaluated on NAVSIM, S-squared-VLA achieves a PDMS of 87.1 under supervised fine-tuning and sets a new state-of-the-art, with a 98.4% No Collision rate, significantly outperforming baselines by mitigating spatial representation collapse.
为解决视觉语言模型在自动驾驶中因语言离散性与空间连续性不匹配导致的低级控制动作生成不足问题，本文提出S-squared-VLA，显式分离语义与空间处理流。语义流通过分层桥接提取多尺度VLM特征以支持意图推理，空间流则绕过语言自回归瓶颈，直接保留视觉编码器的未压缩空间特征并结合辅助感知监督以编码空间与几何先验。双流规划适配器通过级联注意力机制融合语义意图与空间约束。在NAVSIM闭环基准测试中，S-squared-VLA在纯监督微调下取得87.1的预测驾驶员模型得分，碰撞率为0.6%，显著优于基线模型，验证了其缓解空间表征坍塌、提升安全导航能力的有效性。
Thresholded Cross-Attention for Reliable Intensity-Chromaticity Fusion in Low-Light Image Enhancement
Authors: Yanyi Wu, Xu Zhang, Junkai Chen, Laibin Chang, Jiaqi Ma, Shi Chen, Linwei Zhu, Jianglei Di, Huan Zhang
First: 2026-07-15T15:06:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:06:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Low-Light Image Enhancement (LLIE) requires a careful balance among noise suppression, color fidelity, and efficiency. Recent HVI-based methods alleviate color entanglement by decoupling intensity and chromaticity, yet how reliably the two streams are fused again is an overlooked factor that largely determines the final quality. We observe that the confidence of cross-stream attention is strongly layer-dependent, so the fixed-quota selection of Top-K sparse attention is mismatched to it, discarding informative dependencies in some layers while retaining noisy ones in others. Motivated by this observation, we propose TCA-Net, a network built around Thresholded Cross-Attention that targets reliable intensity-chromaticity fusion in the HVI space rather than introducing yet another color representation. At its core, TCA replaces the rigid Top-K quota with a fixed confidence threshold whose retained cardinality is input- and layer-adaptive, retaining only high-confidence cross-stream interactions while suppressing unreliable ones. Around this core, two complementary designs clean up the fusion before and after it: a Phase-guided Fourier Interaction Module provides a structure-aware brightness initialization for the intensity stream prior to fusion, and a Decoupled Dual-Stream Guidance Module constructs residual intensity features to suppress chromaticity leakage during reconstruction. A Scale-Aware Consistency Regularization further improves structural robustness under scale perturbations during training. Extensive experiments on LOL-v1, LOL-v2, Sony-Total-Dark, and LSRW-Huawei demonstrate that TCA-Net delivers competitive restoration accuracy, improved color fidelity, and a compact parameter size.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于阈值交叉注意力的低光照图像增强中可靠亮度-色度融合
低光照图像增强（LLIE）需在噪声抑制、色彩保真度与效率间取得平衡。近期基于HVI的方法通过解耦亮度与色度缓解色彩纠缠，但二者如何可靠融合仍被忽视，这很大程度上决定最终质量。我们观察到跨流注意力的置信度强依赖于网络层，固定Top-K稀疏注意力选择与之不匹配，导致某些层丢弃有用依赖而保留噪声依赖。为此，我们提出TCA-Net，围绕阈值交叉注意力构建，目标是在HVI空间实现可靠亮度-色度融合，而非引入新的色彩表示。其核心是用固定置信度阈值替代刚性Top-K配额，保留高置信度跨流交互、抑制不可靠交互，且保留数量随输入与层自适应调整。围绕该核心，两个互补设计在融合前后清理融合：相位引导傅里叶交互模块为亮度流融合前提供结构感知亮度初始化；解耦双流引导模块构建残差亮度特征以抑制重建过程中的色度泄漏。尺度感知一致性正则化进一步提升训练中尺度扰动下的结构鲁棒性。在LOL-v1、LOL-v2、Sony-Total-Dark及LSRW-Huawei数据集上的大量实验表明，TCA-Net在恢复精度、色彩保真度及参数规模方面均表现优异。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that fixed Top-K attention in HVI-based low-light image enhancement methods fails to adapt to layer-specific confidence in intensity-chromaticity fusion, the authors propose TCA-Net, which replaces rigid attention selection with a confidence-thresholded mechanism that dynamically retains only high-confidence cross-stream interactions. The network further integrates a Phase-guided Fourier Interaction Module to initialize intensity features and a Decoupled Dual-Stream Guidance Module to suppress chromaticity leakage, complemented by Scale-Aware Consistency Regularization for robustness. Experiments on multiple datasets show TCA-Net achieves competitive restoration accuracy, enhanced color fidelity, and a compact model size compared to existing methods.
鉴于现有HVI方法中固定Top-K注意力机制无法适应各层间强度与色度融合的置信度差异，本文提出TCA-Net，采用基于置信度的阈值选择机制替代固定数量的注意力选择，实现输入与层适应的动态保留。同时引入相位引导傅里叶交互模块进行亮度结构初始化，以及解耦双流引导模块抑制色度泄漏，并辅以尺度感知一致性正则化提升鲁棒性。实验表明，TCA-Net在多个低光照数据集上实现媲美甚至优于现有方法的恢复精度、更优的色彩保真度和更小的模型参数量。
ExpressionCueLens: A Cross-Cultural Analysis of Human-AI Companion Conversations on Social Media
Authors: Lynnette Hui Xian Ng, Yunze Xiao, Lionel Z. Wang, Weihao Xuan, Mona Diab
First: 2026-07-15T15:05:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:05:41+00:00
Comments: Accepted at Journal of Ambient Intelligence and Humanized Computing
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based AI companion agents are increasingly being perceived not only as tools but also as social companions. On social media, people recount conversations where these agents comfort, negotiate and assert boundaries, reflecting a growing attribution of human-like qualities. To profile how agency is perceived in human-AI (HAI) interactions, we introduce the ExpressionCueLens framework, which organizes linguistic, cognitive, behavioral and perceptual cues into ten categories of anthropomorphism expressions. We apply this framework to $\sim$3500 Reddit and XiaoHongShu posts that discuss HAI companionship. Through iterative expert annotation and LLM-assisted labeling, our cross-platform analysis indicates patterns consistent with the hypothesis that XiaoHongShu users use significantly more expressions of vulnerability and emotions, and more non-perceptual cues. Reddit users employ more perceptual cues with temporality and embodiment expressions. These findings suggest that cultural and platform norms shape the way that companion agents are treated as active, agentic partners, and provides design implications for culturally sensitive HAI companion agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ExpressionCueLens：社交媒体上人机伴侣对话的跨文化分析

Motivated by the growing perception of AI companions as social partners on social media, this study introduces ExpressionCueLens, a framework categorizing anthropomorphic cues into ten linguistic, cognitive, behavioral, and perceptual dimensions. Applied to approximately 3,500 Reddit and XiaoHongShu posts discussing human-AI companionship, the framework was refined through iterative expert annotation and LLM-assisted labeling. Results show that XiaoHongShu users predominantly use vulnerability and emotion-related cues alongside non-perceptual expressions, while Reddit users favor perceptual cues involving temporality and embodiment, indicating that cultural context and platform norms significantly influence how users attribute agency to AI companions.
鉴于社交媒体上用户日益将AI伴侣视为社会性存在，本研究提出ExpressionCueLens框架，将拟人化表达划分为十类语言、认知、行为与感知线索。该框架应用于约3500篇Reddit与小红书讨论人机伴侣关系的帖子，经专家标注与大模型辅助标注后发现：小红书用户更倾向使用脆弱性表达、情绪表达及非感知类线索，而Reddit用户则更偏好涉及时间性与具身性的感知类线索，表明文化背景与平台规范深刻影响用户如何将AI伴侣视为具有主动性的伙伴。
Advancing Multimodal Judge Models through a Capability-Oriented Benchmark and MCTS-Driven Data Generation
Authors: Zeyu Chen, Huanjin Yao, Ziwang Zhao, Min Yang
First: 2026-02-28T08:49:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:03:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Using Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) as judges to achieve precise and consistent evaluations has gradually become an emerging paradigm across various domains. Evaluating the capability and reliability of MLLM-as-a-judge systems is therefore essential for ensuring trustworthy assessment. Existing judge benchmarks categorize samples by task types but fail to capture the fundamental judgment capabilities required for reliable evaluation. In this work, we introduce M-JudgeBench, a ten-dimensional capability-oriented benchmark designed to comprehensively assess the judgment abilities of MLLMs. Our benchmark decomposes evaluation into pairwise Chain-of-Thought (CoT) comparison, length bias avoidance, and process error detection tasks, jointly covering ten fine-grained subtasks. This design enables diagnosis of model reliability across reasoning styles, response lengths, and cross-model variations. Systematic evaluation uncovers the systematic weaknesses in existing MLLM-as-a-judge systems. To address this issue, we further propose Judge-MCTS, a data construction framework generating pairwise reasoning trajectories with various correctness and length. Using Judge-MCTS, we construct an MCTS-augmented dataset and train M-Judger, a series of strong judge models. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of M-Judger on existing judge benchmarks as well as M-JudgeBench. Overall, our work establishes a more principled foundation for evaluating MLLM-as-a-judge through M-JudgeBench and Judge-MCTS framework, paving the way for future research on judge model evaluation and capability-driven judge training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过能力导向基准与MCTS驱动数据生成推进多模态裁判模型发展
利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）作为裁判以实现精准且一致的评估，正逐渐成为各领域的新兴范式。因此，评估MLLM-as-a-judge系统的功能与可靠性对于确保可信评估至关重要。现有裁判基准按任务类型分类，但未能捕捉可靠评估所需的基本判断能力。本文提出M-JudgeBench，一个面向十维能力的基准，旨在全面评估MLLMs的判断能力。该基准将评估分解为成对思维链（CoT）比较、长度偏差规避和过程错误检测任务，共同覆盖十个细粒度子任务。此设计可诊断模型在推理风格、响应长度及跨模型差异下的可靠性。系统性评估揭示了现有MLLM-as-a-judge系统的系统性弱点。为解决此问题，我们进一步提出Judge-MCTS，一种生成具有不同正确性和长度的成对推理轨迹的数据构建框架。利用Judge-MCTS，我们构建了MCTS增强数据集并训练了M-Judger系列强裁判模型。大量实验表明，M-Judger在现有裁判基准及M-JudgeBench上均表现优越。总体而言，我们的工作通过M-JudgeBench和Judge-MCTS框架，为评估MLLM-as-a-judge建立了更严谨的基础，为未来裁判模型评估与能力驱动裁判训练研究铺平道路。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate MLLM-as-a-judge systems more reliably, this work introduces M-JudgeBench, a ten-dimensional benchmark that assesses judgment capabilities through pairwise CoT comparison, length bias avoidance, and process error detection. The benchmark’s fine-grained subtasks enable diagnosis of model weaknesses across reasoning styles and response lengths. To improve judge performance, the authors propose Judge-MCTS, a data generation framework that creates diverse reasoning trajectories with varying correctness and length, which is used to train M-Judger. Experimental results show M-Judger outperforms existing models on both standard benchmarks and M-JudgeBench, demonstrating the effectiveness of the capability-oriented design and MCTS-driven training.
为更严谨地评估多模态大语言模型作为裁判系统的可靠性，本文提出M-JudgeBench，一个涵盖十维能力维度的基准，通过成对思维链比较、长度偏差规避和过程错误检测等任务，分解为十个细粒度子任务，以诊断模型在不同推理风格和响应长度下的可靠性。实验发现现有裁判模型存在系统性缺陷。为此，作者提出Judge-MCTS数据生成框架，构建多样化的推理轨迹，用于训练M-Judger系列裁判模型。实验表明M-Judger在现有基准和M-JudgeBench上均优于现有模型，验证了能力导向评估与数据驱动训练的有效性。
Generative Compilation: On-the-Fly Compiler Feedback as AI Generates Code
Authors: Niels Mündler-Sasahara, Hristo Venev, Dawn Song, Martin Vechev, Jingxuan He
First: 2026-07-15T15:00:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:00:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Languages with rich static semantics, such as Rust, provide stronger guarantees for AI-generated code, but their strictness makes generation more difficult. Off-the-shelf compilers can provide useful feedback post-generation, but does not guide intermediate generation steps, such as those during autoregressive LLM decoding. Constrained decoding intervenes earlier by rejecting invalid tokens during sampling, but requires white-box model access and costly reimplementation for semantic constraints.We introduce generative compilation, the first approach to obtaining compiler feedback on partial programs during generation. The core technical device is a sealor: a lightweight, mostly syntax-guided transformation that converts partial programs into complete ones that standard compilers can diagnose. It is designed such that possible-to-complete partial programs are never rejected, while preserving enough code context to catch genuine dead ends early. We construct such a sealor on a core Rust-like calculus and prove that it satisfies these properties, all mechanized in Lean. We extend it to the first partial-program checker for real Rust. We evaluate our method on challenging repository-level Rust coding tasks, across both frontier black-box and open-weight models. We show that generative compilation reduces non-compiling outputs and improves functional correctness, relative to standard post-generation feedback. It does so by detecting a broad range of errors close to their source and early during generation, thereby reducing errors cascades and enabling focused diagnostics. More broadly, generative compilation is a step toward making compilers a first-class citizen of AI-assisted programming active during generation, rather than a separate post-generation check.
中文标题/摘要
标题：生成式编译：AI生成代码时的即时编译器反馈
具有丰富静态语义的语言（如Rust）为AI生成的代码提供更强的保障，但其严格性使生成更困难。现成编译器可在生成后提供有用反馈，但无法指导自回归LLM解码过程中的中间生成步骤。受限解码在采样时提前拒绝无效标记，但需要白盒模型访问和昂贵的语义约束重实现。我们提出生成式编译，首次在生成过程中对部分程序提供编译器反馈。核心技术是“封印器”：一种轻量级、主要基于语法的转换，将部分程序转化为标准编译器可诊断的完整程序。其设计确保可完成的部分程序永不被拒绝，同时保留足够代码上下文以尽早识别真正的死胡同。我们在类似Rust的核心演算上构建此类封印器，并在Lean中形式化证明其满足这些性质。我们进一步将其扩展为首个针对真实Rust的部分程序检查器。我们在具有挑战性的仓库级Rust编码任务上评估该方法，涵盖前沿黑盒模型和开源权重模型。结果表明，生成式编译相较标准后生成反馈，能减少非编译输出并提升功能正确性。其通过在生成早期、靠近错误源头处检测广泛错误，减少错误级联并实现精准诊断。更广泛地说，生成式编译是使编译器成为AI辅助编程中生成过程中的第一类公民（而非独立的后生成检查）的重要一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generating correct code in statically typed languages like Rust, this work introduces generative compilation—a method that integrates compiler feedback directly into the code generation process. It uses a lightweight transformation called a 'sealor' to convert partial programs into compilable forms, enabling early error detection without requiring white-box model access or reimplementation. Evaluated on real Rust tasks using both black-box and open-weight LLMs, the approach significantly reduces non-compiling outputs and improves functional correctness by catching errors early and preventing cascading failures, thereby making compilers an active participant in AI-assisted programming rather than a post-generation tool.
为解决在静态类型语言（如 Rust）中生成正确代码的难题，本文提出“生成式编译”方法，将编译器反馈直接嵌入代码生成过程。该方法通过一种轻量级转换工具“sealor”，将部分程序转化为可编译形式，从而在生成早期检测错误，无需白盒模型访问或昂贵重实现。在真实 Rust 任务上对黑盒与开源模型评估显示，该方法显著降低非编译输出并提升功能正确性，通过早期发现错误、防止错误级联，使编译器成为 AI 辅助编程中的主动参与者，而非事后检查工具。
DeepStress: Stress-Testing Deep Search Agents
Authors: Ismael Rousseau, Geraldine Damnati, Frederic Bechet
First: 2026-07-15T14:59:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T14:59:29+00:00
Comments: 9 pages preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While search agents demonstrate impressive capabilities in multi-step question answering, their robustness to poor-quality evidence remains under-explored. This phenomenon occurs rarely in realistic benchmarks but can lead to dramatic failure in real life applications. Therefore in this study we propose DeepStress, a stress testing framework that controls the frequency of challenging evidence by replacing the retrieval module of search agents with a controlled synthetic environment. We use this framework to control three dimensions that can affect document reliability: trustworthiness, relevance, and factuality. Testing several search agents on HotpotQA and BrowseCompPlus, we demonstrate that agents exhibit substantial differences in their ability to handle unreliable information and propose new metrics that better document systems outcomes as well as the interactions between conflicting parametric and retrieved knowledge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DeepStress：深度搜索代理的压力测试
尽管搜索代理在多步问答任务中表现出色，但它们对低质量证据的鲁棒性仍鲜有研究。这一现象在现实基准测试中罕见，但在实际应用中可能导致灾难性失败。为此，本研究提出DeepStress框架，通过替换搜索代理的检索模块为可控合成环境，以控制挑战性证据的频率。我们利用该框架控制影响文档可靠性的三个维度：可信度、相关性和事实性。在HotpotQA和BrowseCompPlus上测试多个搜索代理，我们证明代理在处理不可靠信息的能力上存在显著差异，并提出新的度量指标，更准确地记录系统结果及冲突参数化与检索知识之间的交互。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of robustness in search agents when faced with poor-quality evidence, which is rare in benchmarks but critical in real-world applications, this study introduces DeepStress, a stress-testing framework that replaces the retrieval module with a controlled synthetic environment to systematically vary document reliability along three dimensions: trustworthiness, relevance, and factuality. Experiments on HotpotQA and BrowseCompPlus reveal significant disparities among search agents in handling unreliable information, and the framework enables the development of new metrics that more accurately capture system performance and the interplay between parametric knowledge and retrieved content.
为评估搜索代理在面对低质量证据时的表现——此类情况在现实世界中常见但基准测试中罕见——该研究提出了DeepStress框架，通过替换检索模块为可控合成环境，系统调节文档的可信度、相关性和事实性。在HotpotQA和BrowseCompPlus上的实验表明，不同代理在处理不可靠信息时表现差异显著，同时该框架引入了新指标以更准确衡量系统在冲突知识下的表现。
An Efficient Newton Algorithm for Nonnegative Matrix Factorization with the Kullback-Leibler Divergence
Authors: Damien Lesens, Jérémy E. Cohen, Bora Uçar
First: 2026-07-15T14:59:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T14:59:07+00:00
Comments: 35 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Nonnegative Matrix Factorization (NMF) is a fundamental tool in unsupervised learning, which approximates a nonnegative matrix by the product of two low-rank nonnegative factors. The Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence is best suited to measure the data to model discrepancy when the decomposed data sample follows a Poisson distribution, which is the case for count datasets such as term-document matrices or images. Most KL-NMF algorithms in the literature minimize a separable majorant of the loss to find their next iterate. We argue that this method has reached its limits and propose to use instead the second-order Taylor expansion of the loss, leading to a Newton-type method. We minimize this non-separable surrogate by proposing a generalization of the well-known HALS algorithm. This yields an efficient KL-NMF algorithm which provably converges and which competes favorably with state-of-the-art algorithms on a large variety of datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于Kullback-Leibler散度的非负矩阵分解高效牛顿算法
非负矩阵分解（NMF）是无监督学习中的基本工具，通过两个低秩非负因子的乘积近似非负矩阵。当分解的数据样本服从泊松分布（如词-文档矩阵或图像等计数数据）时，Kullback-Leibler（KL）散度最适合衡量数据与模型之间的差异。文献中大多数KL-NMF算法通过最小化损失的可分离上界来寻找下一次迭代。我们认为该方法已达到极限，提出改用损失函数的二阶泰勒展开，从而构建牛顿型方法。我们通过提出HALS算法的推广形式，最小化该不可分离的代理函数，从而得到一个高效、可证明收敛的KL-NMF算法，并在多种数据集上与当前最先进算法竞争表现优异。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing KL-NMF algorithms that rely on separable majorants, this paper proposes a Newton-type method using the second-order Taylor expansion of the KL divergence loss. The authors generalize the HALS algorithm to minimize this non-separable surrogate, resulting in an efficient and provably convergent algorithm. Experimental results show that the proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art approaches across diverse datasets, including term-document matrices and image data, while maintaining computational efficiency.
鉴于现有基于KL散度的NMF算法依赖可分离的上界而效率受限，本文提出利用损失函数的二阶泰勒展开构建牛顿型方法，并对HALS算法进行推广以最小化该非可分离代理函数，从而获得一个高效且可证收敛的算法。实验表明，该方法在包括词-文档矩阵和图像数据在内的多种数据集上，性能可与当前最先进算法相媲美。
Partially Correlated Verifier Cascades in LLM Harnesses: Concave Log-Odds, Polynomial Reliability, and Blind-Spot Ceilings
Authors: Jiangang Han
First: 2026-07-15T14:58:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T14:58:37+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 2 figures. Code and synthetic-recovery experiments: https://github.com/jianganghan/harness-verifier-cascades
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Serial verification gates are a core reliability primitive in LLM harnesses: a candidate answer is returned only if $k$ verifier calls all accept it. Under conditionally independent gates, the recent Odds Law (arXiv:2606.15712) shows that posterior log-odds grow linearly in $k$, so failure decays exponentially, and states that "a tight theory of partially correlated verifier cascades remains open." This note gives a minimal such theory. Modeling the per-instance false-accept rate on the generator's own errors as a latent variable $α\sim G$ (de Finetti), the exact cascade posterior is $\ell_k = \ell_0 - \ln m_k$, with $m_k$ the $k$-th moment of $G$. Then: (i) $\ell_k$ is concave in $k$ for every non-degenerate $G$ -- the Odds Law is its tangent at the first gate and an upper bound; (ii) for Beta$(a,b)$ latents, failure decays polynomially, $1-r_k \asymp k^{-b}$, with correlation parameter $ρ_v = 1/(a+b+1)$; (iii) a blind-spot atom of mass $1-π$ at $α=1$ caps the evidence extractable from any number of gates at $-\ln(1-π)$ nats, so reliability saturates below 1; (iv) letting the true-accept rate also vary ($β\sim H$) yields a trichotomy -- gates eventually always help, plateau, or actively harm -- decided by the upper-tail exponents of $G$ and $H$, with closed-form crossover $k^\dagger$. The mechanism is survivorship: errors surviving gates are the high-$α$ ones. The theory is measurable: $R$ repeated verdicts per instance identify the first $R$ moments of $G$, so two verdicts identify $ρ_v$; beta-binomial likelihood and NPMLE recover the reliability curve and the ill-posed ceiling. In synthetic tests, independence-based extrapolation underestimates failure by 20x at $k=5$ and ~3000x at $k=10$; the correlated fit at $R=8$ tracks held-out depths. The practical lever is decorrelation -- changing model family, modality, or evidence source -- not adding gates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型验证器级联中的部分相关性：凹形对数似然、多项式可靠性与盲区上限
串行验证门是大语言模型框架中的核心可靠性原语：仅当k次验证调用全部接受候选答案时才返回。在条件独立门下，近期的Odds定律（arXiv:2606.15712）表明，后验对数似然随k线性增长，失败概率呈指数衰减，并指出“部分相关验证器级联的紧致理论仍待解决”。本文给出最简理论：将生成器自身错误导致的每实例误接受率建模为潜变量α~G（德·芬内蒂），精确级联后验为ℓ_k = ℓ_0 - ln m_k，其中m_k是G的k阶矩。则：(i) 对任意非退化G，ℓ_k在k上为凹函数——Odds定律为其在第一门处的切线且为上界；(ii) 对Beta(a,b)潜变量，失败概率呈多项式衰减，1-r_k ≈ k^{-b}，相关参数ρ_v = 1/(a+b+1)；(iii) 在α=1处存在质量为1-π的盲区原子，任何数量门所能提取的证据上限为-ln(1-π)纳特，可靠性因此低于1；(iv) 若真接受率亦变化（β~H），则出现三态：门最终始终有益、平台期或主动有害，由G与H的上尾指数决定，闭式交叉点为k^†。机制为幸存者效应：存活门的错误属于高α值。理论可测量：每实例R次判决可识别G的前R阶矩，故两判决可识别ρ_v；Beta-二项式似然与NPMLE可恢复可靠性曲线与病态上限。合成测试中，基于独立性的外推在k=5时低估失败20倍、k=10时约3000倍；R=8时相关拟合可追踪保留深度。实用杠杆在于去相关——更换模型家族、模态或证据源，而非增加门数。
Summary / 总结
Serial verification gates are a core reliability primitive in LLM harnesses: a candidate answer is returned only if $k$ verifier calls all accept it.
为理解部分相关性下LLM验证器级联的可靠性，本文构建了一个最小理论框架，将误接受率建模为从de Finetti分布G中抽取的潜变量α。核心发现是：后验对数似然随级联深度k呈凹函数，Odds定律为其在第一层的切线与上界；当潜变量服从Beta分布时，失败率随k^{-b}多项式衰减，相关参数ρ_v = 1/(a+b+1)。α=1处的盲点原子使可靠性上限为-ln(1-π)纳特，无法达到完美准确。当真实接受率也变化时，验证器可能最终有益、平台期或有害，取决于G与H的尾部指数，存在闭式交叉点k^†。该理论可测量：每实例R次判决可估计G的前R阶矩，从而通过Beta二项式似然与NPMLE恢复可靠性曲线与上限。实验表明，独立性假设下的外推在k=5时低估失败20倍、k=10时达3000倍，而R=8的关联拟合能准确追踪保留深度，证明“去相关”（如更换模型家族、模态或证据源）比增加验证器更关键。
Computation-aware Energy-harvesting Federated Learning with Pipelined Cyclic Scheduling
Authors: Eunjeong Jeong, Nikolaos Pappas
First: 2025-11-14T23:46:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T14:49:48+00:00
Comments: This paper has been submitted to a peer-reviewed journal
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated learning (FL) is a powerful paradigm for distributed learning, but increasing model complexity leads to significant energy consumption from client-side computations for local training. This challenge is critical in energy-harvesting FL (EHFL) systems, where the participation availability of each device fluctuates because of limited energy. To address this, we propose PipeCycle, a battery-aware distributed learning framework that organizes clients into pipelined cyclic groups. When a group completes its intra-group aggregation, its aggregated model is relayed directly to a newly formed group as a reference for local training, allowing multiple groups to coexist in the pipeline while overlapping client recharging periods with active training in other pipeline stages. We provide a convergence analysis of PipeCycle under a realistic energy consumption model in which local training spans multiple time slots, and show that the cyclic structure of the pipeline imposes a finite-horizon staleness bound that avoids the exponential factors typical of asynchronous FL analyses. Numerical experiments across both IID and non-IID data and various battery charging probabilities show that PipeCycle reaches a target accuracy with substantially lower cumulative energy than existing FL baselines, particularly under severe label skew where competing cyclic schemes collapse to near-chance accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向计算感知的能源采集联邦学习与流水线循环调度
联邦学习（FL）是一种强大的分布式学习范式，但模型复杂度的增加导致客户端本地训练计算能耗显著上升。这一挑战在能源采集联邦学习（EHFL）系统中尤为关键，因为设备参与可用性受制于有限能源而波动。为此，我们提出PipeCycle，一种电池感知的分布式学习框架，将客户端组织为流水线循环组。当一组完成组内聚合后，其聚合模型将直接传递给新形成的组作为本地训练参考，使多个组可同时存在于流水线中，并重叠客户端充电周期与流水线其他阶段的活跃训练。我们在现实能耗模型下（本地训练跨越多个时隙）对PipeCycle的收敛性进行了分析，表明流水线循环结构施加了有限时域的延迟界，避免了异步FL分析中常见的指数因子。数值实验在IID与非IID数据及不同电池充电概率下表明，PipeCycle在累积能耗上显著优于现有FL基线，尤其在严重标签偏斜场景下，其性能远超竞争性循环方案（后者退化至接近随机准确率）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high energy cost of client-side training in federated learning and the intermittent participation of energy-harvesting devices, the authors propose PipeCycle, a pipelined cyclic scheduling framework that organizes clients into overlapping groups to enable concurrent training while aligning recharging periods with active training in other pipeline stages. The method leverages a cyclic structure to bound model staleness and avoid the exponential divergence common in asynchronous FL, and its convergence is analyzed under a realistic energy model allowing multi-slot local training. Experimental results across IID and non-IID datasets and varying battery charging probabilities demonstrate that PipeCycle achieves target accuracy with significantly lower cumulative energy consumption than existing baselines, especially under severe label skew where other cyclic methods fail.
为应对联邦学习中客户端本地训练能耗高、能量采集设备参与不稳定的挑战，作者提出PipeCycle框架，通过将客户端组织成流水线式循环组，使不同组在训练阶段重叠时实现充电与训练并行。该方法通过组内聚合后直接传递模型，避免全局同步，其收敛性在考虑多时段能耗的现实模型下得到理论分析，且流水线结构确保了有限时滞，避免异步联邦学习中的指数级误差增长。实验表明，PipeCycle在IID与非IID数据及不同充电概率下，均以更低总能耗达到目标精度，尤其在标签严重偏斜场景下，优于其他循环方案。
Attentive multilayer fusion for vision transformers
Authors: Laure Ciernik, Marco Morik, Lukas Thede, Luca Eyring, Shinichi Nakajima, Zeynep Akata, Lukas Muttenthaler
First: 2026-01-14T09:50:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T14:49:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the rise of large-scale foundation models, efficiently adapting them to downstream tasks remains a central challenge. Linear probing, which freezes the backbone and trains a lightweight head, is computationally efficient but often restricted to last-layer representations. We show that task-relevant information is distributed across the network hierarchy rather than encoded solely in the last layers. To leverage this distribution of information, we apply an attentive probing mechanism that dynamically fuses representations from all layers of a Vision Transformer. This attentive layer fusion (ALF) learns to identify the most relevant layers for a target task and combines low-level structural cues with high-level semantic abstractions. Across 20 diverse datasets and multiple pretrained foundation models, ALF achieves consistent, substantial gains over standard linear probes. Attention heatmaps further reveal that tasks different from the pre-training domain benefit most from intermediate representations. Overall, our findings underscore the value of intermediate layers and demonstrate a principled, task-aware approach for unlocking their potential for probing-based adaptation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于视觉变换器的注意力多层融合
随着大规模基础模型的兴起，如何高效地将其适配到下游任务仍是一个核心挑战。线性探针通过冻结骨干网络并训练轻量级头部，计算效率高但常受限于最后一层表示。我们表明，任务相关的信息分布于网络层次结构中，而非仅编码在最后一层。为利用这种信息分布，我们应用了一种注意力探针机制，动态融合视觉变换器所有层的表示。该注意力层融合（ALF）能学习识别目标任务最相关的层，并结合低层结构线索与高层语义抽象。在20个多样化数据集和多个预训练基础模型上，ALF相比标准线性探针持续取得显著提升。注意力热图进一步表明，与预训练领域不同的任务最受益于中间表示。总体而言，我们的发现突显了中间层的价值，并展示了一种原则性、任务感知的方法，以释放其在探针式适配中的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of standard linear probing that relies solely on last-layer representations, this work proposes attentive layer fusion (ALF) to dynamically combine information from all layers of a Vision Transformer. ALF employs an attention mechanism to weigh and fuse layer-specific features, prioritizing those most relevant to the downstream task while integrating both low-level and high-level cues. Experiments across 20 datasets and multiple pretrained models show ALF consistently outperforms baseline linear probes, with attention heatmaps confirming that intermediate layers provide critical information for tasks outside the pre-training domain.
为了解决标准线性探针仅依赖最后一层表示导致性能受限的问题，本文提出了一种注意力层融合（ALF）方法，通过注意力机制动态加权融合视觉Transformer中所有层的表示，结合低层结构特征与高层语义信息。在20个多样化数据集和多个预训练模型上的实验表明，ALF显著优于基线方法，注意力热图进一步显示，跨预训练域的任务尤其受益于中间层表示。
The 2nd International StepUP Competition for Biometric Footstep Recognition: From Steps to Strides
Authors: Robyn Larracy, Anant Gupta, Gourav Gupta, Ethan Eddy, Maxime Devanne, Cyril Meyer, Jin-Chern Chiou, Yueh-Shan Lee, Zong-Han Lu, Aaron Tabor, Erik Scheme
First: 2026-07-15T14:46:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T14:46:37+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 2026 IEEE International Joint Conference on Biometrics (IJCB)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The International StepUP Competition Series was launched to advance research in pressure-based footstep biometrics through a standardized and challenging evaluation framework. Using the large-scale StepUP-P150 dataset (with more than 200,000 high-resolution dynamic footsteps from 150 individuals) and a previously unreleased test set, the 2nd edition of the competition addressed three key challenges: (1) generalization to unseen users with limited enrollment data, (2) robustness to domain shift caused by variations in footwear and walking speed and (3) effective fusion of paired left-right footsteps. While the first two challenges built on the inaugural competition, this edition introduced more extreme cross-domain conditions and moved beyond isolated footsteps to stride-level verification, enabling new opportunities for representation learning and inter-step information fusion. The competition attracted 26 registrants from academia and industry, with a best equal error rate of 8.00% achieved by the ArogyaPandit Research Team using a spatiotemporal CNN combined with an ensemble-based scoring strategy. The top solutions showcase the value of harnessing temporal patterns and of incorporating inference-time normalization and calibration strategies to improve scoring. However, the results also reveal that recognizing users in unseen personal footwear remains a challenge, especially in the presence of distractors with similar characteristics.

标题：第二届国际StepUP步态生物识别竞赛：从步态到步幅
国际StepUP竞赛系列旨在通过标准化且具有挑战性的评估框架推动基于压力的步态生物识别研究。本竞赛使用大规模StepUP-P150数据集（包含150名个体超过20万帧高分辨率动态步态）及此前未公开的测试集，聚焦三大关键挑战：（1）在仅有限注册数据情况下对未见用户进行泛化；（2）应对因鞋履类型和步行速度变化导致的域偏移问题；（3）有效融合左右脚步态信息。前两挑战沿袭首届竞赛，本届则引入更极端的跨域条件，并从孤立步态拓展至步幅级验证，为表征学习与步间信息融合提供新机遇。竞赛吸引26支来自学术界与工业界的参赛队伍，由ArogyaPandit研究团队以时空CNN结合集成评分策略取得最佳等错误率8.00%。顶尖方案凸显了利用时序模式、推理时归一化与校准策略提升评分的价值，但结果亦表明，在存在相似特征干扰者的情况下，识别未见个人鞋履仍具挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to advance pressure-based footstep biometrics through standardized evaluation, the 2nd StepUP Competition leveraged the large-scale StepUP-P150 dataset and a new test set to tackle three core challenges: generalizing to unseen users with limited data, handling domain shifts from footwear and walking speed variations, and fusing left-right footstep pairs.
