为推动基于压力的步态生物识别研究，第二届StepUP竞赛通过大规模StepUP-P150数据集和新测试集，聚焦三大核心挑战：对未见过用户的泛化能力、应对鞋履与步速变化引起的域偏移、以及左右脚步对的融合。本届竞赛引入更极端的跨域条件，并转向步态级验证，为表征学习与步间信息融合提供新机遇。26支队伍参赛，冠军团队以8.00%的等错误率夺冠，其方法结合时空卷积网络与集成评分策略。实验表明，建模时序模式和推理时归一化显著提升性能，但用户在陌生鞋履下的识别仍具挑战，尤其在存在相似特征干扰者时。
Inverse-LLaVA: Rethinking Multimodal Alignment via Text-to-Vision Mapping
Authors: Xuhui Zhan, Tyler Derr
First: 2025-08-17T18:36:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T14:44:35+00:00
Comments: 19pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Traditional multimodal learning approaches rely on alignment pre-training to bridge vision and language modalities, typically by projecting visual features into discrete text token spaces using large-scale image--text data. We revisit this design choice and propose Inverse-LLaVA, a multimodal architecture that inverts the conventional mapping direction by projecting text embeddings into continuous visual representation space and performing fusion within intermediate transformer layers. This representation-first design enables effective multimodal reasoning without relying on an explicit alignment pretraining stage and significantly reduces dependence on large alignment datasets. Across nine multimodal benchmarks, Inverse-LLaVA demonstrates strong learning efficiency under reduced supervision, achieving substantial gains on reasoning-intensive tasks while exhibiting selective performance drops on perception tasks that depend on explicit visual--text grounding. Our analysis indicates that these trade-offs primarily reflect differences in supervision regime rather than architectural limitations. Together, these results show that alignment pretraining is not strictly required for effective multimodal reasoning and highlight the importance of preserving continuous modality representations, opening a new direction for multimodal architecture design that decouples representation structure from supervision regime for more flexible and efficient multimodal systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Inverse-LLaVA：通过文本到视觉映射重新思考多模态对齐
传统多模态学习方法依赖对齐预训练以弥合视觉与语言模态，通常通过大规模图像-文本数据将视觉特征投影到离散文本标记空间。我们重新审视这一设计选择，提出Inverse-LLaVA，一种多模态架构，通过将文本嵌入投影到连续视觉表示空间并在中间Transformer层内进行融合，反转了传统映射方向。这种以表示为先的设计无需显式对齐预训练阶段即可实现有效的多模态推理，并显著降低对大规模对齐数据集的依赖。在九个多模态基准测试中，Inverse-LLaVA在监督减少的情况下展现出强大的学习效率，在推理密集型任务上取得显著提升，而在依赖显式视觉-文本对齐的感知任务上则出现选择性性能下降。我们的分析表明，这些权衡主要反映的是监督范式差异而非架构限制。综合来看，这些结果表明对齐预训练并非有效多模态推理的必要条件，强调了保留连续模态表示的重要性，为多模态架构设计开辟了新方向，即解耦表示结构与监督范式，以构建更灵活高效的多模态系统。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and data dependency of traditional alignment pretraining in multimodal models, the authors propose Inverse-LLaVA, which inverts the conventional text-to-vision mapping by projecting text embeddings into a continuous visual space and fusing them within transformer layers. This representation-first approach eliminates the need for explicit alignment pretraining and reduces reliance on large-scale image-text datasets. Experimental results across nine benchmarks show that Inverse-LLaVA achieves strong learning efficiency under reduced supervision, excelling on reasoning-intensive tasks while showing modest performance drops on perception tasks requiring explicit visual-text grounding — a trade-off attributed to supervision regime differences rather than architectural shortcomings.
为解决传统多模态模型对大规模对齐数据的依赖和预训练效率低的问题，作者提出Inverse-LLaVA，通过将文本嵌入映射到连续视觉空间并在Transformer中间层进行融合，反向设计了常规的视觉到文本对齐方式。该方法无需显式对齐预训练，显著降低对大规模图文数据的依赖。在九个基准测试中，该模型在监督减少的情况下表现出高效的训练效果，在推理密集型任务上取得显著提升，而在依赖显式图文对齐的感知任务上略有下降——这一权衡主要源于监督方式差异而非架构缺陷，表明对齐预训练并非多模态推理的必要条件。
High-Order Question Generation in a Multilingual Educational Context
Authors: Suna-Şeyma Uçar, Itziar Aldabe, Nora Aranberri, Orphée De Clercq
Venue: Proceedings of the Fifteenth Language Resources and Evaluation Conference (LREC 2026), pp. 760-769
First: 2026-07-15T14:42:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T14:42:08+00:00
Comments: This paper was accepted at the 15th edition of the Language Resources and Evaluation Conference (LREC 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Critical thinking is a fundamental skill that helps learners move beyond simple memorization. One way to develop this skill is through high-order questioning. However, crafting such questions remains a challenge for educators, and classroom practices tend to rely on low-order questions. Large Language Models have demonstrated strong capabilities in generating high-order questions, especially when guided by prompts based on Bloom's Taxonomy. Yet, existing research has largely centered on this framework and focused only on English. This study addresses these gaps by introducing prompts grounded in two alternative frameworks: Claim-Evidence-Reasoning and Divergent Questioning within a multilingual context using Basque, Spanish, and English. Results indicate that while both an open-source and a proprietary model rather effectively generate questions in all three languages, only about half of the answerable questions are recognized by teachers as high-order. A positive finding is that the alternative frameworks produce structurally and conceptually varied questions, suggesting they could complement each other and provide viable alternatives to Bloom's Taxonomy.

AIMO Interpretability Challenge
Authors: Michal Štefánik, Philipp Mondorf, Andreas Waldis, Qianying Liu, Chuan Yang, Michal Spiegel, Josef Kuchař, Marek Kadlčík, Adam Vawda-Oomerjee, Chaoran Liu, Simon Frieder, Barbara Plank, Fazl Barez, Pontus Stenetorp
Venue: NeurIPS 2026
First: 2026-07-15T14:41:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T14:41:28+00:00
Comments: Accepted Competition at NeurIPS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose the AIMO Interpretability Challenge, a competition on distinguishing robust from spurious reasoning in frontier mathematical language models based on the models' internal mechanisms. The challenge is motivated by a central limitation of standard reasoning benchmarks: strong final-answer accuracy does not reveal whether a model relies on stable reasoning mechanisms or exploits brittle reasoning shortcuts. Building on AI Mathematical Olympiad (AIMO) problems and submissions, together with resources from the Fields Model Initiative, the competition will provide (1) newly-published olympiad-level math reasoning problems and their symbolic representations, allowing generation of novel functional variants, (2) access to frontier reasoning models, and (3) assessments of models' adversarial robustness on these problems. Participants will use these resources, along with our computing infrastructure support, to develop methods for identifying which models solve problems robustly. Our competition will also create a new, open robustness benchmark and baseline systems, aiming to provide a lasting foundation for standard benchmarking in mathematical reasoning and interpretability. Scientifically, the competition connects interpretability and generalization research around a central question in AI research: can we determine if, and to what extent, the decision-making of frontier AI models is generalizable and thus, reliable?
中文标题/摘要
标题：AIMO可解释性挑战赛
我们提出AIMO可解释性挑战赛，旨在基于前沿数学语言模型的内部机制，区分其稳健推理与虚假推理。该挑战源于标准推理基准的核心局限：高最终答案准确率无法揭示模型是依赖稳定推理机制，还是利用脆弱的推理捷径。基于AI数学奥林匹克（AIMO）问题与提交内容，以及菲尔兹模型计划资源，竞赛将提供：（1）新发布的奥林匹克级数学推理问题及其符号表示，支持生成新颖的功能变体；（2）对前沿推理模型的访问权限；（3）对模型在这些问题上的对抗鲁棒性评估。参赛者将利用这些资源及我们的计算基础设施支持，开发识别哪些模型能稳健解决问题的方法。本竞赛还将创建新的开放鲁棒性基准与基线系统，旨在为数学推理与可解释性的标准基准测试提供持久基础。科学上，该竞赛围绕AI研究中的核心问题——能否判断前沿AI模型的决策是否可泛化、因而是否可靠——将可解释性与泛化研究相连接。

Leveraging AI Mathematical Olympiad problems and symbolic representations, along with access to cutting-edge reasoning models and adversarial testing infrastructure, participants develop methods to identify robust reasoning. The competition generates a new open benchmark and baseline systems, aiming to establish a lasting standard for measuring generalization and interpretability in mathematical AI.
鉴于现有基准无法区分数学模型中稳健与脆弱的推理方式，AIMO可解释性挑战赛旨在评估前沿模型是否依赖稳定可泛化的推理机制或脆弱的捷径。基于AI数学奥林匹克题目及其符号化表示，结合前沿模型访问权限与对抗性测试基础设施，参赛者开发识别稳健推理的方法。该竞赛构建了新的开放基准与基线系统，为未来评估数学推理中模型的可靠性与泛化能力提供持续支持。
RF Spectrogram Anomaly Detection with Quantum Kitchen Sinks: Architecture, Representation, and Hardware Validation
Authors: Abdallah Aaraba, Alexis Vieloszynski, Remon Polus, Ola Ahmad, Soumaya Cherkaoui
First: 2026-07-15T14:40:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T14:40:41+00:00
Comments: Paper accepted to IEEE quantum week 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The broadcast nature of wireless channels exposes radio-frequency (RF) networks to anomalous and malicious transmissions, making anomaly detection a fundamental requirement for secure spectrum management. Quantum Kitchen Sinks (QKS) offer a lightweight hybrid quantum feature map suitable for near-term quantum devices, yet their behavior on structured signal data remains poorly understood. In this paper, we extend the standard QKS template with multi-depth data re-uploading and ring entanglement, and evaluate the resulting pipeline on controlled RF spectrogram anomaly detection. We introduce a validation-locked five-stage ablation protocol that systematically separates the effects of shallow architecture, re-uploading depth, episode budget, input representation, and classical readout. Across the completed benchmark, Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT) representations consistently dominate raw and Principal Component Analysis (PCA) inputs, moderate-depth entangled QKS configurations form the strongest operating regime, and QKS improves over matched classical direct-readout baselines across all evaluated representation-readout pairs on the held-out test set, with the best configuration reaching a test Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.8778 and a test F1 of 0.7995. The study bridges two levels of realism: real measured sub-6\,GHz cellular signals on the data side and real-device validation on the ibm_quebec Quantum Processing Unit (QPU) on the computing side, with AUROC deviations below 0.013 relative to simulation. These results provide a practical, reproducible framework for deploying QKS-based anomaly detection in wireless networks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于量子厨房水槽的射频频谱异常检测：架构、表示与硬件验证
无线信道的广播特性使射频（RF）网络易受异常和恶意传输影响，因此异常检测是安全频谱管理的基本要求。量子厨房水槽（QKS）提供了一种适用于近期量子设备的轻量级混合量子特征图，但其在结构化信号数据上的行为尚不明确。本文在标准QKS模板基础上扩展了多深度数据重上传与环形纠缠，并在受控RF频谱图异常检测任务中评估了该流程。我们引入了一种验证锁定的五阶段消融协议，系统性地分离浅层架构、重上传深度、训练预算、输入表示和经典读出的影响。在完整基准测试中，离散余弦变换（DCT）表示始终优于原始数据和主成分分析（PCA）输入；中等深度纠缠的QKS配置形成最强运行模式；QKS在所有评估的表示-读出组合上均优于匹配的经典直接读出基线，在保留测试集上，最佳配置达到0.8778的AUROC和0.7995的F1值。该研究连接了两个现实层面：数据侧为真实测量的低于6GHz蜂窝信号，计算侧为ibm_quebec量子处理单元（QPU）的真实设备验证，AUROC与仿真偏差低于0.013。这些结果为在无线网络中部署基于QKS的异常检测提供了实用、可复现的框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect malicious RF transmissions in wireless networks, this study adapts Quantum Kitchen Sinks (QKS) — a lightweight hybrid quantum feature map — for anomaly detection in RF spectrograms by introducing multi-depth data re-uploading and ring entanglement. A five-stage ablation protocol isolates the impact of architecture depth, re-uploading, episode budget, input representation, and classical readout. Results show that Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT) inputs outperform raw or PCA representations, moderate-depth entangled QKS configurations yield the best performance, and QKS consistently outperforms classical baselines across all representation-readout pairs, achieving an AUROC of 0.8778 and F1 of 0.7995 on held-out test data. Validation on real ibm_quebec QPU hardware shows minimal deviation from simulated performance (AUROC deviation < 0.013), establishing a practical, reproducible framework for QKS-based anomaly detection in real-world wireless systems.
为应对无线网络中恶意RF传输的检测需求，本研究在量子厨房水槽（QKS）架构基础上引入多深度数据重上传与环形纠缠机制，以提升RF频谱图异常检测性能。通过五阶段消融协议，作者分别评估了浅层架构、重上传深度、训练预算、输入表示及经典读出方法的影响，发现离散余弦变换（DCT）输入表现最优，中等深度纠缠QKS配置效果最佳。在真实6GHz以下蜂窝信号数据与IBM ibm_quebec量子处理器硬件上验证，最优QKS配置在测试集上达到AUROC 0.8778与F1值0.7995，全面优于经典基线模型，且模拟与硬件性能偏差小于0.013。
Fine-Grained Vision-Language Pretraining with Organ-Conditioned Pattern Tokens for CT Understanding
Authors: Guoliang You, Xiaomeng Chu
First: 2026-07-15T14:36:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T14:36:43+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Computed tomography (CT) vision-language pretraining from paired volumes and radiology reports is a scalable yet challenging task. Existing methods commonly adopt global scan-report contrast, which is scalable but obscures heterogeneous organ evidence. Meanwhile, direct organ-level alignment remains coarse, since the same anatomy can exhibit multiple distinct radiological appearances. Therefore, pretraining requires a finer alignment unit: the organ-conditioned radiological pattern. In this work, we propose OCP-CT, an organ-conditioned pattern-token alignment framework for CT vision-language pretraining. Specifically, OCP-CT preserves a stable global CT-report contrastive branch and introduces an organ pattern interface: sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routes image and text tokens according to latent radiological patterns, learnable slots query the routed tokens into continuous pattern tokens, and paired token contrast aligns image-text pattern tokens with structured soft targets built from report-derived clinical similarity. On the publicly available CT-RATE and RAD-ChestCT benchmarks, OCP-CT achieves average AUROCs of 84.5% and 69.9% for zero-shot abnormality diagnosis, respectively. Compared with the strongest prior reported results, these results yield absolute AUROC gains of 6.7 and 0.8 percentage points.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于器官条件模式令牌的细粒度视觉-语言预训练用于CT理解
基于配对的CT体积与放射学报告的计算断层扫描（CT）视觉-语言预训练是一项可扩展但具有挑战性的任务。现有方法通常采用全局扫描-报告对比，虽具可扩展性，但掩盖了器官间异质性证据。同时，直接的器官级对齐仍较粗略，因为同一解剖结构可呈现多种不同的放射学表现。因此，预训练需要更精细的对齐单元：器官条件放射学模式。本文提出OCP-CT，一种面向CT视觉-语言预训练的器官条件模式令牌对齐框架。具体而言，OCP-CT保留稳定的全局CT-报告对比分支，并引入器官模式接口：稀疏Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）根据潜在放射学模式路由图像与文本令牌，可学习的槽位查询路由令牌生成连续模式令牌，配对令牌对比将图像-文本模式令牌与从报告推导出的临床相似性构建的结构化软目标对齐。在公开的CT-RATE和RAD-ChestCT基准测试中，OCP-CT分别实现零样本异常诊断的平均AUROC为84.5%和69.9%。与最强的先前结果相比，这些结果分别提升了6.7和0.8个百分点。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more precise alignment between CT images and radiology reports, this work addresses the limitation of global contrast methods that obscure organ-specific radiological patterns. OCP-CT introduces an organ-conditioned pattern-token framework that combines a global contrastive branch with a sparse Mixture-of-Experts routing mechanism to align image and text tokens based on latent radiological patterns. Learnable slots query these routed tokens into continuous pattern tokens, which are then contrasted with structured soft targets derived from clinical similarity in reports. Experiments on CT-RATE and RAD-ChestCT show OCP-CT achieves 84.5% and 69.9% average AUROC for zero-shot abnormality diagnosis, outperforming prior methods by 6.7 and 0.8 percentage points respectively.
为提升CT图像与放射报告间更精细的语义对齐，该研究针对现有全局对比方法掩盖器官特异性影像证据的问题，提出OCP-CT框架。该框架通过稀疏MoE模块按潜在影像模式路由图像与文本token，再由可学习槽位查询并生成连续模式token，最终通过对比学习与报告衍生的临床相似性软目标对齐。在CT-RATE和RAD-ChestCT公开数据集上，OCP-CT实现零样本异常检测AUROC分别为84.5%和69.9%，较当前最优方法提升6.7和0.8个百分点。
Survival Dynamics of Neural and Programmatic Policies in Evolutionary Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Anton Roupassov-Ruiz, Yiyang Zuo
First: 2026-01-07T20:09:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T14:35:54+00:00
Comments: Remove the Acknowledgement
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In evolutionary reinforcement learning tasks (ERL), agent policies are often encoded as small artificial neural networks (NERL). Such representations lack explicit modular structure, limiting behavioral interpretation. We investigate whether programmatic policies (PERL), implemented as soft, differentiable decision lists (SDDL), can match the performance of NERL. To support reproducible evaluation, we provide the first fully specified and open-source reimplementation of the classic 1992 Artificial Life (ALife) ERL testbed. We conduct a rigorous survival analysis across 4000 independent trials utilizing Kaplan-Meier curves and Restricted Mean Survival Time (RMST) metrics absent in the original study. We find a statistically significant difference in survival probability between PERL and NERL. PERL agents survive on average 201.69 steps longer than NERL agents. Moreover, SDDL agents using learning alone (no evolution) survive on average 73.67 steps longer than neural agents using both learning and evaluation. These results demonstrate that programmatic policies can exceed the survival performance of neural policies in ALife.
中文标题/摘要
标题：进化强化学习中神经策略与程序化策略的生存动态
在进化强化学习任务（ERL）中，智能体策略常编码为小型人工神经网络（NERL）。此类表示缺乏显式模块化结构，限制了行为解释。我们研究程序化策略（PERL），以可微分的软决策列表（SDDL）实现，能否与NERL匹敌。为支持可复现评估，我们首次提供了经典1992年人工生命（ALife）ERL测试平台的完整开源重实现。我们通过Kaplan-Meier曲线和受限平均生存时间（RMST）指标，在4000次独立试验中进行严格生存分析，这些指标原研究未使用。结果发现PERL与NERL的生存概率存在统计显著差异：PERL智能体平均比NERL智能体多存活201.69步。此外，仅通过学习（无进化）的SDDL智能体平均比同时使用学习与评估的神经智能体多存活73.67步。这些结果表明，在ALife环境中，程序化策略可超越神经策略的生存表现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of interpretability in neural policies used in evolutionary reinforcement learning, this study compares programmatic policies implemented as soft, differentiable decision lists (SDDL) against neural policies (NERL) in the classic 1992 ALife testbed. Using a fully open-source reimplementation and rigorous survival analysis across 4000 trials with Kaplan-Meier curves and RMST metrics, the results show that SDDL agents outperform NERL agents, surviving on average 201.69 steps longer. Notably, SDDL agents even outperform NERL when trained solely via learning without evolutionary evaluation, surviving 73.67 steps longer on average, indicating that programmatic representations may offer superior survival performance in this domain.
为了解决进化强化学习中神经网络策略缺乏可解释性的问题，该研究将软可微分决策列表（SDDL）实现的程序化策略与神经策略（NERL）进行对比，测试于经典ALife环境。通过4000次独立实验和生存分析方法（如Kaplan-Meier曲线与RMST），研究发现程序化策略平均生存步数比神经策略多201.69步。更值得注意的是，仅通过学习训练的SDDL策略（无需进化）平均比同时使用学习与评估的神经策略多生存73.67步，表明程序化策略在进化环境中可能更具鲁棒性。
A-IC3: Learning-Guided Adaptive Inductive Generalization for Hardware Model Checking
Authors: Xiaofeng Zhou, Guangyu Hu, Hongce Zhang, Wei Zhang
First: 2026-04-23T13:53:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T14:35:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The IC3 algorithm represents the state-of-the-art (SOTA) hardware model checking technique, owing to its robust performance and scalability. A significant body of research has focused on enhancing the solving efficiency of the IC3 algorithm, with particular attention to the inductive generalization process: a critical phase wherein the algorithm seeks to generalize a counterexample to inductiveness (CTI), which typically is a state leading to a bad state, into a broader set of states. This inductive generalization is a primary source of clauses in IC3 and thus plays a pivotal role in determining the overall effectiveness of the algorithm. Despite its importance, existing approaches often rely on fixed inductive generalization strategies, overlooking the dynamic and context-sensitive nature of the verification environment in which spurious counterexamples arise. This rigidity can limit the quality of generated clauses and, consequently, the performance of IC3. To address this limitation, we propose a lightweight machine-learning-based framework that dynamically selects appropriate inductive generalization strategies in response to the evolving verification context. Specifically, we employ a multi-armed bandit (MAB) algorithm to adaptively choose inductive generalization strategies based on real-time feedback from the verification process. The agent is updated by evaluating the quality of generalization outcomes, thereby refining its strategy selection over time. Empirical evaluation on a benchmark suite comprising 914 instances, primarily drawn from the latest HWMCC collection, demonstrates the efficacy of our approach. When implemented on the state-of-the-art model checker rIC3, our method solves 26 to 50 more cases than the baselines and improves the PAR-2 score by 194.72 to 389.29.
中文标题/摘要
标题：A-IC3：基于学习的自适应归纳泛化硬件模型检测
IC3算法因其稳健性能和可扩展性，代表了当前最先进的硬件模型检测技术。大量研究致力于提升IC3算法的求解效率，尤其关注归纳泛化过程：该过程是算法将导致坏状态的反例（CTI）泛化为更广泛状态集的关键阶段，也是IC3中主要的子句来源，对算法整体有效性起决定性作用。尽管其重要性显著，现有方法常依赖固定归纳泛化策略，忽视了产生虚假反例的验证环境的动态性和情境敏感性，这种僵化可能限制生成子句的质量，进而影响IC3性能。为解决此局限，我们提出一种轻量级基于机器学习的框架，根据验证环境的动态变化自适应选择合适的归纳泛化策略。具体而言，我们采用多臂老虎机（MAB）算法，依据验证过程的实时反馈动态选择泛化策略，通过评估泛化结果质量不断更新智能体，优化策略选择。在包含914个实例的基准测试集（主要来自最新HWMCC数据集）上的实证评估表明，当在当前最先进的模型检测器rIC3上实现时，我们的方法比基线方法多解决26至50个案例，PAR-2得分提升194.72至389.29。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve the efficiency of the IC3 hardware model checking algorithm, this work targets its inductive generalization phase, which is critical for generating effective clauses but often handled by rigid, fixed strategies. The authors propose A-IC3, a lightweight machine learning framework that uses a multi-armed bandit algorithm to dynamically select generalization strategies based on real-time feedback from the verification process, adapting over time to improve clause quality. Experimental results on 914 benchmark instances show that A-IC3 solves 26 to 50 more cases than baseline methods and improves the PAR-2 score by 194.72 to 389.29 when integrated with rIC3.
为提升IC3硬件模型检测器的效率，本文聚焦其关键的归纳泛化阶段，该阶段常采用固定策略，易忽略验证环境的动态性。作者提出A-IC3框架，利用多臂老虎机算法根据验证过程的实时反馈动态选择泛化策略，并随时间优化策略选择。在914个基准实例上的实验表明，A-IC3比基线方法多解决26至50个案例，PAR-2得分提升194.72至389.29。
PiVoT: A Variational Solution for Real-time Large-scale Multi-object Detection and Tracking under Heavy Clutter
Authors: Runze Gan, Qing Li, Simon J. Godsill, Mike E. Davies, James R. Hopgood
First: 2026-07-15T14:35:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T14:35:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-object detection and tracking from noisy point clouds remain challenging in many data-scarce radar applications. Current Bayesian trackers based on Poisson measurement models offer a training-free solution but struggle to achieve accuracy and efficiency under severe clutter, large object populations, and full-resolution Doppler point clouds. We address this with PiVoT, a fast, clutter-resilient multi-object tracker for both positional and Doppler measurements. PiVoT performs end-to-end detection and tracking of a large and time-varying number of objects without external clustering or detectors, through joint inference of object states, shapes, existence probabilities, data association, and measurement rates. Its efficiency is driven by several variational inference innovations, such as theoretically justified birth pruning, quadratic-to-linear complexity reductions for exact updates, and a computationally efficient Doppler Poisson model. Experiments show that PiVoT substantially outperforms existing Bayesian trackers in challenging scenes, while also demonstrating exceptional scalability to a thousand objects, robustness to clutter visually inseparable from objects, and real-time operation on full-scale modern automotive radar datasets, where it attains performance comparable to a deep-learning detection benchmark as a training-free joint detector and tracker.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PiVoT：一种用于重杂波环境下实时大规模多目标检测与跟踪的变分解法
在许多数据稀缺的雷达应用中，从噪声点云中进行多目标检测与跟踪仍具挑战性。当前基于泊松测量模型的贝叶斯跟踪器虽无需训练，但在严重杂波、大量目标及全分辨率多普勒点云条件下难以兼顾精度与效率。我们提出PiVoT，一种适用于位置与多普勒测量的快速、抗杂波多目标跟踪器。PiVoT通过联合推断目标状态、形状、存在概率、数据关联及测量率，实现无需外部聚类或检测器的大规模且随时间变化的目标端到端检测与跟踪。其高效性得益于多项变分推断创新，如理论支撑的出生剪枝、精确更新的二次到线性复杂度降低，以及计算高效的多普勒泊松模型。实验表明，PiVoT在复杂场景中显著优于现有贝叶斯跟踪器，同时展现出对千级目标的卓越可扩展性、对视觉上难以区分目标与杂波的鲁棒性，以及在全规模现代汽车雷达数据集上的实时运行能力，其作为无训练联合检测器与跟踪器的性能可媲美深度学习检测基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time, large-scale multi-object detection and tracking in cluttered radar environments with limited data, PiVoT introduces a variational Bayesian tracker that jointly infers object states, shapes, existence probabilities, data associations, and measurement rates without external detectors or clustering. It employs theoretical innovations like birth pruning and complexity reductions to enable efficient inference, while using a computationally efficient Doppler Poisson model. Experimental results show PiVoT significantly outperforms existing Bayesian trackers in cluttered, high-object-density scenes, scales to over a thousand objects, and operates in real-time on automotive radar data, matching deep-learning benchmarks in performance without training.
为解决雷达环境下高杂波、大规模目标的实时检测与跟踪难题，PiVoT提出一种变分贝叶斯框架，联合推断目标状态、形状、存在概率、数据关联及测量率，无需外部检测器或聚类模块。其通过理论驱动的出生剪枝、复杂度降低的变分更新等方法，实现对高杂波、大规模目标及全分辨率多普勒数据的高效处理。实验表明，PiVoT在复杂场景下显著优于现有贝叶斯跟踪器，可扩展至千级目标，对视觉上难以区分的杂波具有鲁棒性，并能在汽车雷达数据集上实现实时运行，其性能媲美深度学习检测基准作为无训练联合检测器与跟踪器。
Experience Memory Graph: One-Shot Error Correction for Agents
Authors: Wenjun Wang, Yuchen Fang, Fengrui Liu, Zibo Liang, Kai Zheng
First: 2026-07-15T14:33:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T14:33:15+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown remarkable capabilities in autonomous decision-making by generating sequential trajectories of states, actions, and observations. However, in complex, long-horizon tasks, these agents frequently suffer from compounding errors and struggle to recover from failures. Existing self-correction mechanisms rely on prompt-based reflection, which is inherently brittle, incurs heavy time and API costs due to iterative trial-and-error loops, and produces task-specific memory that may be hard to generalize to new scenarios. To address this, we propose Experience Memory Graph (EMG), a framework that reformulates agent failure recovery as a graph matching problem. At training time, we convert both failed exploration trajectories and successful expert trajectories into directed action decision graphs. By matching these graphs, we extract common subgraphs (successful workflows) and graph edit paths that explicitly indicate how to correct failures (e.g., which actions to add, delete, or relabel under a given observation), and store them in a memory graph with intra-task nodes and cross-task edges. At test time, EMG retrieves relevant insights and guides the agent in a single, loop-free execution. Experiments on ALFWorld and ScienceWorld show that EMG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art reflection baselines in success rate and average reward, while requiring no test-time trial-and-error.
中文标题/摘要
标题：经验记忆图：面向代理的一次性错误纠正
大型语言模型（LLM）代理通过生成状态、动作和观测的序列轨迹，在自主决策中展现出卓越能力。然而，在复杂、长时域任务中，这些代理常因累积错误而难以从失败中恢复。现有自纠正机制依赖基于提示的反思，固有脆弱，因迭代试错循环导致高时间和API开销，且生成的任务特定记忆难以泛化至新场景。为此，我们提出经验记忆图（EMG），将代理失败恢复重构为图匹配问题。训练时，将失败探索轨迹与成功专家轨迹均转化为有向动作决策图；通过图匹配提取公共子图（成功工作流）及图编辑路径（明确指示如何纠正失败，如在给定观测下添加、删除或重标记动作），并存储于包含任务内节点与跨任务边的记忆图中。测试时，EMG单次执行即可检索相关洞察并引导代理，无需试错循环。在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld上的实验表明，EMG在成功率和平均奖励上持续优于现有最先进反思基线，且无需测试时试错。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the brittleness and inefficiency of prompt-based self-correction in LLM agents, this work introduces Experience Memory Graph (EMG), a graph-based framework that treats failure recovery as a graph matching problem. During training, EMG converts both failed and successful trajectories into directed action decision graphs, then extracts common subgraphs and edit paths that encode corrective actions, storing them in a memory graph with intra-task and cross-task connections. At test time, EMG retrieves relevant graph patterns to guide agents in a single, loop-free execution without trial-and-error. Experiments on ALFWorld and ScienceWorld demonstrate that EMG significantly outperforms existing reflection methods in success rate and average reward while eliminating the need for iterative testing.
为解决大语言模型代理在复杂任务中因提示式反思机制导致的脆弱性与高成本问题，本文提出经验记忆图（EMG）框架，将失败恢复建模为图匹配任务。训练阶段将失败与成功轨迹转化为有向动作决策图，提取公共子图（成功流程）及图编辑路径（明确修正动作），并存储于含跨任务边的记忆图中。测试时直接检索相关图结构引导代理单次执行，无需试错。在ALFWorld与ScienceWorld上的实验表明，EMG在成功率与平均奖励上显著优于现有反思基线方法。
Verifying formulas for interventional distributions
Authors: Francesco Freni, Leonard Henckel, Sebastian Weichwald
First: 2026-07-15T14:33:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T14:33:08+00:00
Comments: Equal contribution between Leonard Henckel and Sebastian Weichwald
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We formalize verification in causal graphical models: deciding whether a given observational formula identifies a target interventional distribution. This opens a problem complementary to identification, asking not whether any identifying formula exists, but whether the given formula is identifying. We show that even sound and complete solutions to identification do not solve verification. We propose a falsifier as a first practical route forward, prove that it induces an almost-surely correct verifier for regular exponential-family models, and use the resulting verifier to develop the gateway test, which finds all sets admissible for use in a front-door formula.
中文标题/摘要
标题：干预分布公式的验证
我们在因果图模型中形式化了公式的验证：判断给定的观测公式是否能识别目标干预分布。这提出了一个与识别互补的问题，即不问是否存在任何识别公式，而是问给定公式是否具有识别性。我们证明，即使对识别问题有完备且正确的解决方案，也无法解决验证问题。我们提出了一种反例生成器作为首个实用的解决方案，证明其在正则指数族模型中几乎必然正确，并利用该验证器开发了门路检验，用于找出所有可用于前门公式中的可接受集合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to verify whether a specific observational formula correctly identifies a target interventional distribution in causal graphical models, the authors propose a falsifier-based approach that complements traditional identification methods. They demonstrate that existing sound and complete identification solutions are insufficient for verification, and instead introduce a verifier that is almost surely correct for regular exponential-family models. Experimental results show that this verifier enables the development of the gateway test, which successfully identifies all admissible sets for use in front-door formulas.
为解决因果图模型中特定观测公式是否能正确识别目标干预分布的问题，作者提出一种基于反例生成器的验证方法，该方法补充了传统识别方法的不足。他们证明现有完备的识别方法无法直接用于验证，进而设计了一种在正则指数族模型下几乎必然正确的验证器，并基于此开发了“门控测试”，成功找出所有可用于前门公式的可接受集合。
Adaptive Conformal Inference through the Lens of Blackwell Approachability
Authors: Guillaume Principato, Gilles Stoltz
First: 2025-10-17T17:06:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T14:32:05+00:00
Comments: 59 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This article considers an online version of conformal inference, called adaptive conformal inference [ACI] and introduced by Gibbs and Candès (2021): prediction sets are issued sequentially, after observing features and before the outcomes are revealed. These sets are evaluated both in terms of validity (the fraction of rounds where the outcome was lying in the prediction set) and efficiency (the average lengths of the prediction sets). The two criteria point to different directions (validity favors larger sets). We also target a wide range of scenarios, with exchangeable data and arbitrary data (lack of any stochastic guarantees) as two extremes. A series of existing strategies for ACI typically guarantee that empirical coverage converges to the desired level for arbitrary sequences, but they generally lack simultaneous efficiency guarantees. To provide a unified study, we first formulate ACI as a repeated two-player game with finite action sets and vector-valued payoffs encoding validity and efficiency. Building on this reformulation, we introduce a strategy based on Blackwell approachability and on its opportunistic extension by Bernstein et al. (2014) that ensures validity while adapting the efficiency of the prediction intervals to the underlying degree of stochasticity of the opponent player. The resulting guarantee is "best of many worlds": it recovers the relevant efficiency guarantees in exchangeable and adversarial settings, and provides guarantees in intermediate settings that arise in typical applications such as the forecasting of time series.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过Blackwell可接近性视角的自适应符合推断
本文研究了Gibbs和Candès（2021）提出的自适应符合推断（ACI）的在线版本：预测集在观察特征后、结果揭示前依次生成。这些集合从有效性（结果落在预测集中的比例）和效率（预测集平均长度）两个维度进行评估。这两个标准指向不同方向（有效性偏好更大的集合）。我们还涵盖广泛场景，从可交换数据到任意数据（无任何随机保证）作为两个极端。现有ACI策略通常保证对任意序列经验覆盖收敛至期望水平，但一般缺乏同时的效率保证。为统一研究，我们首先将ACI形式化为具有有限行动集和向量值收益（编码有效性与效率）的重复双人博弈。在此基础上，我们引入基于Blackwell可接近性及其Bernstein等（2014）提出的机遇性扩展的策略，确保有效性的同时，根据对手玩家的随机性程度自适应调整预测区间的效率。所得保证为“多世界最优”：在可交换和对抗性场景中恢复相关效率保证，并在典型应用（如时间序列预测）中出现的中间场景提供保证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance validity and efficiency in adaptive conformal inference, this work formulates the problem as a repeated two-player game with vector-valued payoffs representing coverage and interval length. It then introduces a strategy grounded in Blackwell approachability and its opportunistic extension, which adapts prediction interval efficiency to the underlying stochasticity of the data. Experimental results show that this approach achieves "best of many worlds" guarantees: it recovers optimal efficiency in exchangeable settings, maintains validity under adversarial conditions, and provides robust performance across intermediate scenarios typical in real-world time series forecasting.
为在自适应置信推断中平衡覆盖有效性与区间效率，本文将问题建模为具有向量收益的重复双人博弈，收益分别对应覆盖率与区间长度。基于Blackwell可接近性及其机会扩展策略，提出一种自适应调整预测区间大小的方法，使其根据数据结构动态优化效率。实验表明，该方法实现“最佳多世界”保障：在可交换数据下恢复最优效率，在对抗性场景下保持覆盖有效性，并在典型应用如时间序列预测的中间场景中提供稳健性能。
Learning Forward & Reverse Skills from a Single Unfinished Demonstration for Constrained Manipulation Tasks
Authors: Yexin Hu, Haoyi Zheng, Johannes Heidersberger, Dongheui Lee
First: 2026-07-15T14:31:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T14:31:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Learning from demonstration (LfD) enables robots to learn manipulation skills directly from expert demonstrations but remains challenging for contact-rich tasks involving geometric constraints and force interaction. Existing approaches typically require multiple complete demonstrations and do not support reverse skill execution. In this paper, we present a unified one-shot framework for constrained manipulation that learns both forward and reverse execution from a single, possibly unfinished demonstration. Our method decomposes demonstrations into non-contact and contact phases, with non-contact motion encoded with dynamic movement primitives (DMP), and contact motion represented as a sequence of screw motion primitives segmented by our proposed geometry-driven twist-direction segmentation algorithm. During execution, screw primitives are executed sequentially under admittance-guided pose correction and speed regulation, enabling task completion beyond the demonstrated trajectory length as well as reverse skill execution without additional learning data. Experiments on peg insertion, battery insertion, lock opening, and screw driving tasks demonstrate improved success rates and robustness over segmentation and one-shot trajectory learning baselines. Details are available on the project website: https://tuwien-asl.github.io/LfD-Screw/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从单个未完成的演示中学习正向与反向技能以应对受限操作任务
从演示中学习（LfD）使机器人能够直接从专家演示中学习操作技能，但在涉及几何约束和力交互的接触密集型任务中仍具挑战性。现有方法通常需要多个完整演示，且不支持反向技能执行。本文提出了一种统一的一次性框架，可从单个（可能未完成的）演示中学习正向与反向执行。我们的方法将演示分解为非接触和接触阶段，非接触运动通过动态运动原语（DMP）编码，接触运动则表示为由我们提出的几何驱动的旋转向量分割算法分割的螺动原语序列。执行时，螺动原语在阻抗引导的姿态校正和速度调节下顺序执行，从而实现超出演示轨迹长度的任务完成以及无需额外学习数据的反向技能执行。在销插入、电池插入、锁开启和螺丝驱动等任务上的实验表明，该方法相比分割和一次性轨迹学习基线具有更高的成功率和鲁棒性。项目详情见：https://tuwien-asl.github.io/LfD-Screw/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of learning manipulation skills from incomplete or single demonstrations in contact-rich tasks, this paper proposes a unified one-shot framework that learns both forward and reverse execution. The method decomposes demonstrations into non-contact phases, encoded via dynamic movement primitives, and contact phases, represented as screw motion primitives segmented by a geometry-driven twist-direction algorithm. During execution, screw primitives are sequentially executed with admittance-guided pose correction and speed regulation, enabling completion beyond the demonstrated trajectory and reverse execution without additional data. Experiments on peg insertion, battery insertion, lock opening, and screw driving show improved success rates and robustness compared to segmentation and one-shot trajectory learning baselines.
为解决从不完整演示中学习接触密集型操作技能的难题，本文提出一种统一的一次性框架，可从单个演示中同时学习正向与反向执行。该方法将运动分解为非接触阶段（使用动态运动原语编码）和接触阶段（使用旋量运动原语表示），接触阶段通过几何驱动的扭力方向分割算法进行分段。执行时，旋量原语在阻抗引导的姿态修正与速度调节下顺序执行，支持超出演示轨迹长度的任务完成及无需额外数据的反向执行。在插销、电池插入、开锁和拧螺丝等任务实验中，该方法相比分割与一次性轨迹学习基线，展现出更高的成功率与鲁棒性。
